The month-end count is adopted, not the floor. Discounting must use the grid on which S04-R-12 accretes, otherwise the schedule does not settle: under the floor the CHK-137 CSP would be 856,830.00 and the 60th instalment would leave a residual above one minor unit.<br>2. S04-R-12a applies only to a deferred payment (JET-11a), and only when at least one month end of the inception schedule falls on or before L. The suspended interest is not caught up. It is recognised over the remaining months, weighted by the inception schedule's exact monthly interest, so only integer powers are used (CV-31). An advance payment (JET-11b) is never suspended, because the contract liability exists from receipt.<br>3. The memo columns keep the ≤ 0 sign of S04-R-02 and are not magnitudes. 04 DB-17 V1, DG-ENG-05, DG-AK-54 and dev-guide §9.7 P1 all read Σ `allocated_amount` = `transaction_price − consideration_payable_amount`, which gives the gross allocation basis only with the signed member.<br>Change record: `docs/reviews/B4-numeric-residue.md` |

This file is the entry point of the engine specification. Section I lists every section of both files. Section 0 fixes the conventions every stage obeys: bundle schemas, determinism, numbers, rounding helpers, findings, trace, constants and the shared state contract. Sections 1 to 8 specify stages 01 to 08. `docs/accounting/ENGINE_SPEC_B.md` specifies stages 09 to 15.

---

## 0. Conventions

### 0.1 Scope, authority and identifiers

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| CV-01 | **Division of authority.** Where POLICIES publishes an ALG, JET or CHK, its arithmetic governs and this file cites it. This file adds the pipeline (stage order, inputs, outputs, state), the algorithms POLICIES leaves to the engine specification (Step 1 gate, POB builder, transaction-price build-up, SSP resolution pipeline, onboarding mechanics), trace content, findings and invariants. A policy choice not registered in POLICIES §1 or §5.0 is never added here; a gap is an open question (POLICIES §0.1) |
| CV-02 | **Evidence labels.** [F] fact with a citation (Codification paragraph in FASB format, IFRS 15 paragraph, or a named document id); [J] judgement of this document with a one-line rationale; [A] assumption used only to make an example concrete (POLICIES §0.2) |
| CV-03 | **Identifiers.** Conventions `CV-nn`; stage rules `S<nn>-R-<nn>`; stage invariants `S<nn>-INV-<nn>`; worked examples `EX-<nn>-<letter>`; open questions `OQ-A-nn`. `ENGINE_SPEC_B.md` uses the same pattern for stages 09 to 15 and `OQ-B-nn`. Ids are stable and never renumbered; a retired id is marked `Retired` in place |
| CV-04 | **Literals.** Enumeration literals, problem slugs, and finding and exception codes are 04 literals (D-73), cited verbatim. Registry keys and option literals are POLICIES §1 literals (D-13a). A code this file needs that 04 does not catalogue is marked "proposed", listed in §16 with its open question, and is not emitted by the build loop until the supervisor adopts it. Rev 1.2: D-76 adopted every identifier that rev 1.0 proposed (OQ-A-06 to OQ-A-13, OQ-A-16, OQ-A-18, OQ-A-19, OQ-A-21, OQ-A-24), and 04 rev 1.2 publishes each one, so none remains proposed. After the design freeze (D-77) a missing literal is a Spec question in `PROGRESS.md` |
| CV-05 | **Normative words.** "Must", "never" and imperative pseudocode are binding. "May" marks a permitted option. Pseudocode is Python 3.12 over the types of §0.4 and §0.11; a helper whose body is not shown is specified by the prose of the rule that names it |

Crosswalk to the restated conventions of `ENGINE_SPEC_B.md` §0.2: C-01 = CV-30 to CV-36; C-02 = CV-37; C-03 = CV-20 to CV-24; C-04 and C-05 = CV-12 and CV-13; C-06 = CV-14; C-07 = CV-50 to CV-56; C-08 = CV-40 to CV-45; C-09 = CV-46.

### 0.2 Pipeline, stage packages and module map

DG-ENG-07 requires stage modules under `backend/erev_engine/stages/` to take the names and order fixed by this specification. Each stage is a package whose `__init__.py` exports its entry functions; submodules are private to the stage (`ENGINE_SPEC_B.md` §0.4 uses the same pattern).

| 02 | Contract identification | ENGINE_SPEC §2 | `erev_engine/stages/s02_contract_identification/` | `run(ctx: BookContext, cb: CanonicalBundle, tb) -> IdentifiedState`; `apply(ctx, st: AllocatedState, ev: EventView, tb) -> AllocatedState` | STG-03 |

### 0.3 Orchestration inside `compute`

```python
# erev_engine/__init__.py
def compute(bundle: InputBundle) -> OutputBundle:
    guards.no_floats(bundle)                                   # FLOAT_DETECTED (DG-ENG-03)
    if bundle.engine_version != ENGINE_VERSION:
        raise EngineError("ENGINE_VERSION_MISMATCH", "bundle engine version differs")
    with decimal.localcontext(money.DECIMAL_CONTEXT):          # ENG-03
        tb = TraceBuilder(engine_version=ENGINE_VERSION)       # book-independent nodes of stage 01
        cb = s01_canonicalize.run(bundle, tb)                  # §1; one canonical view for every book
        results = s13_books.run_books(cb, STAGES)              # ENGINE_SPEC_B §13: per book, memoised
        output = assemble_output(bundle, cb, results)          # §0.5; diagnostics sorted (CV-43)
    guards.no_floats(output)
    return output

def fold_book(ctx: BookContext, cb: CanonicalBundle, tb: TraceBuilder) -> AllocatedState:
    st2 = s02_contract_identification.run(ctx, cb, tb)         # inception gate and deposit mode
    st3 = s03_pob_builder.run(ctx, st2, tb)                    # obligations from every member booking
    st4 = s04_transaction_price.run(ctx, st3, tb)              # inception transaction price
    st = s05_allocation.run(ctx, st4, tb)                      # inception allocation: first segment per obligation
    for ev in cb.boundary_events:                              # ENG-06 order; CV-20
        st = BOUNDARY_HANDLERS[ev.event_type](ctx, st, ev, tb) # Table 0.3-A
    return st                                                  # consumed by stage 09 (ENGINE_SPEC_B §0.5)
```

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| CV-10 | **Inception fold.** Stages 02 to 05 run once per book over the group's inception state: every `CONTRACT_BOOKED` event of every member contract at `known_at`, whatever its effective date, because combination re-runs allocation from the group's inception (REQ-CON-009; RCP-01) [F, 606-10-25-9]. Membership is taken from `GroupInput.member_contract_keys` (bundle assembly, RCP-15); `COMBINATION_CHANGED` events therefore change the member set, not the fold (S02-R-09) |
| CV-11 | **Boundary fold.** After stage 05, the boundary events of Table 0.3-A are applied in ENG-06 order. A boundary handler receives the state before the event and returns the state after it, adding allocation segments (§0.11) and never editing an earlier segment. Every other event is a measure event consumed by stages 09 to 12 through `CanonicalBundle.measure_events` and `QuantityLedger` (§1) |
| CV-12 | **Periods.** `dates.period_of(entity, d)` returns the period of `EntityInput.periods` whose `[start_date, end_date]` contains d; a date outside every period raises `EngineError("ENGINE_INVARIANT_VIOLATED", detail={"rule": "CV-12"})`, because bundle assembly must supply the calendar from the group's inception through the horizon (RCP-15). The entity that owns an amount decides the calendar (ENGINE_SPEC_B C-04) |
| CV-13 | **Horizon.** The evaluation horizon of (entity, book) is the last period whose state is `open`, `closing` or `reopened` (RCP-03). Stages 01 to 08 compute state as of every effective date in the fold; stages 09 to 15 evaluate targets through the horizon |
| CV-14 | **Targets, not movements.** Stages produce cumulative targets and state; movements and posting intents are differences taken by stage 14 against `posted` (RCP-06). No stage of this file reads `InputBundle.posted` except stage 07 for the opening baseline check (§7) and stage 08 for late-event registers (§8) |

Table 0.3-A: boundary events and their handlers (extends RCP-01 with `OPENING_BALANCE_ESTABLISHED`; [J] cutover state replaces inception state and must precede measure events that follow it)

| E-03 event type | Handler | Section |
|---|---|---|
| `CONTRACT_BOOKED` of a member after the first | none (absorbed by CV-10) | §2.3 |
| `COLLECTIBILITY_ASSESSED`, `CONTRACT_CRITERIA_MET`, `SIGNIFICANT_CHANGE_FLAGGED` | `s02_contract_identification.apply` | §2.3 |
| `CONTRACT_AMENDED`, `CONTRACT_TERMINATED`, `REGROUPED`, `LINE_ATTRIBUTES_CHANGED`, `MATERIAL_RIGHT_EXERCISED` | `s06_modifications.apply` | §6.3 |
| `OPENING_BALANCE_ESTABLISHED` | `s07_onboarding.apply` | §7.3 |
| `ESTIMATE_CHANGED` | `s08_estimates_late_events.apply` | §8.3 |

`CONTRACT_ACTIVATED`, `MATERIAL_RIGHT_EXPIRED`, `HOLD_APPLIED`, `HOLD_RELEASED`, `MEMO_UPDATED`, `PRE_STANDARD_REVENUE_RECORDED`, `MANUAL_ADJUSTMENT_APPLIED` and every measure event of E-03 are not boundaries. `EVENT_VOIDED` and `CONTRACT_VOIDED` are resolved by stage 01 (S01-R-12, S01-R-13).

### 0.4 Input bundle schema

`backend/erev_engine/bundle.py` holds the binding field list of DG §7. This section fixes the field detail. Every class is `@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)`. Money and exact values arrive as `Decimal`, converted once by `money.to_fraction` in stage 01; dates as `date`; timestamps as timezone-aware `datetime`. Tuples are sorted by the key in the right-hand comment; stage 01 asserts the order (S01-R-02).

```python
class InputBundle:                              # DG §7 field list
    format_version: int                         # 1
    engine_version: str                         # == ENGINE_VERSION
    trigger: str                                # E-87 literal, or "DRY_RUN" (05 §3.2, ENG-12)
    known_at: datetime                          # record-time cutoff; excluded from input_sha256 (ENG-08)
    tenant_preset: str                          # "DEFAULT" | "LEGACY_PARITY" (informational; switches are policy values, DG-ENG-09)
    currencies: CurrencyTable                   # ISO code -> CurrencySpec; every code the group uses
    books: tuple[BookInput, ...]                # book_code in RCP-11 order ASC606, IFRS15, LEGACY
    entities: tuple[EntityInput, ...]           # code
    group: GroupInput
    contracts: tuple[ContractInput, ...]        # external_id
    events: tuple[EventInput, ...]              # (effective_date, record_seq, event_key) = ENG-06
    ssp_versions: tuple[SspVersionInput, ...]   # (ssp_book_code, version_no)
    pob_template_versions: tuple[TemplateInput, ...]   # (template_code, version_no)
    rule_set_versions: tuple[RuleSetInput, ...] # (rule_set_code, version_no)
    estimate_versions: tuple[EstimateVersionInput, ...] # (estimate_key, version_no)
    fx_rates: tuple[FxRateInput, ...]           # (rate_type, base, quote, effective_date, version_key)
    posted: tuple[PostedAmountInput, ...]       # (book_code, entity_code, subject_key, entry_kind, account_role,
                                                #  clearing_purpose or "", period_key, origin_period_key or "",
                                                #  posting_class, reason_code or "")

class BookInput:
    book_code: str                              # E-02
    is_primary: bool
    entity_codes: tuple[str, ...]               # entities keeping the book (T-REF-03), sorted
    policies: tuple[ResolvedPolicyInput, ...]   # (code, scope, subject_key)
    account_mapping: AccountMappingInput        # PUBLISHED version pinned for the computation (T-REF-14, -15)

class ResolvedPolicyInput:                      # DG-KRN-REG-03: the orchestrator resolves, the engine reads
    code: str                                   # POLICIES §1 key, for example "mod.ssp_basis"
    scope: str                                  # "GROUP" | "CONTRACT" | "OBLIGATION" | "ENTITY" | "PERIOD"
    subject_key: str                            # "" for GROUP; contract external_id; obligation subject key;
                                                # entity code; "<entity code>@<period_key>" for PERIOD
    value: str | tuple[str, ...] | Mapping[str, str]   # literal(s) exactly as POLICIES §1 Options; decimals as strings
    level: str                                  # "O" | "C" | "P" | "B" | "E" | "T" | "DEFAULT" (DG-KRN-REG-01)
    source_ref: str                             # registry version, template version, product or override key
    pin: str                                    # "K" | "P"

class AccountMappingInput:
    version_key: str; content_sha256: str
    rules: tuple[MappingRuleInput, ...]         # (account_role, clearing_purpose, -specificity, -priority, account_code)

class MappingRuleInput:
    account_role: str; clearing_purpose: str | None; entity_code: str | None; book_code: str | None
    product_code: str | None; revenue_category: str | None; account_code: str
    default_dimensions: Mapping[str, str]; priority: int; specificity: int

class EntityInput:
    code: str; functional_currency: str; time_zone: str
    calendar_pattern: str                       # E-50
    periods: tuple[PeriodInput, ...]            # start_date; group inception through the horizon plus the future
                                                # periods containing every subject end date (CV-12, CV-13)
class PeriodInput:
    period_key: str                             # FY<year>-P<nn> (T-REF-05)
    fiscal_year: int; period_no: int; start_date: date; end_date: date
    states: tuple[tuple[str, str], ...]         # (book_code, E-04 state) at known_at, sorted by book_code

class GroupInput:
    group_key: str                              # combination_group.code (T-CON-03)
    transaction_currency: str
    inception_date: date                        # earliest member inception (T-CON-03)
    member_contract_keys: tuple[str, ...]       # members at known_at (T-CON-04), sorted
    criterion: str | None                       # "25_9_A" | "25_9_B" | "25_9_C"; None for singletons
    previous_stream_heads: tuple[tuple[str, int], ...]  # (contract key, stream_version) included in the previous
                                                # computation of the group; () for the first (activity columns, §0.11)
    products: tuple[ProductInput, ...]          # every product referenced by a member line or event; code
    portfolios: tuple[PortfolioInput, ...]      # portfolio code -> member contract keys at known_at (T-CON-22)

class ProductInput:                             # T-REF-20, T-REF-21
    code: str; sku_number: str | None; product_family: str | None; revenue_category: str | None
    default_template_code: str | None; principal_agent: str; distinctness_default: str
    unit_of_measure: str; is_bundle: bool; policy_values: Mapping[str, str]
    assurance_cost_per_unit: Decimal | None     # T-REF-20 column (04 rev 1.2; OQ-A-06 resolved by D-76); None = no accrual (S03-R-08)
    components: tuple[BundleComponentInput, ...]  # (sequence, component_product_code)

class BundleComponentInput:
    component_product_code: str; quantity_per_bundle: Decimal; split_basis: str   # "relative_ssp" | "fixed_percentage"
    split_ratio: Decimal | None; sequence: int; valid_from: date; valid_to: date | None

class ContractInput:                            # T-CON-01 header projection at known_at (informational); the
    external_id: str                            # booking payload is authoritative for terms (S01-R-20)
    customer_code: str; related_party_group: str | None; contracting_entity_code: str
    transaction_currency: str; inception_date: date; signature_date: date | None; document_ref: str | None
    termination_party: str | None; termination_has_penalty: bool | None; termination_notice_days: int | None
    has_commercial_substance: bool; region: str | None; channel: str | None; contract_type: str | None
    renewal_of_contract_key: str | None
    judgements: tuple[JudgementInput, ...]      # REVIEWED records only; judgement_key
    material_rights: tuple[MaterialRightInput, ...]   # obligation_key (T-CON-14)
    modifications: tuple[ModificationInput, ...]      # APPROVED or APPLIED objects referenced by events; modification_key
    noncash_consideration: tuple[NoncashInput, ...]   # booking payload member, 04 API-S-NoncashConsideration (rev 1.2; OQ-A-07
                                                #   resolved by D-76); a CONTRACT_AMENDED member replaces the list from its effective date
    consideration_payable: tuple[PayableInput, ...]   # booking payload member, 04 API-S-ConsiderationPayable (rev 1.2; OQ-A-08
                                                #   resolved by D-76); replaced by a CONTRACT_AMENDED member as above
    payment_schedule: tuple[PaymentPointInput, ...]   # booking payload member, 04 API-S-PaymentPoint (rev 1.2; OQ-A-09 resolved by D-76)
    scope_605_35: bool                          # T-CON-01 column (rev 1.2; ENGINE_SPEC_B S11-R-15); header projection: the value in force
                                                #   at a date is the latest booking or CONTRACT_AMENDED payload carrying the member (S01-R-20)

class JudgementInput:                           # T-CON-19; the engine reads structured outcomes only
    judgement_key: str; topic: str              # E-56
    subject_key: str; book_code: str | None     # None = every book
    outcome: Mapping[str, str]                  # questionnaire members listed per topic in Table 0.4-A

class EventInput:                               # T-CON-05
    event_key: str                              # "<contract external_id>/EV-<stream_version, 6 digits>" (CV-22)
    contract_key: str; stream_version: int; event_type: str; schema_version: int
    effective_date: date; recorded_at: datetime; record_seq: int
    origin: str; is_manual: bool; obligation_keys: tuple[str, ...]
    payload: Mapping[str, object]               # 04 §16.3 payload with handles resolved to natural keys
    payload_sha256: str; idempotency_key: str | None
    supersedes_event_key: str | None            # EVENT_VOIDED target
    modification_key: str | None; estimate_version_key: str | None; manual_adjustment_key: str | None

class SspVersionInput:                          # T-REF-28 to T-REF-31
    ssp_book_code: str; version_key: str        # "<ssp book code>@v<version_no>"
    version_no: int; resolution_mode: str       # "EFFECTIVE_DATE" | "BY_LABEL"
    legacy_version_label: str | None; effective_from_date: date | None; effective_to_date: date | None
    status: str                                 # "APPROVED" | "SUPERSEDED" (only versions pinned or effective)
    approved_at: datetime; content_sha256: str
    scope_entity_code: str | None; scope_currency: str | None; scope_channel: str | None; scope_segment: str | None
    entries: tuple[SspEntryInput, ...]          # (product_code, stratification, region, channel, segment,
                                                #  deal_size_band, term_band, currency)
class SspEntryInput:
    entry_key: str                              # "<version_key>/<product_code>/<stratification>/<dims>/<currency>"
    product_code: str; stratification: str; region: str | None; channel: str | None; segment: str | None
    deal_size_band: str | None; term_band: str | None; currency: str
    method: str                                 # E-47
    value_basis: str                            # E-49
    unit_list_price: Decimal | None; midpoint_discount_ratio: Decimal | None; range_ratio: Decimal | None
    cost_basis: Decimal | None; margin_ratio: Decimal | None; distinctness: str
    revenue_account_code: str | None
    observable_point: Decimal | None            # T-REF-30 column (04 rev 1.2; OQ-A-10 resolved by D-76); None = absent (S05-R-07)
    ranges: tuple[SspRangeInput, ...]           # (band_dimension, band_from)
class SspRangeInput:
    band_dimension: str; band_from: Decimal | None; band_to: Decimal | None
    point_value: Decimal | None; low_value: Decimal | None; mid_value: Decimal | None; high_value: Decimal | None

class TemplateInput:                            # T-REF-23 columns verbatim plus identity
    template_code: str; version_key: str; version_no: int; content_sha256: str
    obligation_kind: str; distinctness: str; series_increment_unit: str | None
    satisfaction_pattern: str; over_time_criterion: str; recognition_method: str; ratable_convention: str | None
    start_date_rule: str; end_date_rule: str; term_months: int | None; principal_agent: str
    warranty_type: str; licence_nature: str; sfc_assessment_required: bool; revenue_category: str | None
    disaggregation: Mapping[str, str]; account_role_overrides: Mapping[str, str]
    stratification_label: str | None; is_excluded_from_netting_attribution: bool
    policy_values: Mapping[str, str]
    effective_from: date; effective_to: date | None   # SC-V effective range

class RuleSetInput:
    rule_set_code: str; kind: str; version_key: str; version_no: int; content_sha256: str
    effective_from: date; effective_to: date | None
    rules: tuple[RuleInput, ...]                # (-specificity, -priority, rule_key)
class RuleInput:
    rule_key: str; priority: int; specificity: int
    conditions: tuple[Mapping[str, object], ...]; outputs: Mapping[str, str]

class EstimateVersionInput:                     # T-CON-12 and T-CON-13; APPROVED versions referenced by included events
    estimate_key: str                           # "<contract external_id>/<element_code>" or "PORTFOLIO:<code>/<element_code>"
    estimate_kind: str; element_code: str; method: str; vc_element_type: str | None
    direction: str | None                       # T-CON-12 {INCREASE, DECREASE}; None ⇒ the B3-DG-17 default (D-97 (23); rev 1.10)
    allocation_target: str; target_obligation_keys: tuple[str, ...]; obligation_key: str | None
    version_key: str                            # "<estimate_key>@v<version_no>"
    version_no: int; status: str; effective_date: date
    scenarios: tuple[Mapping[str, Decimal], ...]   # {outcome, amount, probability}
    parameters: Mapping[str, object]            # T-CON-13 parameter schema by kind
    unconstrained_amount: Decimal | None; most_conservative_amount: Decimal | None
    constrained_amount: Decimal | None; rate: Decimal | None; expected_total_amount: Decimal | None
    expected_quantity: Decimal | None; amortization_months: int | None; currency: str | None
    supersedes_version_key: str | None; judgement_key: str | None; content_sha256: str

class FxRateInput:                              # T-REF-11, T-REF-12
    rate_key: str; version_key: str; rate_type: str; base_currency: str; quote_currency: str
    effective_date: date; period_key: str | None; rate: Decimal

class PostedAmountInput:                        # RCP-05, grouped also by origin period and posting class (OQ-B-19 resolved by D-76)
    book_code: str; entity_code: str; subject_key: str; entry_kind: str; account_role: str
    clearing_purpose: str | None; counterparty_entity_code: str | None
    period_key: str                             # posting period
    origin_period_key: str | None               # subledger_line.origin_period_id, None when posted in its own period
    posting_class: str                          # "EVENT" (E-31 ENGINE_COMPUTE, MANUAL_ADJUSTMENT, VOID_REVERSAL) |
                                                # "TIME" (CLOSE_RELEASE, FX_REMEASUREMENT, NETTING_RECLASS)
    txn_currency: str; functional_currency: str
    amount_txn: int; amount_functional: int     # signed minor units (debit positive)
    reason_code: str | None = None              # subledger_line.reason_code of the summed lines (S14-R-27; D-98 candidate 19 (supervisor ruling on lane ENG-C8 Q-1, 2026-09-19));
                                                # None when the lines carry none. CV-25: omitted from the hash view when None
                                                # (the D-93 (5) `direction` precedent), so a bundle without tagged lines hashes as before
                                                # the member existed apart from the engine_version stamp
                                                # tuple order extends §0.4: (…, period_key, origin_period_key or "", posting_class, reason_code or "")
    rate_refs: tuple[tuple[str, str], ...] = ()  # rev 1.43: the distinct (rate_key, version_key) of the rates stamped on the summed
                                                # lines (T-SL-04 fx_rate_id), in that order (CV-45); not a grouping member. Stage 14
                                                # names them on a delta that only reverses the amount (ENGINE_SPEC_B S14-R-28); a
                                                # reference may belong to a rate-set version superseded since and is not required to be
                                                # in fx_rates. Empty for a same-currency amount. CV-25: omitted from the hash view when
                                                # empty (the reason_code precedent), so a single-currency bundle hashes as before
```

Table 0.4-A: judgement outcomes the engine reads (`JudgementInput.outcome`; stored in `judgement_record.questionnaire` and validated by the 04 T-CON-19 questionnaire schemas by topic, rev 1.2; OQ-A-21 resolved by D-76; answer keys supply these members through the judgement member `questionnaire` of dev-guide §9.5.4, rev 1.3)

| E-56 topic | Members read | Used by |
|---|---|---|
| `COLLECTIBILITY` | none (the event payload `is_probable` decides; the record is evidence) | §2 |
| `NOT_A_CONTRACT` | `consideration_nonrefundable` (`true`/`false`), `event_c_met_on` (date, optional) | §2.3 S02-R-07 |
| `CONTRACT_TERM` | `enforceable_end_date` (date), `termination_penalty_substantive` (`true`/`false`) | §2.3 S02-R-06 |
| `COMBINATION` | none | §2 |
| `POB_DISTINCT_OVERRIDE` | `obligation_key`, `distinctness` (E-105), `integrates_into_obligation_key` (optional) | §3.3 S03-R-05 |
| `SERIES_CLASSIFICATION` | `obligation_key`, `series_increment_unit` | §3.3 S03-R-06 |
| `PRINCIPAL_AGENT` | `product_code` or `obligation_key`, `conclusion` (E-89), `gross_to_net_basis` (`COMMISSION_RATE` \| `FIXED_FEE` \| `SUPPLIER_COST`), `rate` or `amount` | §3.3 S03-R-09 |
| `LICENCE_NATURE` | `obligation_key`, `nature` (E-90), `activities_significantly_affect_ip` (`true`/`false`, IFRS15 book), `functionality_expected_to_change_substantively` and `customer_required_to_use_updated_ip` (`true`/`false`, ASC606 book, answered together, `FUNCTIONAL` only; 606-10-55-62(a), (b); D-91, rev pending ENA-4b) | §3.3 S03-R-10 |
| `WARRANTY_TYPE` | `obligation_key`, `warranty_type` (E-91) | §3.3 S03-R-08 |
| `SFC_ASSESSMENT` | `obligation_key` or `""`, `significant` (`true`/`false`), `exception_32_17` (`A` \| `B` \| `C` \| `NONE`) | §4.3 S04-R-10 |
| `CONSTRAINT` | `estimate_key` (the element code of the record's contract, as 04 T-CON-19 and dev-guide §9.5.4 define it; the engine also accepts the §0.4 qualified key `<contract external_id>/<element_code>`; the record may sit on the contract or on the element's obligation — D-93 (3)), `remote` (`true`/`false`) | §4.3; ENGINE_SPEC_B §9 (breakage) |
| `REPURCHASE_CLASSIFICATION` | `obligation_key`, `outcome` (`FINANCING` \| `LEASE` \| `RIGHT_OF_RETURN` \| `SALE`) | §3.3 S03-R-12 |
| `BILL_AND_HOLD` | `obligation_key`, `reason_substantive`, `identified_as_customer_product`, `ready_for_physical_transfer`, `cannot_use_or_direct_to_another_customer` (`true`/`false`; 606-10-55-83(a) to (d)) | ENGINE_SPEC_B §9.2.4 S09-R-12 (rev 1.2; OQ-B-04 resolved by D-76) |
| `OTHER` | `pol_044_override` (`true`) with `estimate_key`; `claim_enforceable` (`true`) with `estimate_key` (the element code of the record's contract; the engine also accepts the §0.4 qualified key; the record may sit on the contract or on the element's obligation — D-93 (3), S04-R-04 gate); `returns_immaterial` (`true`) with `obligation_key`; `discount_exception_bundle` (bundle product code) | §5.4 S05-R-11, S05-R-14; §4.3 S04-R-04, S04-R-08; §6.4 S06-R-20 |

Types referenced and not expanded above (`MaterialRightInput`, `ModificationInput`, `NoncashInput`, `PayableInput`, `PaymentPointInput`, `PortfolioInput`) carry exactly the 04 columns or payload members named in the section that consumes them (§3.3, §6.2, §4.3).

### 0.5 Output bundle schema

```python
class OutputBundle:                             # DG §7 field list
    engine_version: str
    input_sha256: str
    books: tuple[BookOutput, ...]               # RCP-11 order
    diagnostics: tuple[Diagnostic, ...]         # non-blocking findings (WARNING, INFO), CV-43 order
    def sha256(self) -> str: ...                # canonical.sha256_hex(self)

class BookOutput:
    book_code: str
    contract_version: ContractVersionOut | None # T-CON-08 columns without surrogate ids; trace_nodes column -> node id for every value column (CV-50 links); version_no assigned by the orchestrator;
                                                # None for the LEGACY book, which carries only posting intents and trace (OQ-B-16 resolved by D-76)
    status_in_book: tuple[tuple[str, str], ...] # (contract key, E-17) per member
    obligation_versions: tuple[ObligationVersionOut, ...]   # T-CON-11 columns; trace_nodes measure -> node id; subject key
    balances: tuple[BalanceOut, ...]            # T-CON-09 (stage 10); trace_nodes measure -> node id for every value column (CV-50 links)
    schedules: tuple[ScheduleLineOut, ...]      # T-ENG-02 (ENGINE_SPEC_B §0.6)
    cost_asset_versions: tuple[CostAssetVersionOut, ...]    # T-CON-16 (stage 11)
    loss_provision_versions: tuple[LossProvisionOut, ...]   # T-CON-17 (stage 11)
    fx_layer_movements: tuple[FxLayerMovementOut, ...]      # T-CON-18 (stage 12)
    posting_intents: tuple[PostingIntent, ...]  # balanced per entry (stage 14)
    proposals: tuple[ProposalOut, ...]          # modification treatments (§6.4) and allocation proposals (§5.4)
    time_triggers: tuple[TimeTrigger, ...]      # RCP-10: (kind, date, subject_key)
    trace: Trace                                # DG §5.16; one per book

class Diagnostic:
    code: str; severity: str                    # "WARNING" | "INFO"
    book_code: str | None; subject_key: str | None; event_key: str | None
    detail: Mapping[str, str]                   # no personal data (DG-ENG-06)
```

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| CV-15 | **Blocking findings.** When a stage has collected at least one finding of severity `ERROR` (CV-41), `compute` raises `EngineError(code, message, subject_key, detail)` with `code` = the code of the first `ERROR` finding in CV-43 order and `detail["findings"]` = canonical JSON of every finding collected so far, in CV-43 order. Nothing else is returned (ENG-09). The orchestrator records status `QUARANTINED` and one `exception_item` per distinct blocking code (`source = ENGINE`, `dedupe_key = 'ENGINE:<code>:<group id>'`; RCP-20). Any other exception is a programming error (status `FAILED`) |
| CV-16 | **Proposals are not findings.** Engine-proposed treatments (E-23 per obligation; REQ-MOD-002), discount-exception proposals (POL-076) and combination suggestions are returned in `proposals`. A proposal never changes accounting until an approved command appends the chosen treatment |
| CV-17 | **Policy reads.** Stages read policy values only through `ctx.policies.value(code, contract=, obligation=, entity=, period=)`, which returns the `ResolvedPolicyInput` of the most specific scope present (OBLIGATION, CONTRACT, ENTITY, GROUP) for pin `K` codes and the PERIOD scope for pin `P` codes. A missing value for a code a stage declares in `STAGE_POLICY_KEYS` (§0.10) is a programming error of bundle assembly |

### 0.6 Determinism, natural keys and hashing

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| CV-20 | **Event order.** Events are processed in `(effective_date, record_seq, event_key)` order (ENG-06). Within a boundary handler, obligations are processed ascending by obligation subject key (CV-21) |
| CV-21 | **Subject keys.** Obligation `<contract external_id>/<obligation_key>`; contract and entity `<contract external_id>@<entity code>`; group and entity `<group code>@<entity code>`; estimate element `<contract external_id>/<element_code>` or `PORTFOLIO:<portfolio code>/<element_code>`; cost asset `<contract external_id>/COST/<event local key>`; recognition component `<obligation subject key>#<component code>` (ENGINE_SPEC_B §0.3). In every component the characters `%`, `/`, `@`, `#` and `:` are percent-encoded (`%25`, `%2F`, `%40`, `%23`, `%3A`); every other character, including spaces, is kept. Keys compare by Unicode code point. **Lookups (rev 1.23; D-98 candidate 76, ENG-COST-ENC-1, ENG-COST-LOOKUP-R1).** Every engine index, ledger or pin lookup keys on this encoded form — the estimate pins (`EstimatePins.of_contract`), the quantity ledger, the subject and trace node ids — and never matches an unencoded external id as a raw string prefix. Malformedness is a property of the entry's own structure, never of the requested raw prefix (ENG-COST-LOOKUP-R1): an element key is exactly `<head>/<element>` — one separator, both parts non-empty, no unencoded `/`, `@` or `#`, `:` only as the `PORTFOLIO:` marker of the head, and every `%` one of the five escapes in their canonical uppercase form, compared case-sensitively (a lowercase token such as `%2f` in a stored key is malformed, never normalised into acceptance; ENG-COST-LOOKUP-R1a) — and an entry whose structure is malformed is refused by name (`ENGINE_INVARIANT_VIOLATED`, rule CV-21, the entry and the requested contract) rather than skipped, so a lookup can never fall back to a default silently; a well-formed entry under the same raw prefix whose encoded head is another contract's is that contract's valid key and is simply not selected (raw `C%2F2` encodes to `C%252F2`; the key `C%2F2/EAC` belongs to raw `C/2`) (the defect: a contract id containing `/` lost its approved `RENEWAL_EXPECTATION` in stage 11 and amortised over the contract term, moving the CHK-EX2 P01 carrying by 714.29). [J] Obligation keys are unique only within a contract, and a combination group computes several contracts at once |
| CV-22 | **Event keys.** Global event key `<contract external_id, CV-21 encoded>/EV-<stream_version zero-padded to 6 digits>`, for example `Contract 1/EV-000004`. The local form `EV-000004` is used only inside keys already prefixed by the contract (cost assets). FX layer keys use the global form: `layer_key` = `<balance role>:<global event key>` (04 T-CON-18 rev 1.2; OQ-A-02 resolved by D-76) |
| CV-23 | **Collections.** Every mapping or set is iterated in sorted key order (ENG-05). Output tuples are sorted: obligation versions and balances by subject key; schedule lines by (schedule_kind, subject_key, line_type, period start); proposals by (kind, subject key); time triggers by (date, kind, subject key); diagnostics per CV-43 |
| CV-24 | **No generated identity.** Stages never create UUIDs or read clocks; `version_no`, surrogate ids and `created_at` are assigned by the orchestrator (ENG-01) |
| CV-25 | **Input hash.** `input_sha256 = canonical.sha256_hex(view)` where `view` is the `InputBundle` with `known_at` removed (ENG-08). Reference versions contribute their full content and `content_sha256`; events contribute `payload` and `payload_sha256`; `posted`, period states and `previous_stream_heads` are included because they change outputs. Exception (D-93 (5); rev 1.8): the estimate-version member `direction` is omitted from `view` when it is absent or equal to its documented default (`bundle.py` `hash_view`), so a bundle authored before the member existed (pre-0.3.0) canonicalises to the same view as before. Limits: a non-default `direction` stays in the hash; the raw dataclass canonicalisation still encodes the member — the exception applies to the CV-25 view only; the `ENGINE_VERSION` stamp still contributes to `input_sha256` and to every output hash, so the exception does not make historical full hashes equal across 0.2.0 → 0.3.0 and does not make stored computations byte-identically replayable; replay is version-pinned to the stored `engine_release` (RCP-28..30) and REL-06 candidate-upgrade validation remains mandatory (SOP-3) |
| CV-26 | **Output hash and commuting arrivals (rev 1.25, D-97 (6)).** (a) Hash identity: `OutputBundle.sha256()` covers every field including `input_sha256`; the orchestrator stores it as `contract_version.output_sha256` for every book of the computation; for identical canonical input it is identical across runs, processes and machines (byte-identical, version-pinned replay; the D-96 RCP-28a L0 oracle). (b) Commuting-arrival equivalence, over a defined domain: the permuted events keep stable business identities and payloads, every dependency- or order-sensitive relationship (supersession, status updates naming an earlier line, receipts naming an invoice, late events) is preserved, and only the declared arrival metadata (record sequence, `known_at`) is permuted; same-date stage 12 layers may regroup and are compared by transaction and functional amount, unit and currency per (book, entity, movement kind, role pair, date, currencies): for permutations of commuting arrivals (events whose canonical order is not fixed by effective date and ENG-06 order) every book and every output value column — obligation versions, balances, schedule lines, posting intents — is figure-identical and the stage 12 layers are total-identical per (book, entity, movement kind, role pair, date, currencies), while `input_sha256` and same-date layer keys may differ, because the arrival order is canonical input. PROP:P8 asserts (a) and (b) in that form |
| CV-27 | **Time triggers.** Stage 03 emits `TimeTrigger("MATERIAL_RIGHT_EXPIRY", expiry_date, obligation subject key)` for every open option with an `expiry_date` (RCP-10); no other trigger kind exists in 1.0 |

### 0.7 Numbers, the Decimal context and rounding helpers

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| CV-30 | **Types.** Inside stages, exact amounts, quantities, ratios and rates are `fractions.Fraction`; posted amounts are `int` minor units; `Decimal` exists only in bundle fields and is converted by `money.to_fraction` in stage 01. `compute` runs inside `decimal.localcontext(money.DECIMAL_CONTEXT)` (prec 38, `ROUND_HALF_UP`, traps `FloatOperation`, `InvalidOperation`, `DivisionByZero`, `Overflow`; ENG-03). `guards.no_floats` runs on entry and exit (`FLOAT_DETECTED`) |
| CV-31 | **Forbidden operations.** No `float`, builtin `round`, `math` function, fractional power or `Decimal.quantize` outside `erev_engine/money.py` (ENG-04; DG-ARC-02, DG-ARC-06). Compounding uses integer powers of rational rates only (§4.3 S04-R-12) |
| CV-32 | **Division.** Every division is exact. A stage checks a divisor before dividing; a zero divisor with a non-zero dividend raises the finding `NON_FINITE_AMOUNT` (`ERROR`) with `detail = {formula_id, subject_key}`, except where a rule names a specific code (`MOD_PROGRESS_UNDEFINED`, `TOTAL_SSP_ZERO`, `TOTAL_WEIGHT_ZERO`). Zero divided by zero is defined per rule and is never NaN |
| CV-33 | **Minor units.** μ(c) = `bundle.currencies[c].minor_unit`. No code assumes two decimals (DG-KRN-MONEY-04) |
| CV-34 | **Quota pairs.** An allocation is carried as `Quota(x_exact: Fraction, a_posted: int)`. For a total T_posted in minor units apportioned over weights w_p: `a_posted = largest_remainder(T_posted, w, keys)[p]` and `x_exact = (T_posted ÷ 10^μ) × w_p ÷ Σw`. The quota is taken of the posted total, never of an unrounded total, so that \|x_exact × 10^μ − a_posted\| < 1 (ALG-01 §2.1.2 guarantee); the rounding residue of the total itself is traced on the `transaction_price` node (§4.4) |
| CV-35 | **Posted cumulative amounts.** Every posted cumulative amount derived from an allocation is `money.cumulative_posted(x_exact, a_posted, f, μ)` (ALG-01 §2.1.3 as confirmed by D-11a; DG-KRN-MONEY-03); period amounts are differences of posted cumulative amounts (`money.period_amounts`). A cumulative amount not derived from an allocation (deposit balances, option SSP, interest, measured targets) is `money.round_half_up(exact cumulative, μ)`, and its period amount is the difference of rounded cumulative values. Rounding `a_posted × f` is forbidden (DG-KRN-MONEY-03; CHK-007) |
| CV-36 | **Apportionment.** Every split of one amount into parts (allocation, bundle explosion, agent supplier split, pools, portfolio results, intercompany) uses `money.largest_remainder` with the keys of the parts. Tie order: larger fractional remainder, larger weight, ascending key by Unicode code point (D-11; DG-KRN-MONEY-02). The key of an obligation is its subject key |
| CV-37 | **Signs.** D-12: `net_position` positive means contract liability. Cumulative targets are signed; posting intents carry a side and a non-negative amount (POLICIES §0.9). `round_half_up` is symmetric for negatives (−0.005 USD → −0.01) |

EX-00-A: rounding helpers (checks CHK-001, CHK-003c, CHK-003d, CHK-007; USD, μ = 2)

| Input | Helper | Result |
|---|---|---|
| T 10,000 minor units; weights 1, 1, 1; keys POB-001 to POB-003 | `largest_remainder` | 3,334 / 3,333 / 3,333 (33.34 / 33.33 / 33.33) |
| T −10,000; weights 1, 1, 1 | `largest_remainder` | −33.34 / −33.33 / −33.33 |
| T 3; weights 1, 2, 3 (remainders 1/2, 0, 1/2) | `largest_remainder` | 0 / 1 / 2 (tie broken by the larger weight) |
| X = 1,300 × 368 ÷ 2,018 = 237.066402…; A = 23,707; f = 1/2 | `cumulative_posted` | 11,853 (118.53, not round(237.07 × 0.5) = 118.54) |
| same, f = 1 | `cumulative_posted` | 23,707; period amount 118.54 |
| X = −100/3; A = −3,334; f = 1/2 | `cumulative_posted` | −1,667 (−16.67), bounded to [A, 0] |

### 0.8 Findings

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| CV-40 | **Type.** `Finding(code, severity, subject_key, detail)` in `erev_engine/stages/state.py` (ENGINE_SPEC_B §0.6), with two further members `stage: int` and `event_key: str \| None` used for ordering only |
| CV-41 | **Severity.** 04 §15.4 finding severities. `ERROR` blocks the computation (CV-15) and maps to exception severity `BLOCKING` (E-43). `WARNING` and `INFO` become `OutputBundle.diagnostics` and exception items of the same severity |
| CV-42 | **Collection.** A stage processes every subject it can before returning, so that one computation reports every independent blocking finding. After a stage returns, `compute` raises CV-15 if any `ERROR` exists; no later stage runs |
| CV-43 | **Order.** Findings sort by (stage, code, subject_key or `""`, event_key or `""`, canonical JSON of detail) |
| CV-44 | **Import-stage codes.** Codes of 04 table 15.4-A whose stage is header, type, cross-row, file or cross-file are raised by the platform import pipeline (05 IPL-03 to IPL-05) and never by the engine, except the cross-file codes that stage 01 re-checks over the whole stream (Table 0.8-A), because a void or a late event can make an earlier valid stream invalid |
| CV-45 | **Programming errors.** A malformed bundle (unsorted tuple, unknown literal, a referenced version absent, V6 violated in an approved estimate version, a period missing) is a programming error of bundle assembly: `ValueError` or `EngineError("ENGINE_INVARIANT_VIOLATED")`, status `FAILED` or `QUARANTINED` per 05 §3.9 |
| CV-46 | **Invariants.** A stage invariant `S<nn>-INV-<nn>` that fails raises `EngineError("ENGINE_INVARIANT_VIOLATED", detail={"invariant": id, …})`, persisted as exception `ENGINE_INVARIANT_VIOLATION` (DG-ENG-05; 04 table 15.4-D) |
| CV-47 | **Legacy export columns of T-CON-11 (D-98 candidates 88 and 90; supervisor rulings of 2026-09-20; lane ENG-T1F slice T1F-2).** (a) **Memos.** `memo_1`, `memo_2`, `memo_3` of an obligation version are passed through unchanged: per memo, the value of the latest `MEMO_UPDATED` event of the obligation with effective date ≤ d_v that names it, else the booking line's memo (CONTRACT_BOOKED `lines[].memo_1..3`), else NULL; text, no node. **Which members an event names (Codex T1F2-MEMO-R1):** an event carrying the presence set `named` (04 §16.3; an additive optional member under DG-KRN-EVT-03's additive-optional-member clause) names exactly those members, and a named member takes the event's value — a supplied null clears the memo; members outside `named` keep their earlier value whatever the serialized payload shows for them (the stored form writes every model member, so an omitted sibling appears as null). `named` admits every member the writer can supply — `memo_1`, `memo_2`, `memo_3`, `custom_attributes`, `dimensions` (the `update-memos` member set) — so a structured-only or mixed update is valid; the structured members keep their existing semantics (the member sent replaces; the engine reads the three memos only) (Codex T1F2 structured-members finding on 8628b6e3). `named` is server-derived from the members the writer actually submitted, at every producer: the API `update-memos` (the request's supplied members), the generic record-events / preview route (the submitted payload members; a client-supplied `named` must equal that set or the item is a finding — it never overrides the submitted members), the legacy progress import (the non-blank memo cells). The header projection written at append time (`erev_api/events/stream.py`, T-CON-01 header scope) reads EVERY header member — the three memos and `custom_attributes` — from `named` alone when it is present (a member the stored form shows as null but `named` does not list is not written), so header rows and the engine's replay agree for new events; this holds equally for an approval submission (a mixed `LINE_ATTRIBUTES_CHANGED` + `MEMO_UPDATED` batch is stored as `payload_json` bodies, re-parsed and appended on approval): the stored body carries `named`, the re-parse never re-derives presence from its nulls, and an omitted `custom_attributes` clears nothing. A submission stored before this revision (no `named`) keeps the earlier `model_fields_set` reading of its re-parsed body — no presence set is invented for it (Codex 1510 (b)). **Events without `named` (persisted before this revision):** the engine's replay reads them as "strings replace, nulls are omissions" — a NARROW compatibility rule for the obligation-version columns only, which preserves exactly the engine fold's own prior reading; it recovers no historical writer intent (the stored body cannot distinguish an omitted member from a supplied null) and it does not restate the header projection, which applied a supplied null from the live model at append time. Stored header rows, stored versions and stored event bytes are preserved as they are; no historical presence set is manufactured; HTTP idempotency keeps hashing the original parsed request body (null ≠ omission). (b) **Unit revenue rates.** `original_unit_revenue_rate` = x_p ÷ Q (S05-R-16; NULL when Q = 0) and `remaining_unit_revenue_rate` = exact remaining allocation (Table 0.9-A `remaining_allocation`, exact value) ÷ `remaining_quantity`, 0 when the remaining quantity is 0 (legacy 01 §3.6, §3.7: NaN → 0); both exact, emitted as version-state nodes `original_unit_revenue_rate:<ob>:-` and `remaining_unit_revenue_rate:<ob>:-` by the stage 13 output step (`books.unit_revenue_rate.v1`) and linked (CV-50 links; a NULL rate has no node). **Operands and sources (D-98 candidate 117; rev 1.28):** the original rate divides the RAW quota x_p by the RAW quantity Q (never the 18-place values of encoded nodes); its inputs cite the node(s) that produced the CURRENT original allocation, last the `original_quantity:<ob>:-` node: at inception the `original_allocated_exact:<ob>:-` node together with the obligation's targeted share nodes `targeted_vc_allocated@<estimate key>:<ob>:-` (their exact values sum to x_p, S05-R-10), or after an approved SSP repin (S06-R-26, `attributes.reallocate`) the repin's `mod_share@<event key>:<ob>:-` share node alone; for an obligation a modification boundary CREATES, the node that produced its quota at creation — `mod_share@<event key>:<ob>:-` for a native added good (S06-R-14), `allocated_amount@<event key>:<ob>:-` for a legacy retrospective addition (S06-R-29, `legacy_templates`) and for a material-right CONTINUATION good (S06-R-23, `exercise`) — and, because its stage 03 draft is built in a scratch builder, stage 06 emits the created obligation's `original_quantity:<ob>:-` node into the trace at creation (`pob.template_match.v1`, citing the creating event's line as its `contract_event` source, CV-53); the state carries the producing node ids (`original_allocation_nodes`). The alternatives named here are the committed constructors of obligations, not an exhaustive grammar: a constructor added later stamps its producer or the rate is refused, never fabricated from an inception source. The raw operands travel in params `allocation` / `quantity`, and their relationship to the cited producers is a STATED bounded one, at two levels, because the trace cannot carry a non-terminating rational exactly: every node value and `rounding_residue` is a CV-51 `format_exact` encoding, half up at 18 places (an exact-class node such as `original_allocated_exact` holds the encoding and no residue; a posted node holds `format_money` plus `rounding_residue = format_exact(exact − posted)`), and P14 re-evaluation feeds a formula the recomputed VALUES of its inputs, never their residues (DG-KRN-EXP-04). (i) At emission the step binds each raw operand to the EXACT values of its cited nodes (`value + rounding_residue`, DG-KRN-EXP-03): the distance from the operand to Σ exact is at most n × ½ × 10⁻¹⁸ for n cited nodes — the actual contract, full stop: equality follows only when the reconstruction sum happens to equal the raw value, which the bound does not establish for n ≥ 2 (Codex 0304: 301/2 against 101/2 + (100 + 10⁻¹⁸) is admitted) — else `ENGINE_INVARIANT_VIOLATED` (never a silent substitution). (ii) At re-evaluation `reevaluate` supplies `books.unit_revenue_rate.v1` — a formula registered in `formulas.EXACT_INPUT_FORMULAS` — each cited node's DG-KRN-EXP-03 exact value (the recomputed value plus the node's stored `rounding_residue`, trace data that is not recomputed; the value itself for an exact-class node), (dev-guide DG-KRN-EXP-04, rev 1.52), so the formula binds the params against the SAME reconstruction the emitting step read through `TraceBuilder.exact`, under the same limit: the allocation within n × ½ × 10⁻¹⁸ of the sum of the n cited exact values, the quantity within ½ × 10⁻¹⁸ of the quantity node's. The limit is derived from the CV-51 representation — half a unit at 18 places per independently encoded component — never from a currency, trailing digits, denominator reduction or observed deltas. Refused at both levels: a cited 600 beside a raw 600.5, a cited quantity 1 beside 2, a cited 0.1 beside 0.2, the inception node after a repin (600 against 2000/3), the relative share alone beside a targeted total (101/2 against 301/2); accepted on both paths: the repin's 2000/3 against posted 666.67 with residue −0.003333333333333333 (reconstruction error 1/(3 × 10¹⁸)). No minor-unit tolerance exists at either level, and neither level is a rate tolerance: the published rate is the exact raw quotient; the limit qualifies only the source relationship. A node whose STORED `rounding_residue` does not reconstruct its producer's exact value is refused at both levels (the residue is the authentication, not a copy). The remaining rate cites `remaining_allocation` and `remaining_quantity`, its `allocation` param being the exact value of the posted node. (c) **`Current Remaining SSP` export semantics.** The legacy export (04 §17.1 rule 7; LM-CL-34, LM-CL-49) writes the remaining SSP after deliveries — `remaining_quantity × unit_ssp` of the version, exact, which equals legacy's `remaining_ssp − Σ deliveries × unit SSP` since the last re-basing (legacy 01 §3.7) — as a DERIVED export value; the stored `obligation_version.remaining_ssp` keeps its allocation semantics (the extended SSP re-based at stage-06 boundaries only, S06-R-29) and is unchanged. A reporting-semantics alignment, not an allocation change; **flagged for Ray's G12 review**. No answer-key or posted-amount change. |

Rev 1.2: the three codes proposed in rev 1.0 (`PRINCIPAL_AGENT_NOT_ASSESSED`, `VC_TARGET_TOLERANCE_EXCEEDED`, `OPENING_BALANCE_INCONSISTENT`) are adopted by D-76, catalogued in 04 table 15.4-C and listed above. `COMBINATION_SUGGESTED` (04 table 15.4-C) is produced by the platform combination-suggestion function (S02-R-14), which records the exception item with the source that 04 gives it; no engine stage emits it.

### 0.9 Calculation trace

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| CV-50 | **Node ids.** `<measure>:<subject key>:<period key or '-'>` (DG-KRN-EXP-02). Version-state nodes (the T-CON-08 and T-CON-11 columns) use `-` and hold the value as of `obligation_version.effective_date`. Period-end targets use the period key. Nodes of one boundary event use the measure `<name>@<global event key>`, for example `catch_up@Contract 1/EV-000012:Contract 1/POB %233:-`; the measure part never contains `:` because event keys are CV-21 encoded. **Qualifier.** A version-date re-measurement of a column whose boundary node of the same event holds a different value takes the qualifier `returns` in the period slot: `allocation_adjustment@<event key>:<ob>:returns` is the returns-netted adjustment (S09-R-23 REDUCE, stage 09) when `allocation_adjustment@<event key>:<ob>:-` holds the boundary's gross one (stage 06); likewise `expected_returns_amount@<event key>:<group>:returns` and `transaction_price@<event key>:<group>:returns` (stage 13 output measures, `books.version_adjustment.v1`) hold the memo at d_v when the boundary's build-up node holds the expected returns at the unit rate in force before the event; the qualified node is the link (lane ENG-T1F, Codex T1F-R3 ruling). A boundary's build-up nodes are traced as the handler measured them: the handler prices the state before its event, so the returnable unit rates are those in force before the event when the rates after it do not reproduce the appended price. **Links (D-97 (8)).** Every `erev.money` and `erev.exact` column of T-CON-08, T-CON-09 and T-CON-11 the engine publishes links the node that produced its stored value (DG-KRN-EXP-01): a column re-measured at a reallocating boundary links that boundary's `<column>@<event key>` node, never the inception version-state node when the two differ (T1-F-2: `fixed_consideration` 1,200.00 links `fixed_consideration@<event>:<group>:-`, not the inception 1,000.00); a column summed over several nodes links a sum node the producing stage emits (T-CON-09 member balances per `<contract>@<entity>` and period, T-CON-08 obligation sums); zero balances link their zero node. **`original_allocated_exact` and `original_allocated_amount` (D-98 candidate 121; lane ENG-T1F, rev 1.28)** link the node that produced the CURRENT original allocation: the stage 05 version-state node at inception; with targeted shares the S05-R-10 sum nodes `<column>@<booking event key>:<ob>:-` stage 05 emits over the relative node and the `targeted_vc_allocated@` shares (`alloc.original_total.v1` — exact inputs, its `exact` param bound to the components' exact values within the CV-51 limit; `alloc.original_total_amount.v1` — Σ of the cited POSTED contributions, each counted once, which is the column's actual computation: S05-R-10 `totals` sums the posted base share and every posted targeted share separately, a repin or a creation preserves the already apportioned posted share, and the assembler never posts or rounds the raw total again (Codex 0312) — so the amount node is never a re-rounding of the exact node and the two are not derived from each other); after an S06-R-26 repin and for an obligation a boundary creates (S06-R-14, S06-R-29, S06-R-23) the `<column>@<event key>:<ob>:-` pair the boundary emits over its producer (`mod_share@`, `allocated_amount@`). The assembler selects the link by PRODUCER IDENTITY, never by value: the pair stamped on the state at the write of the original allocation (`original_allocation_links` — the three writes: inception, the common added-obligation constructor, the SSP repin; a later recognition or current-segment event never replaces it), else the stage 05 version-state node; it then asserts that the identified node holds the stored value (the posted amount exactly; the exact quota within half a unit at 18 places) and fails closed otherwise — never the partial relative node beside a targeted total, never a stale inception node after a repin, never a node chosen because it happens to hold the value. **The six snapshot columns (D-98 candidates 123 and 124; lane ENG-T1F, rev 1.29)** — `original_ssp_selected`, `original_unit_ssp`, `original_total_contract_ssp`, `original_stated_price`, `original_total_contract_price`, `allocation_weight` — link the node that produced the CURRENT value: the stage 05 version-state node at inception (the residual alias unchanged); after an S06-R-26 repin, for the target, `original_ssp_selected@<event key>:<ob>:-` (stage 05's own resolution emitted at the repin with the corrected version's canonical `ssp_entry` sources — never the currency-less `mod_weight@` amount node) and `original_unit_ssp@<event key>:<ob>:-` over that node and `original_quantity`, and for every reallocated obligation `original_total_contract_ssp@<event key>:<ob>:-` (`alloc.original_total.v1` — exact inputs, its `exact` param bound to the raw Σ within the CV-51 limit (the later clause of this row; Codex production-20260921-1010 §1 C1 aligned the label) — over the repin's `mod_weight@<event key>:<member>:-` weight nodes — the corrected and inception selected SSPs of the group's members, the summands the repin actually used); the inception nodes stay in the trace as history and are not the link. For an obligation a boundary creates (S06-R-14 native, S06-R-29 legacy retrospective, S06-R-23 continuation) whose stage 03 draft is built in a scratch builder, stage 06 emits the draft's stage 03 `original_quantity` node into the trace at creation (its `stated_price` node is NOT re-emitted — the boundary state derives its `stated_price@<event key>` node from the line and would double it) and the creating boundary's `<column>@<event key>:<ob>:-` nodes over its own producers: `original_ssp_selected@` from the added line's SSP resolution (its canonical sources), `original_unit_ssp@` over it and the quantity node, `original_stated_price@` echoing the boundary state's `stated_price@<event key>:<ob>:-` node (emitted once the boundary state exists, under an id reserved at creation), `original_total_contract_price@` echoing the boundary's `transaction_price@<event key>:<group>:-` build-up, `original_total_contract_ssp@` the constructor's weight total (the S06-R-11 boundary weights `mod_weight@<event key>` summed for native and continuation creations; the legacy template's stored Σ remaining SSP re-evaluated from the template's own per-key parameters), and `allocation_weight@` the governed dimensionless ratio w ÷ Σw of that constructor (native: the added line's SSP over the pooled and added boundary weights; continuation: the good's weight over the optioned goods' weights, the posted / exact transfer budgets untouched; legacy retrospective: (remaining + delivered SSP) over Σ(remaining + delivered SSP) — NOT the stored total) — never the missing-node zero default, which stays for the excluded-line row alone (S06-R-11 admits a non-negative weight: a legitimate 0 ÷ Σw ratio is 0 and still links its real producer; no positivity requirement); a NULL point-resolution field needs no fabricated node. The three derived producers carry their RAW operands as params bound to the cited producers within the CV-51 limit and replay to the raw value — one emission / replay contract (Codex production-20260921-0954): `original_unit_ssp@` = `alloc.unit_ssp.v1` with params `ssp` (the raw selected SSP of the resolution) and `quantity` bound to the cited selected-SSP and quantity nodes (without params the formula is unchanged: the stage 05 inception node); `original_total_contract_ssp@` = `alloc.original_total.v1` over the boundary's weight nodes with `exact` = the raw total; `allocation_weight@` = `alloc.original_weight.v1` (new; declared for stages 05 and 06) over the own weight node and the total node with params `weight` / `total` = the raw operands, returning the raw governed ratio w ÷ Σw; the legacy template's roles `total_ssp` / `weight` re-evaluate from its own parameters. **Required producers:** the assembler selects these links by producer identity stamped on the state at the write (`snapshot_producer_links`), asserts each holds the published value, and REFUSES the assembly when a stamped producer is absent from the trace or does not hold the value — no silent skip, no fabricated node, no equal-value alias; the reserved `original_total_contract_price@` echo REFUSES when stage 13 traced no `transaction_price@<event key>` build-up for the creating event (the price basis actually consumed). **Contract-permitted absences (Codex production-20260921-0958 §1; -1010 §1 C2; -1025 §3; -1035 §2):** a snapshot column's `trace_nodes` link may name, instead of a node id, a contract-permitted ABSENCE of the producer — a STABLE identifier: the fixed prefix `absent:` and a closed enumeration of reasons declared once (`erev_engine.trace`: `ABSENT_ZERO_TOTAL` = `absent:zero-total`, `ABSENT_NO_RESOLUTION` = `absent:no-ssp-resolution`, and `ABSENCE_COLUMNS`, the reason admitted per column); the explanation lives here, never in the stored marker. The assembler asserts a stamped absence BY IDENTITY against the reason admitted for that column (a generic `absent:` prefix, an unknown reason, or a known reason on another column is refused, `ENGINE_INVARIANT_VIOLATED`), accepts it only when the column publishes the existing display 0 (refused otherwise), fabricates no node, and publishes the named absence itself as the column's link — a consumer tells it from an unlinked column and from a node id, and the explain service answers "not traced" naming the absence; a stamped node id that is absent from the trace, and a reserved echo whose build-up stage 13 did not trace, keep refusing (the required-producer contract above). The two admitted states, each from its identified constructor: (1) `absent:zero-total` for `allocation_weight` of an obligation a legacy PROSPECTIVE template creates when Σw = Σ RemSSP′ = 0 (DEV-054: the pool is 0 or goes to the completed receivers) — there is no governed ratio, no 0 ÷ 0 is manufactured, no `allocation_weight@` node exists, the column publishes the DEV-054 display 0, and the total node (Σ RemSSP′ = 0, role `total_ssp`) is emitted and linked; (2) `absent:no-ssp-resolution` for `original_ssp_selected` of a LEGACY-VC line a legacy template creates (`added_mod_ssp`'s positive-Q VC branch returns the zero band without a lookup — no resolution, no `original_ssp_selected@` node fabricated), publishing the existing non-NULL 0 through `_selected_ssp` and a NULL `original_unit_ssp`. That non-NULL selected 0 is RESOLVED, not under review: the marker is a REPRESENTATION of the constructor-established state 04 line 3275 already rules (non-NULL `original_ssp_selected` = 0 for VC lines; the S06-R-31 clamped modification SSP), not a ruling on the legacy no-resolution / zero admission itself and not an admission of any other missing resolution — a failed non-VC lookup stays its separate refusal path and is never represented as an absence . **`progress_ratio` producer (rev 1.31; D-98 candidate 134; T1F-LP-PR-1 after Codex production-20260921-1249's diagnosis):** the T-CON-11 `progress_ratio` column links a stage-09 producer at every version date: the measured progress node of the `FIXED` component, or — under a V4 / V5 / S09-R-13 guard, or when no `FIXED` component exists — the `progress_ratio:<ob>:<period | ->` node of `rec.progress.unmeasured.v1` (value 0; params `as_of` and `reason` = the guard string with its status, or `no-fixed-component`; arity 0), the rule-produced zero of S02-R-03 with its status and date preserved — never the missing-node zero default, never an absence marker, never a node of another measure |
| CV-51 | **Values.** Posted amounts: `format_money` with `rounding_residue = format_exact(exact − posted)`; exact values: `format_exact(x)` with `rounding_residue = None` (DG-KRN-EXP-03) |
| CV-52 | **Formula ids.** `<area>.<name>.v<n>` registered in `erev_engine/formulas.py` with a re-evaluation function (RCP-25). Areas of stages 01 to 08: `ingest`, `step1`, `pob`, `tp`, `ssp`, `alloc`, `mod`, `onb`, `estimate`, `late`; the area `input` holds `input.echo.v1`, the identity over one input, for a stored column that echoes a canonical input field or copies another node (D-97 (8)). Each stage section lists its formula ids |
| CV-53 | **Inputs.** Node inputs are node ids or `SourceRef` with `ref_id` = natural key (event key, SSP entry key, rule key, rate key, estimate version key, registry `source_ref`, import row reference from the event payload). The explain service resolves natural keys to rows (DG-KRN-EXP-06). A source input carries its value in `params["value"]` (DG-KRN-EXP-04) or in `detail["value"]`; an `input.echo.v1` node cites the canonical input field it echoes as one `SourceRef` whose `detail` names `member` (the 04 column or payload member) and `value` (D-97 (8)); a `member` may only name a canonical input member that holds the cited value (a derived figure — an extended SSP, a per-event revenue effect, a posted change — is a calculation node over its canonical sources, or cites the event with `detail["role"]` naming the contribution instead of a `member`; lane ENG-T1F, Codex T1F-R1). An `ssp_range` source names the band row `<entry key>/<band_dimension>/<band_from>` (`band_from` empty when open). Alias (D-98 candidate 23, T1F-Q-5): a residual candidate's `original_ssp_selected` column links the existing `residual_ssp:<ob>:-` node that produced R (S05-R-12) — one value, one node; the stage 05 snapshot node keeps 0 (L4-3-Q-13) and no second node is emitted |
| CV-54 | **Narratives.** `narrative_key` = the formula id without its version suffix (for example `mod.pool`) |
| CV-55 | **Aliases.** Stage 13 aliases nodes of memoised stages (RCP-12); a stage never emits an alias itself |
| CV-56 | **Parity measures.** The seven measures of DG-PAR-05 and DG-OQ-05 are emitted with exactly the 04 column names, as version-state nodes, per Table 0.9-A (B1 §6.2 obligation 2) |

Table 0.9-A: measures with posted and exact values (d_v = version effective date; E and C per CV-63)

| Measure (04 column) | Posted value | Exact value (value + `rounding_residue`) | Emitted by |
|---|---|---|---|
| `revenue_cum` | C(d_v) | E(d_v) | stage 09 |
| `remaining_allocation` | a_k − C(d_v) of the segment in force | x_k − E(d_v) | stage 09 |
| `position_obligation` | `billed_cum` − C(d_v) | `billed_cum` − E(d_v) | stage 10 |
| `catch_up_amount` | Σ CU_posted of the boundary events first included in the version (`EventView.is_new`) | Σ CU_exact of the same boundaries | stage 09, summing the stage 06 and stage 08 nodes `catch_up@<event key>` |
| `catch_up_cum` | Σ CU_posted of every boundary with effective date ≤ d_v | Σ CU_exact | stage 09 |
| `netting_reclass_amount` | attribution of the period-end reclass (ALG-02 step 5) | exact attribution quota before largest remainder | stage 10 |
| `remaining_billing` | billing plan − `billed_cum` | exact billing plan − `billed_cum` | stage 10 |

At a boundary with effective date d: CU_exact = E_after(d) − E_before(d) and CU_posted = C_after(d) − C_before(d) (§6.5, §8.3). For a parity template E is the legacy full-precision state, so `exact(catch_up_amount)` equals the golden `cumulative_catchup` value within D-17 tolerance.
