### 0.11 Shared state contract and segment semantics

`ENGINE_SPEC_B.md` §0.5 declares the state stages 09 to 15 consume. This section is authoritative for how stages 01 to 08 produce it (OQ-B-02) and adds the members stages 01 to 08 need. Every class is a frozen dataclass in `erev_engine/stages/state.py`.

```python
class CanonicalBundle:                          # stage 01 output, book-independent
    bundle: InputBundle                         # the original, for hashing and source references
    currencies: CurrencyTable
    group: GroupView                            # GroupInput with products and portfolios as mappings by code
    contracts: Mapping[str, ContractView]       # external_id -> header, booking payload, judgements, material rights, modifications
    events: tuple[EventView, ...]               # after voids (S01-R-12), ENG-06 order
    boundary_events: tuple[EventView, ...]      # Table 0.3-A types, excluding the first CONTRACT_BOOKED of each member
    measure_events: tuple[EventView, ...]       # every other included event
    ledger: QuantityLedger                      # S01-R-16
    templates: TemplateIndex; rule_sets: RuleSetIndex; ssp_books: SspIndex
    estimates: EstimatePins                     # pin(estimate_key, at, before_event_key=None) -> EstimateVersionView | None
    fx: RateIndex; posted: PostedIndex
    books: Mapping[str, BookInput]; entities: Mapping[str, EntityView]
    findings: tuple[Finding, ...]

class EventView:
    event_key: str; contract_key: str; event_type: str; effective_date: date; record_seq: int
    recorded_at: datetime; obligation_subject_keys: tuple[str, ...]
    payload: Mapping[str, object]               # decimals converted to Fraction (CV-30)
    is_new: bool                                # stream_version > previous_stream_heads[contract_key]
    source: SourceRef                           # ref_type "contract_event", ref_id = event_key

class QuantityLedger:                           # cumulative quantities per obligation after each measure event
    def at(self, subject_key: str, *, before: EventView | None = None, on: date | None = None) -> LedgerPoint: ...
class LedgerPoint:
    delivered_cum: Fraction; returned_cum: Fraction; costs_cum: Fraction; hours_cum: Fraction
    output_ratio: Fraction; milestone_weight_cum: Fraction; usage_quantity_cum: Fraction
    billed_cum: Fraction; credited_cum: Fraction; paid_cum: Fraction
    last_event_key: str | None

class Quota:                                    # CV-34
    x_exact: Fraction; a_posted: int

class AllocationSegment:                        # ENGINE_SPEC_B §0.5 members, plus the members marked "added"
    component: str; effective_date: date; event_key: str | None; cause: str; basis: str
    x_exact: Fraction; a_posted: int; base_revenue_posted: int
    base_revenue_exact: Fraction                # added: b_k, exact cumulative revenue at the boundary (CV-63)
    base_progress: ProgressBase; totals: ProgressTotals
    progress_measure: str                       # added: E-11 literal, or "SSP_DELIVERED" (POL-107 parity) or "UNITS_SINCE_BOUNDARY"
    unit_ssp: Fraction | None                   # added: current unit SSP after the boundary (parity reclass key, DEV-075)
    remaining_ssp: Fraction                     # added: exact remaining SSP after the boundary
    remaining_billing_plan: Fraction            # added: billing plan after the boundary (stated price basis)
    estimate_pair: tuple[str | None, str | None]
    modification_boundary_no: int               # added: 25-13(a) boundaries applied through this segment (REQ-MOD-020)
```

`ObligationState` carries the ENGINE_SPEC_B §0.5 members and, added by this file: `template_version_key`, `product_code`, `sku_number`, `stratification`, `series_increment_unit`, `over_time_criterion`, `warranty_type`, `material_right` (option terms and resolved option SSP), `ssp` (the `SspResolution` snapshot of §5.3 feeding the `original_ssp_*` and `ssp_*` columns), `original_quantity`, `original_stated_price`, `stated_price`, `original_allocation: Quota`, `original_total_contract_price`, `original_total_contract_ssp`, `is_vc_line` (parity `VC_LINE`), `gross_to_net` (agent pass-through basis, §3.3), `lineage_pre_modification: tuple[str, ...]` (POL-106), `assurance_cost_per_unit`, `last_modification_key`. `AllocatedState` carries the ENGINE_SPEC_B §0.5 members and `proposals`, `time_triggers`, `tp_history: tuple[TpBuildUp, ...]` (one per boundary, S04-R-01), `targeted_vc_quotas` (latest quota; S05-R-14), `targeted_vc_quota_history` (dated quotas in ENG-06 order; S05-R-14, S08-R-07, ENGINE_SPEC_B S15-R-09; rev 1.6), `refund_components` (S06-R-09, S06-R-21), `concession_history` (the dated `CONCESSION` additions per producing event and receiving obligation, in ENG-06 order; S06-R-09, D-88 L7-5-Q-4 (iii), ENGINE_SPEC_B S10-R-26 rev 1.8) and `findings`. `ObligationState` also carries `inception_weight` (S08-R-04; rev 1.6).

Table 0.11-A: production of the shared state

| Member | Produced by |
|---|---|
| `AllocatedState.contracts`, `status_in_book` | stage 02 (§2) |
| `ObligationState` terms, kind, distinctness, measure, dates, entities, product attributes, option records, warranty split, agent basis, `recognition_start_date` | stage 03 (§3) |
| `tp_unconstrained`, `tp_history`, `specialist_targets` (EMOD-12 from stage 02; EMOD-18, EMOD-21, EMOD-23 and the EMOD-22 memo, ordinary release and asset from stage 04) | stages 02 and 04 (§2.4, §4) |
| `BalanceState.customer_consideration` (the EMOD-22 share-based reduction and the composed release, produced at the stage 10 tail; `RecognitionState.allocated` is not rewritten; consumed by stages 12, 13 and 14) | stage 10 (ENGINE_SPEC_B §10.1, S10-R-26; D-91) |
| `ObligationState.ssp`, `original_*`, first `AllocationSegment` (cause `INCEPTION`) | stage 05 (§5) |
| Segments with cause `MODIFICATION`, `TERMINATION`, `MATERIAL_RIGHT_EXERCISE`; `lineage_pre_modification`; `proposals` of kind `MODIFICATION_TREATMENT` | stage 06 (§6) |
| Segments with cause `OPENING_BALANCE`; `opening` | stage 07 (§7) |
| Segments with cause `ESTIMATE_CHANGE` and `TP_CHANGE`; `estimates` pins | stage 08 (§8); stage 01 builds the pin index |
| `terminated_on` | stage 06 |
| `events`, `measure_events`, `ledger` | stage 01 |
| `return_paths` | stage 04 (S04-R-08b) |

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| CV-60 | **Segments never overlap.** An obligation's segments are ordered by (effective_date, event order). Segment k applies to measure events after its boundary event in ENG-06 order and before the next boundary |
| CV-61 | **INCEPTION basis.** `base_revenue_exact = 0`, `base_revenue_posted = 0`; progress f_k(t) is measured from the obligation's start over the updated totals of the segment: units `delivered_cum(t) ÷ Q_k`; cost `costs_cum(t) ÷ EAC_k`; time `progress.time_fraction(convention, start_k, end_k, t)` (ALG-11); `SSP_DELIVERED` `ssp_delivered_cum(t) ÷ (ssp_delivered_cum(t) + remaining_ssp_k(t))` |
| CV-62 | **PROSPECTIVE basis.** `base_revenue_exact` = E_before(d_k) (parity) or the posted C_before(d_k) as a Fraction (native 25-13(a), ALG-04 posted pool); progress g_k(t) is measured since the boundary over the remaining totals: units `(delivered_cum(t) − delivered_cum(d_k)) ÷ RQ_k`; time elapsed since d_k over the remaining term; cost `(costs_cum(t) − costs_cum(d_k)) ÷ (EAC_k − costs_cum(d_k))`. A remaining total of 0 gives g_k = 1 |
| CV-63 | **Exact and posted cumulative revenue.** E(t) = b_k + (x_k − b_k) × p_k(t), with p_k = f_k (INCEPTION, b_k = 0) or g_k (PROSPECTIVE). Posted C(t) = `money.cumulative_posted(x_k, a_k, φ, μ)` with φ = E(t) ÷ x_k when x_k ≠ 0 (φ = 1 exactly when p_k = 1), and C(t) = 0 when x_k = 0 (then a_k = 0). Stage 09 implements recognition over segments with exactly these formulas; stages 06 and 08 use them to measure catch-ups. [J] One formula keeps D-11a composition on the total cumulative amount, so catch-ups never create one-minor-unit noise on untouched obligations (POLICIES §2.1.3 rationale) |
| CV-64 | **Activity columns.** T-CON-11 activity columns (`delivered_quantity`, `revenue_amount`, `billed_amount`, `ssp_delivered`, `catch_up_amount`, `pre_standard_revenue_amount`) aggregate the effects of events with `is_new = true` (DEV-051: every period measure is 0 on non-delivery events). **Exact revenue activity (rev 1.30; D-98 candidate 134; T1F-89-1 after Codex production-20260921-1155 §1 / -1249 §2):** the `revenue_amount` node keeps its posted value P = Σ (C_after − C_before) over the version's admitted new events (`rec.activity_sum.v1`, unchanged) and binds the exact activity A = Σ (E_after − E_before) as its exact (`rounding_residue` = A − P), E being the exact segment target `Evaluation.exact` at the event's OWN ENG-06 before / after positions — never a difference of period-end snapshots. The exact side is its own replayable chain, per admitted event and side: the endpoint progress node `progress_ratio_<side>@<event key>:<ob>:-` (the production progress formula and params at that position), the `FIXED` exact endpoint `revenue_target_exact_<side>@<event key>:<ob>#FIXED:-` (the production `rec.target_exact.*` formula over that progress, raw params), the endpoint sum `revenue_exact_<side>@<event key>:<ob>:-` (`rec.exact_endpoint.v1` over the `FIXED` endpoint and the admitted realised `PERIOD_VC` amounts, its raw sum bound to the cited inputs; a guarded endpoint is the defined 0 with its guard named), the event delta `revenue_exact_delta@<event key>:<ob>:-` (`rec.exact_difference.v1`, inputs (after, before), raw operands bound to the cited endpoints within the CV-51 limit) and the companion `revenue_amount_exact:<ob>:-` (`rec.exact_activity.v1` over exactly the admitted deltas, multiplicity preserved, its raw sum bound); the posted node names the companion in `params.exact_node`, and trace validation checks EACH stored field against ITS OWN encoding of the replayed exact — `companion.value` = Q18(A_replayed) and the posted node's `rounding_residue` = Q18(A_replayed − P) — where A_replayed is re-derived by replay through the companion's own formula chain (`rec.exact_activity.v1` over the `rec.exact_difference.v1` deltas over the bound endpoint operands), never read from a raw params copy; the two checks are independent and neither is derived from the other (D-98 134 amendment 1, Codex production-20260921-1422: signed half-up at 18 places does not commute with translation by P when A and A − P sit on opposite-signed ties — A = 1.0049999999999999995 encodes 1.005 while A − P = −0.0050000000000000005 encodes −0.005000000000000001, so `value + rounding_residue` is NOT required to equal the companion; no tolerance, no rounding change, and such an admitted input is never marked exact-unavailable) (an empty admitted activity has the defined exact sum 0). BOTH endpoints of EVERY admitted event are examined: an endpoint whose target is adjusted (a manual adjustment, a hold's frozen level, the S02-R-04 netting — targets defined in posted units) or whose exact chain is not authored (a returns target, a ROYALTY component) makes the exact activity UNAVAILABLE — the posted node keeps `rounding_residue` 0, names the basis in `params.exact_basis` (`unavailable: <reason> <event key> <side>`) and emits no companion; the segment exact is never substituted for an adjusted target, the posted value is never read as exact, and older traces without the companion are unavailable. Posted values, journals, quantities, event membership, the legacy 1e-4 comparison tolerance and CV-35 are unchanged; Current Rev Rec only |

---

## 1. Stage 01: ingest and canonicalisation

### 1.1 Scope

Stage 01 has two parts. Part A is the platform contract that turns raw inputs into contract events: source identity and version dedupe, and the mapping of legacy template v1 rows (05 STG-01, §5.1, §5.5). It is pure code in `erev_api/domain/imports/legacy_v1/` (`setup.py`, `progress.py`, `modification.py`, `sku_ssp.py`) and `erev_api/domain/integrations/normalise.py`; it is specified here because it decides what the engine receives. Part B is the engine package `erev_engine/stages/s01_canonicalize/` (`convert.py`, `voids.py`, `ledger.py`, `pins.py`, `indexes.py`), which turns the `InputBundle` into the book-independent `CanonicalBundle`.

### 1.2 Inputs and outputs

| Part | Inputs | Outputs |
|---|---|---|
| A | Files (legacy v1 XLSX, CSV v2), API commands, adapter objects; the tenant's committed state for cross-file validation | `source_record` rows; normalised `source_*` rows; events with idempotency keys; modifications and estimate versions awaiting approval; import findings (04 table 15.4-A) |
| B | `InputBundle` (§0.4) | `CanonicalBundle` (§0.11); findings of Table 0.8-A for stage 01; ledger trace nodes |

### 1.3 Part A: source identity, dedupe and event identity

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S01-R-01 | **Source identity.** Every inbound object is a `source_record` identified by (`source_system`, `object_type`, `external_id`, `external_version`) (T-SRC-01). File rows use `external_id = '<import_upload id>:<sheet name>:<row number>'` and `external_version = '1'`. An identity already stored is logged and skipped (`ux_source_record__identity`; REQ-DAT-011) |
| S01-R-02 | **Version order.** Within (`source_system`, `object_type`, `external_id`) objects are processed by `version_order`, never by arrival; a version lower than the highest processed one is stored and skipped with `STALE_SOURCE_VERSION` (`WARNING`; 05 ADP-02). `payload_sha256` is taken over the canonical tokenised payload (ADP-04) |
| S01-R-03 | **Event idempotency keys.** Adapter events: `'src:' ‖ hex(SHA-256(source_system ‖ ':' ‖ object_type ‖ ':' ‖ external_id ‖ ':' ‖ external_version ‖ ':' ‖ ordinal))` (ADP-03). Import events: `'imp:' ‖ hex(SHA-256(file_sha256 ‖ ':' ‖ template_code ‖ ':' ‖ template_version ‖ ':' ‖ business_key ‖ ':' ‖ ordinal))`, where `business_key` is the aggregated key of S01-R-07 (`Contract 1 / POB #1 / Hardware 1`) and `ordinal` is the 1-based position of the event among the events emitted for that key (S01-R-08 order). API events: the request `Idempotency-Key` ‖ `':'` ‖ ordinal. A replay of the same source version or file creates no event (`ux_contract_event__idempotency`); a byte-identical file is refused before validation with `IMPORT_FILE_DUPLICATE` (05 IPL-01; DEV-011) |
| S01-R-04 | **Record time.** `recorded_at` and `record_seq` are assigned by the database at append (DB-08); within one import commit, `record_seq` follows S01-R-08 order |

### 1.4 Part A: legacy template v1 to events

Headers are the exact sets of 04 T-IMP-01 (header check IPL-03). Numbers enter through their shortest decimal string (REQ-DAT-007).

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S01-R-05 | **SKU SSP (`legacy_sku_ssp`).** Products are created when absent (`name = code`). One `ssp_book_version` per distinct `SSP Version` label in book `LEGACY-SKU-SSP` (`resolution_mode = BY_LABEL`), status APPROVED after the import approval. Each row → `ssp_entry` with `method = legacy_range`, `unit_list_price` L, `midpoint_discount_ratio` d, `range_ratio` r, `distinctness` (`Distinct` → `distinct`, `Nondistinct` → `nondistinct`), `revenue_account_code`, and one `NONE` band with `mid_value = L × (1 − d)`, `low_value = mid × (1 − r)`, `high_value = mid × (1 + r)` at full precision (T-REF-31; LM-SSP-01 to 09) |
| S01-R-06 | **Contract setup (`legacy_contract_setup`).** Rows group by `Contract Unique Name` across the whole file (never per batch; DEV-032, POL-214). Per contract: one `source_order`; `inception_date` = minimum `Current Period`; one `CONTRACT_BOOKED` event effective on that date with header `contracting_entity_code` = `Selling Entity` of the first row in worksheet order and `transaction_currency` = tenant reporting currency; each row → a booking line: `obligation_key` = `POB Unique ID`, `product_code` = `SKU Name`, `stratification`, `quantity`, `total_price`, `start_date`, `end_date`, `performing_entity_code` = `Selling Entity`, `ssp_version_label`, `account_overrides` {`CONTRACT_LIABILITY`: Deferred Revenue Account; `CONTRACT_ASSET` and `UNBILLED_RECEIVABLE`: Unbilled A/R Account}, memos (LM-TPL-SETUP). A row with stratification `VC` becomes a line of `obligation_kind = VC_LINE` plus a contract-level VC estimate element `<contract>/VC-<obligation_key>` (`ENTERED_AMOUNT`, version 1 constrained amount = `total_price`, effective on the inception date, `allocation_target = CONTRACT`) (POL-213; REQ-TP-016). A contract already activated is rejected (`SETUP_CONTRACT_EXISTS`); a draft contract receives the new lines, and allocation runs at activation over all lines (DEV-032) |
| S01-R-07 | **Progress aggregation (`legacy_progress_tracking`).** Rows sum by key (`Contract Unique Name`, `POB Unique ID`, `SKU Name`) before validation, whatever their memos (REQ-REC-025; DEV-012). The version memo is the last non-blank value in worksheet order. Each blank `Memo 1`, `Memo 2` or `Memo 3` cell raises `PROGRESS_MEMO_BLANK` (`WARNING`) on its worksheet row, and the row is processed (D-30a; DEV-010) |
| S01-R-08 | **Progress events.** For each aggregated key in ascending business-key order: net delivery q > 0 → `DELIVERY_RECORDED` (`quantity` q, `trigger` `DELIVERY`), q < 0 → `RETURN_RECORDED` (`quantity` \|q\|); net billing b > 0 → `BILLING_RECORDED` (`amount` b, `invoice_number` and `line_external_id` = `'<import_upload id>:<contract>:<POB>'`, synthetic `source_invoice`), b < 0 → `CREDIT_MEMO_RECORDED` (`amount` \|b\|, same synthetic number); pre-standard amount p ≠ 0 → `PRE_STANDARD_REVENUE_RECORDED` (`amount` p, signed); a non-blank memo → `MEMO_UPDATED`. Zero emits nothing. Every event takes the upload parameter `effective_date` (LM-TPL-PROG; D-22; 04 §16.3). Validation: `RETURN_EXCEEDS_DELIVERED`, `REFUND_EXCEEDS_BILLED` against cumulative totals; `PROGRESS_OVER_DELIVERY`, `PROGRESS_OVER_BILLING` against remaining quantity and billing plan (DEV-020, DEV-021) |
| S01-R-09 | **Modification (`legacy_contract_modification`).** One `modification` per contract with `template_mode` = upload `mode`, `effective_date` = upload parameter and `kind` = `VC_CHANGE` for `pob_price_change`, `QUANTITY_CHANGE` or `ADD_OBLIGATION` otherwise. Each row → `lines[]` {`obligation_key`, `product_code`, `start_date` = Mod Start Date, `end_date` = Mod End Date, `stratification`, `consideration_delta` = Mod Billing, `quantity_delta` = Mod Qty, `selling_entity_code`, `account_codes`, `ssp_version_label`, memos} (LM-TPL-MOD). `action = ADD` when the key is unknown, Mod Qty > 0, Mod Billing ≥ 0, every attribute is present and the SSP key resolves (DEV-018); otherwise `CHANGE`, and an unknown key is `POB_NOT_FOUND`. Every obligation of the contract is in scope, including untouched ones (DEV-053). Under POL-100 `USER_SELECTED_TEMPLATE` the chosen treatment of every obligation is `LEGACY_PROSPECTIVE`, `LEGACY_RETROSPECTIVE` or `LEGACY_POB_VC` by `template_mode` (ALG-04 §2.5.7); under `ENGINE_PROPOSES_PREPARER_CONFIRMS` stage 06 proposes. After approval the platform appends `CONTRACT_AMENDED` with `treatments`, `lines` and `ssp_basis` copied from the modification |
| S01-R-10 | **Adapters.** Salesforce activated orders → `CONTRACT_BOOKED`; order amendments → `DRAFT` modifications (ADP-05); Stripe invoices and credit notes → `BILLING_RECORDED` and `CREDIT_MEMO_RECORDED` with service periods; usage records → `USAGE_REPORTED`; payments → `PAYMENT_RECEIVED` (ADP-16, ADP-17) |

### 1.5 Part B: engine canonicalisation

```python
def run(bundle: InputBundle, tb: TraceBuilder) -> CanonicalBundle:
    findings: list[Finding] = []
    b = convert.decimals_to_fractions(bundle)              # S01-R-11; every Decimal via money.to_fraction
    guards.assert_sorted(b)                                # §0.4 keys; ValueError on violation (CV-45)
    for ev in b.events:
        if ev.recorded_at > b.known_at:
            raise ValueError("event after known_at")       # bundle assembly filters (RCP-15)
    events = voids.resolve(b.events)                       # S01-R-12, S01-R-13
    for ev in events:                                      # S01-R-15
        if ev.effective_date < contract_inception(b, ev.contract_key):
            findings.append(Finding("EVENT_BEFORE_INCEPTION", "ERROR", ev.contract_key,
                                    {"event_key": ev.event_key}, stage=1, event_key=ev.event_key))
    boundary = tuple(ev for ev in events if is_boundary(ev, b))            # Table 0.3-A
    measure = tuple(ev for ev in events if ev not in boundary and ev.event_type != "CONTRACT_BOOKED")
    ledger, lf = ledger_mod.build(measure, booked_quantities(b))           # S01-R-16, S01-R-17
    pins, pf = pins_mod.build(events, b.estimate_versions)                 # S01-R-18
    findings += lf + pf
    for subject in ledger.subjects():                                      # S01-R-19
        pt = ledger.at(subject)
        tb.node(measure="delivered_quantity_cum", subject_key=subject, period_key=None,
                value=pt.delivered_cum - pt.returned_cum, currency=None, minor_unit=None,
                formula_id="ingest.ledger_sum.v1", inputs=ledger.sources(subject, "DELIVERY"),
                narrative_key="ingest.ledger_sum")
    return CanonicalBundle(bundle=bundle, events=events, boundary_events=boundary, measure_events=measure,
                           ledger=ledger, estimates=pins, findings=tuple(sorted(findings, key=cv43)), ...)
```

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S01-R-11 | **Conversion.** Every `Decimal` field becomes a `Fraction` through `money.to_fraction`; strings with exponents or non-finite values raise `ValueError` (DG-KRN-MONEY-05). Literals are checked against `erev_engine.enums` |
| S01-R-12 | **Voids.** `EVENT_VOIDED` names its target by `supersedes_event_key`. The target and the void are both removed from `events`; the void never becomes a measure or boundary event. A void of a void, or of an event of another contract, is a programming error (CV-45). [J] Removing both makes the replay equal to a stream in which the voided event never existed; stage 14 posts the difference as target minus posted in the first open period (RCP-06, ALG-09) |
| S01-R-13 | **Contract void.** `CONTRACT_VOIDED` sets the member's `status_in_book` to `VOIDED` in every book (stage 02) and removes every other event of that contract from the fold; the contract's lines take no part in allocation from inception. Every target of the contract is therefore 0, and stage 14 reverses everything posted for it (REQ-CON-015) |
| S01-R-14 | **Classification.** Boundary events are the E-03 types of Table 0.3-A except the first `CONTRACT_BOOKED` of each member (CV-10). `MATERIAL_RIGHT_EXERCISED` is a boundary under both POL-028 options, because both restructure obligations (§6.3). All other included events are measure events |
| S01-R-15 | **Inception check.** An event effective before its contract's `inception_date` raises `EVENT_BEFORE_INCEPTION` (`ERROR`; DEV-024) |
| S01-R-15a | **Billing identity present (D-97 (28)).** A `BILLING_RECORDED` whose payload lacks `invoice_number` or `line_external_id` (both required, 04 §16.3) raises `BILLING_IDENTITY_MISSING` (`ERROR`, CV-15; detail `missing`): the S10-R-07 identity is bare and no consumer can classify the line against its updates. One finding per such event (CV-42). The API schema requires both members; the finding is the engine's backstop for imported streams |
| S01-R-16 | **Ledger.** For each obligation subject key, measure events in ENG-06 order update `LedgerPoint`: `DELIVERY_RECORDED` adds `quantity` to `delivered_cum`; `RETURN_RECORDED` adds to `returned_cum`; `COST_INCURRED` with `purpose = PROGRESS_INPUT` adds `amount` to `costs_cum`; `PROGRESS_RECORDED` sets `output_ratio` and adds `hours_to_date` deltas to `hours_cum`; `MILESTONE_ACHIEVED` sets `milestone_weight_cum`; `USAGE_REPORTED` adds `quantity` to `usage_quantity_cum`; `BILLING_RECORDED` adds `amount` to `billed_cum`; `CREDIT_MEMO_RECORDED` adds to `credited_cum`; `PAYMENT_RECEIVED` adds to `paid_cum` at contract level. Unreferenced billing, credits and payments accrue on the contract subject key. After each update: `returned_cum > delivered_cum` raises `RETURN_EXCEEDS_DELIVERED`; `credited_cum > billed_cum` raises `REFUND_EXCEEDS_BILLED` (`ERROR`; REQ-TP-009; DEV-021). [J] The engine re-checks because a void or a late event can invalidate a stream the API accepted |
| S01-R-17 | **Over-delivery.** For an obligation with no quantity-changing boundary, `delivered_cum − returned_cum` above the booked quantity raises `PROGRESS_OVER_DELIVERY` (`ERROR`; REQ-REC-024). Obligations changed by a modification are checked by stage 06 (S06-R-08) |
| S01-R-18 | **Estimate pins.** Every `ESTIMATE_CHANGED` event must name an `APPROVED` version present in `estimate_versions`, otherwise `ESTIMATE_VERSION_NOT_APPROVED` (`ERROR`). `pin(estimate_key, at, before)` returns the version with the greatest `effective_date ≤ at`, ties by greater `version_no`, among versions whose `ESTIMATE_CHANGED` event precedes `before` in ENG-06 order (all included events when `before` is None). Portfolio versions apply to every member contract that has its own `ESTIMATE_CHANGED` event (T-CON-13 `applied_event_ids`; 05 §3.6.8). **In force at inception (rev 1.145; item ENG-INCEPTION-ESTIMATE-1).** A version whose `ESTIMATE_CHANGED` event is effective on or before the group inception is in force at inception: `pin` returns it whatever `before` is. Its event is no boundary (S04-R-01, S08-R-01), so a boundary event of the inception date recorded before it — the Step 1 assessment the product records before the activation, a significant-change flag, a line attribute change, an amendment — does not keep the version out of the price. The exception of D-92 (1)/(1a) keeps its precedence: a version that a `CONTRACT_AMENDED` of the same date recorded after it takes (S06-R-15, S06-R-17) applies at that amendment and is not in force at inception by this rule. At the inception date the two record orders of an estimate change and an amendment therefore measure differently: recorded before the amendment, the version joins the modification; recorded after it, the inception price holds the version — until item ENG-INCEPTION-AMEND-ORDER-1 (register index 149; candidate AD-77) rules the inception date |
| S01-R-19 | **Ledger trace.** Stage 01 emits the version-state nodes `delivered_quantity_cum` and `returned_quantity_cum` per obligation (formula `ingest.ledger_sum.v1`, inputs the event source references). Stage 13 copies stage 01 nodes into every book's trace; no other stage emits these two measures. `billed_cum` is emitted by stage 10 for every obligation, because stage 10 attributes unreferenced billing to obligations per document (ENGINE_SPEC_B S10-R-01, S10-R-03; OQ-B-17 resolved by D-76) |
| S01-R-20 | **Booking payload is authoritative.** Terms come from the booking payload and approved events, never from the `ContractInput` projection, which serves only header facts absent from payloads (`related_party_group`, `renewal_of_contract_key`) |

### 1.6 Invariants

| Id | Invariant |
|---|---|
| S01-INV-01 | `events` is strictly increasing in (effective_date, record_seq, event_key) and contains no `EVENT_VOIDED` event and no voided target |
| S01-INV-02 | `boundary_events` ∪ `measure_events` ∪ {first `CONTRACT_BOOKED` per member} = `events`, disjointly |
| S01-INV-03 | Every `LedgerPoint` has `delivered_cum ≥ returned_cum ≥ 0` and `billed_cum ≥ credited_cum ≥ 0` unless a blocking finding was raised |
| S01-INV-04 | `pin` is a function of (estimate_key, at, before) and the bundle only |

### 1.7 Findings, trace and references

| Item | Content |
|---|---|
| Findings | `EVENT_BEFORE_INCEPTION`, `BILLING_IDENTITY_MISSING` (rev 1.15), `RETURN_EXCEEDS_DELIVERED`, `REFUND_EXCEEDS_BILLED`, `PROGRESS_OVER_DELIVERY`, `ESTIMATE_VERSION_NOT_APPROVED` (engine); Part A raises the import codes of 04 table 15.4-A and 15.4-B |
| Trace formula ids | `ingest.ledger_sum.v1` |
| POL | POL-043 (import semantics `DELTA` → full versions v(n) = v(n−1) + delta at commit), POL-078, POL-211, POL-212, POL-213, POL-214 |
| ALG, JET, CHK | ALG-06 §2.7.4 (negative rows); ALG-09 (voids and late events post as deltas); CHK-061 (golden return mapping) |
| Answer-key families | `ONB`, `PAR`, `RET`, `LATE` |
| DEVIATIONS | DEV-010, DEV-011, DEV-012, DEV-016, DEV-017, DEV-018, DEV-020, DEV-021, DEV-024, DEV-032, DEV-053; probes P1 to P4 |
| Requirements | REQ-DAT-001 to -017, REQ-INT-001, REQ-REC-024, REQ-REC-025, REQ-TP-009, REQ-MOD-019 |

### 1.8 Worked example

EX-01-A [A]: a legacy progress workbook for Contract 1 with upload `effective_date` 2023-01-31.

| Excel row | Contract / POB / SKU | Current Delivery | Current Billing | Pre-ASC606 | Memo 1 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 2 | Contract 1 / POB #1 / Hardware 1 | 1 | 100 | 0 | Jan batch |
| 3 | Contract 1 / POB #1 / Hardware 1 | 1 | 200 | 0 | (blank) |
| 4 | Contract 1 / POB #2 / Software 1 | 1 | 0 | 66 | Go-live |
| 5 | Contract 1 / POB #3 / Consulting 1 | 0.5 | 0 | 88 | Phase 1 |

Memo 2 and Memo 3 hold values on every row. Result: one finding `PROGRESS_MEMO_BLANK` (`WARNING`) on row 3, column Memo 1; the upload commits. Rows 2 and 3 aggregate to delivery 2 and billing 300 with memo "Jan batch". Nine events in this order: POB #1 `DELIVERY_RECORDED` 2, `BILLING_RECORDED` 300.00, `MEMO_UPDATED`; POB #2 `DELIVERY_RECORDED` 1, `PRE_STANDARD_REVENUE_RECORDED` 66.00, `MEMO_UPDATED`; POB #3 `DELIVERY_RECORDED` 0.5, `PRE_STANDARD_REVENUE_RECORDED` 88.00, `MEMO_UPDATED`. The first event's idempotency key is `imp:` followed by 64 hexadecimal characters of SHA-256(file hash ‖ `:legacy_progress_tracking:1:Contract 1 / POB #1 / Hardware 1:1`). The ledger after these events: POB #1 `delivered_cum` 2 and `billed_cum` 300; POB #2 1; POB #3 1/2. The recognised figures follow in EX-08-A (128.84 / 118.53 / 48.32; CHK-022).

---

## 2. Stage 02: contract identification

### 2.1 Scope, inputs and outputs

Stage 02 applies the Step 1 gate per book (606-10-25-1 to 25-8), fixes the enforceable term (25-3, 25-4), measures deposit accounting while a contract fails Step 1 (EMOD-12), and fixes the accounting unit (combination group, 25-9). Package `erev_engine/stages/s02_contract_identification/` (`status.py`, `term.py`, `deposits.py`, `group.py`). Combination suggestions and ingestion grouping are platform functions in `erev_api/domain/contracts/combination.py` and `erev_api/domain/integrations/grouping.py` (§2.4).

| Direction | Content |
|---|---|
| Inputs | `CanonicalBundle`: member contracts and booking payloads; boundary events `CONTRACT_ACTIVATED` (read as a gate), `COLLECTIBILITY_ASSESSED`, `CONTRACT_CRITERIA_MET`, `SIGNIFICANT_CHANGE_FLAGGED`, `CONTRACT_VOIDED`; measure events `PAYMENT_RECEIVED`, `BILLING_RECORDED`, `DELIVERY_RECORDED`, `CONTRACT_TERMINATED` payloads; judgements `NOT_A_CONTRACT`, `CONTRACT_TERM`; policies of Table 0.10-A row 02 |
| Outputs | `IdentifiedState`: per member and book a status timeline `tuple[StatusSegment, ...]`; `EnforceableTerm` per contract; transition records; deposit targets (`SpecialistTargets.deposit`); findings |

```python
class StatusSegment:
    contract_key: str; book_code: str; from_date: date; from_event_key: str | None
    status: str                                 # E-17: DRAFT | NOT_A_CONTRACT | ACTIVE | TERMINATED | VOIDED (COMPLETED: stage 09)
    reason: str                                 # BOOKED | ACTIVATED | NOT_PROBABLE | NO_COMMERCIAL_SUBSTANCE |
                                                # MUTUAL_TERMINATION_UNPERFORMED | CRITERIA_MET | EVENT_25_7_A |
                                                # EVENT_25_7_B | EVENT_25_7_C | TERMINATED | VOIDED; the reason of the latest segment is
                                                # persisted as T-CON-08 status_reason_in_book (04 rev 1.2; OQ-A-24 resolved by D-76)
    transition: str | None                      # CATCH_UP_AT_TRANSITION | PROSPECTIVE_FROM_TRANSITION (POL-013) on CRITERIA_MET

class EnforceableTerm:
    contract_key: str; end_date: date | None; basis: str   # STATED_TERM | JUDGEMENT | STATED_TERM_PENDING_JUDGEMENT
    truncated_lines: tuple[tuple[str, date, Fraction | None], ...]  # (obligation_key, end_date, enforceable consideration)
```

### 2.2 Pseudocode

```python
def run(ctx: BookContext, cb: CanonicalBundle, tb: TraceBuilder) -> IdentifiedState:
    findings = []
    group.assert_single_currency(cb)                                   # S02-R-10
    timelines = {}
    for c in sorted(cb.group.member_contract_keys):
        segs = [StatusSegment(c, ctx.book_code, booking_date(cb, c), None, "DRAFT", "BOOKED", None)]
        for ev in events_of(cb, c):                                    # ENG-06 order, all included events of c
            prev = segs[-1].status
            nxt = status.transition(ctx, cb, c, prev, ev, findings)    # Table 2.2-A; None = no change
            if nxt is not None:
                segs.append(nxt)
                tb.node(measure=f"status@{ev.event_key}", subject_key=c, period_key=None, value=STATUS_CODE[nxt.status],
                        currency=None, minor_unit=None, formula_id="step1.status.v1",
                        inputs=[ev.source], params={"status": nxt.status, "reason": nxt.reason},
                        narrative_key="step1.status")
        timelines[c] = tuple(segs)
    terms = {c: term.enforceable(ctx, cb, c, tb) for c in sorted(cb.group.member_contract_keys)}   # S02-R-06
    deposit_targets = deposits.targets(ctx, cb, timelines, tb)         # S02-R-08
    return IdentifiedState(timelines=timelines, terms=terms, deposit_targets=deposit_targets,
                           findings=tuple(findings), ...)
```

Table 2.2-A: status transitions per book (evaluated per event in ENG-06 order)

| From | Event and condition | To | Reason |
|---|---|---|---|
| any | `CONTRACT_VOIDED` | `VOIDED` | `VOIDED` (S01-R-13) |
| `DRAFT` | `CONTRACT_ACTIVATED`; latest `COLLECTIBILITY_ASSESSED` for this book at or before the event has `is_probable = false` | `NOT_A_CONTRACT` | `NOT_PROBABLE` (25-1(e); POL-011) |
| `DRAFT` | `CONTRACT_ACTIVATED`; `has_commercial_substance = false` | `NOT_A_CONTRACT` | `NO_COMMERCIAL_SUBSTANCE` (25-1(d)) |
| `DRAFT` | `CONTRACT_ACTIVATED`; termination party `BOTH`, no substantive penalty (S02-R-06), `termination_notice_days` null or 0, and the ledger shows no delivery, billing or payment before the event | `NOT_A_CONTRACT` | `MUTUAL_TERMINATION_UNPERFORMED` (25-4) |
| `DRAFT` | `CONTRACT_ACTIVATED` otherwise | `ACTIVE` | `ACTIVATED` |
| `NOT_A_CONTRACT` (reason `MUTUAL_TERMINATION_UNPERFORMED`) | first `DELIVERY_RECORDED`, `BILLING_RECORDED` or `PAYMENT_RECEIVED` | `ACTIVE` | `CRITERIA_MET`, transition per POL-013 |
| `NOT_A_CONTRACT` | `CONTRACT_CRITERIA_MET` for this book | `ACTIVE` | `CRITERIA_MET`, transition per POL-013 |
| `ACTIVE` | `COLLECTIBILITY_ASSESSED` with `is_probable = false` for this book, preceded by `SIGNIFICANT_CHANGE_FLAGGED` since the last assessment | `NOT_A_CONTRACT` | `NOT_PROBABLE` (25-5, 25-6: prospective only) |
| `NOT_A_CONTRACT` | 25-7 event of S02-R-07 | `TERMINATED` for (b); unchanged for (a) and (c) | `EVENT_25_7_A`, `EVENT_25_7_B`, `EVENT_25_7_C` (recorded on the deposit targets) |
| `ACTIVE` | `CONTRACT_TERMINATED` with `termination_kind = FULL` | `TERMINATED` | `TERMINATED` (applied by stage 06, S06-R-24) |

### 2.3 Rules

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S02-R-01 | **Per book.** The gate runs per book; the ASC606 and IFRS15 books can differ (research 04 §13 #1, #2). `COLLECTIBILITY_ASSESSED` and `CONTRACT_CRITERIA_MET` apply only to the book named in their payload. The engine never derives collectibility from a percentage; the payload boolean and its reviewed judgement record decide (POL-011; U-01) |
| S02-R-02 | **Activation is a gate.** `DRAFT` computations are provisional: stage 14 emits no posting intent for a contract whose status is `DRAFT` (API-S-ContractCreate). After `CONTRACT_ACTIVATED`, targets from inception apply and post through RCP-04 |
| S02-R-03 | **Revenue guard (V4).** Revenue targets of stage 09 are 0 for every interval in which the status is `DRAFT`, `NOT_A_CONTRACT` or `VOIDED`, except the 25-7 recognition of S02-R-07. A violation is a programming error (05 §3.6.7) |
| S02-R-04 | **Criteria met.** On `ACTIVE` with reason `CRITERIA_MET`: POL-013 `CATCH_UP_AT_TRANSITION` → the obligations keep their inception segments (basis `INCEPTION`), so revenue for performance to date appears in the transition period with `reason_code = 'STEP1_MET'`, measured per obligation as max(C_p(d) − R25_p, 0) where R25_p is the obligation's attributed S02-R-07 revenue through the transition (ENGINE_SPEC_B S14-R-25), so cumulative revenue never exceeds max(transaction price, revenue already recognised under S02-R-07) (606-10-32-1; D-91; rev pending ENA-2b); the deposit balance transferred is the balance after those recognitions (JET-01b; CHK-021). `PROSPECTIVE_FROM_TRANSITION` → stage 05 opens each obligation's first segment at the transition date with basis `PROSPECTIVE`, `base_revenue_exact = 0` and progress since the transition over the remaining totals (CV-62). The deposit balance at the transition transfers to the contract liability (S02-R-08) |
| S02-R-05 | **Prospective failure.** `ACTIVE` → `NOT_A_CONTRACT` after a significant change keeps revenue recognised through the event date (the stage 09 targets are frozen at C(d)); later consideration received posts to the deposit liability; receivables already recognised are outside revenue (credit losses, POL-125; 606-10-55-106 to 55-109) |
| S02-R-06 | **Enforceable term (POL-014).** `STATED_TERM` → basis `STATED_TERM`. `TO_EARLIEST_TERMINATION_WITHOUT_SUBSTANTIVE_PENALTY` → when `termination_party ∈ {CUSTOMER, BOTH}`: substantive = the reviewed `CONTRACT_TERM` outcome `termination_penalty_substantive`, else `termination_has_penalty`. If not substantive: with a reviewed `CONTRACT_TERM` outcome `enforceable_end_date`, basis `JUDGEMENT` and every line whose `end_date` is later is truncated to that date, with total price replaced by the outcome member `enforceable_consideration[obligation_key]` when present; without it, basis `STATED_TERM_PENDING_JUDGEMENT` and nothing is truncated (the platform activation checklist requires the judgement, REQ-CON-005, REQ-CON-013). Periods beyond the enforceable term are customer options, recorded by the preparer as material-right lines when they give a material right (POL-026) |
| S02-R-07 | **25-7 events while `NOT_A_CONTRACT`.** Evaluated on every event of the contract and, for (a) and (c), at the dated points below through the horizon. (a) Every obligation fully transferred — ledger totals at the booked quantities, output ratio 1 or milestone weight 1, or, for an obligation whose stage 03 classification is `OVER_TIME` `TIME_ELAPSED` with a resolved term, its term end date reached (the S02-R-06 truncated end where truncated; the transfer date is the term end date whatever the ALG-11 convention, D-79, D-91); an obligation with an unresolved term (`CONTROL_TRANSFER` or `FIRST_USAGE` start not observed) is never transferred by time; every obligation of the contract must qualify — cumulative receipts ≥ the stated consideration of the lines (the S02-R-06 enforceable consideration where the term is truncated), and outcome `consideration_nonrefundable = true` → recognise the deposit balance. Every recognition under (a), (b) or (c) is capped at the stated consideration less revenue already recognised under this rule; a receipt above it stays in the deposit liability as a customer credit. The time test is evaluated at T = max(term end) as a dated point ordered after every event of T (node `deposit_to_revenue@<ISO date>`), and the recognition is dated the first date the conditions hold: T when receipts are complete, else the completing receipt's event; a receipt dated T completes the receipts before the dated point, so the recognition is at T as a `TIME` amount, and the completing-receipt branch applies to a receipt after T only (D-94 (1); rev 1.9). A stage 09 hold at T does not defer the evaluation. (b) `CONTRACT_TERMINATED` and `consideration_nonrefundable = true` → recognise the deposit balance net of `refund_amount`; status `TERMINATED`. (c) Only when POL-012 is `ENABLED` in the book: outcome `event_c_met_on` ≤ t and `consideration_nonrefundable = true` → recognise the deposit balance at that date and every later receipt on its receipt date. The amount recognised is Dr `DEPOSIT_LIABILITY` / Cr `REVENUE` (JET-01b), attributed to the contract's in-scope obligations by ENGINE_SPEC_B S14-R-25 and posted as `EVENT` at an event or as a `CLOSE_RELEASE` `TIME` amount at a dated point (Table 14-A) [F, 606-10-25-7, 25-8; IFRS 15.15 lists (a) and (b) only]. 1.0-rc: the ledger test only, (a) evaluated on events only, and a non-zero `deposit_to_revenue_cum` fails closed (D-91; rev pending ENA-2b, END-4b) |
| S02-R-08 | **Deposit targets (EMOD-12).** Per (contract, contracting entity, book) and date t, with money cumulative values rounded by CV-35: `deposit_received_cum(t)` = Σ `PAYMENT_RECEIVED.amount` of events effective ≤ t while the status is `NOT_A_CONTRACT`; `deposit_refunded_cum(t)` = Σ `CONTRACT_TERMINATED.refund_amount` effective ≤ t while `NOT_A_CONTRACT`; `deposit_to_revenue_cum(t)` per S02-R-07; `deposit_to_revenue_time_cum(t)` its portion recognised at dated points without an event (the `CLOSE_RELEASE` share, D-91; rev pending ENA-2b); `deposit_to_contract_liability_cum(t)` = the deposit balance at each `CRITERIA_MET` transition effective ≤ t; `deposit_liability(t)` = received − refunded − to revenue − to contract liability, never below 0 (S02-INV-02). The deposit liability is excluded from the net position (POL-128). Rev 1.2 (D-25b): in a currency other than the contracting entity's functional currency it is a monetary item. Stage 12 creates its layer at the receipt-date spot rate, remeasures it to the closing rate at every period end, and remeasures the settled portion to spot at `CRITERIA_MET`, at a refund on termination and at 25-7 derecognition (JET-10d; POL-164 `ASSET_POSITIONS_ENGINE_AR_AND_MONETARY_LIABILITIES`; ENGINE_SPEC_B S12-R-11, S12-R-20; CHK-084 (b)). The targets of this rule stay in transaction currency |
| S02-R-09 | **Accounting unit.** The unit of Steps 2 to 5 is the combination group at `known_at` (T-CON-03; 606-10-25-9). Every member's booking lines enter the inception fold (CV-10). A change of membership reruns the group from its inception and posts differences as deltas (REQ-CON-009; D-19). A member leaving a group is computed in its own singleton group from its own inception |
| S02-R-10 | **Single currency.** Every member has the group's transaction currency; otherwise programming error (REQ-CON-019; D-75 03 Q3) |
| S02-R-11 | **Invoices before criteria met.** `BILLING_RECORDED` while `NOT_A_CONTRACT` raises `INVOICE_ON_NOT_A_CONTRACT` (`WARNING`; once per (contract, S10-R-07 identity), so a status update or repeated line of the same identity adds no second finding — D-97 (29), rev 1.15) and is excluded from `billed_cum` in the book until the transition; at `CRITERIA_MET` every such invoice enters `billed_cum` dated the transition date. [J] Under POL-004 `ERP` the ERP has posted the invoice to the contract liability, and POL-123 requires the subledger position to tie once the contract exists |
| S02-R-12 | **Portfolio approach.** POL-015 changes no Step 1 outcome; it only permits portfolio-scope estimate versions (§1.5 S01-R-18; PT-06) |

### 2.4 Platform functions (grouping and suggestions)

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S02-R-13 | **Ingestion grouping (REQ-CON-011).** Tenant setting `integration.grouping_fields` lists up to five source fields. The grouping key of a normalised order is the canonical JSON array of their values in setting order. An order whose key equals the key recorded on an existing contract becomes a candidate modification of that contract (`DRAFT`, ADP-05); otherwise a new contract with `external_id` = the source order number |
| S02-R-14 | **Combination suggestions (POL-016; REQ-CON-010).** For a booked contract c, suggest every other contract d of the same customer or related-party group with \|inception(c) − inception(d)\| ≤ `combination.detection_window_days`, or whose external id appears in c's `document_ref` or custom attributes, and that is not in c's group. Suggestions are proposals; a combination is committed only by an approved `COMBINATION_GROUP` command recording the 25-9 criterion. Window 0 disables detection |

### 2.5 Invariants, findings, trace and references

| Item | Content |
|---|---|
| Invariants | S02-INV-01: stage 09 revenue targets are 0 while the status is not `ACTIVE`, `COMPLETED` or `TERMINATED`, except S02-R-07 amounts. S02-INV-02: `deposit_liability(t) ≥ 0` for every t. S02-INV-03: after a `CRITERIA_MET` transition the deposit liability is 0 unless later receipts arrive while `NOT_A_CONTRACT`. S02-INV-04: every member shares the group currency |
| Findings | `INVOICE_ON_NOT_A_CONTRACT` (`WARNING`) |
| Trace nodes | `status@<event key>:<contract>:-` (value = E-17 ordinal, params status and reason); `deposit_liability:<contract>@<entity>:<period>`; `deposit_to_revenue@<event key or ISO date>:<contract>@<entity>:-` (the date form for a dated evaluation, ordered after the date's events; L1-2-Q-7); `deposit_to_revenue_time_cum:<contract>@<entity>:<period>`; `deposit_revenue_share:<obligation>:<period>` and `deposit_revenue_time_share:<obligation>:<period>` (stage 09, ENGINE_SPEC_B S14-R-25; the time share is the dated-point portion stage 14 posts as `TIME`; D-91, D-94 (6)); `deposit_to_contract_liability@<event key>:<contract>@<entity>:-`; `enforceable_end_date:<contract>:-` (value = ordinal day number, params date and basis) |
| Formula ids | `step1.status.v1`, `step1.enforceable_term.v1`, `step1.deposit.v1`, `step1.event_25_7.v1`, `step1.transition_catch_up.v1`, `step1.deposit_revenue_share.v1` (emitted by stage 09; D-91, rev pending END-4b) |
| POL, JET, CHK | POL-011 to POL-016, POL-128, POL-164; JET-01b, JET-10d (stage 12); CHK-021, CHK-084 (b) |
| Answer-key families | `STP1`, `IFRS` (event (c)); AK:S1-EX1-CASEA, AK:S1-EX1-CASEB-C, AK:S1-COMBINATION-OWN; AK-FAM:termination |
| DEVIATIONS | DEV-085 (combination absent in legacy; preset POL-016 = 0) |
| Requirements | REQ-CON-002, -003, -004, -009, -010, -011, -013, -015, -019 |

### 2.6 Worked examples

EX-02-A (CHK-021; research 04 S1-EX1-CASEB-C Case C; `MONTHLY_EVEN`; USD). Three-year service at 20.00 a month (stated consideration 720.00, one obligation). `COLLECTIBILITY_ASSESSED` `is_probable = false` before activation, so activation gives `NOT_A_CONTRACT`. Receipts of 20.00 at the end of months 1 to 6.

| Date | Status | `deposit_liability` | Revenue target | Stage 14 lines (JET-01b, JET-02) |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| End of month 5 | `NOT_A_CONTRACT` | 100.00 | 0.00 | each receipt: Dr `BILLING_CLEARING` (`UNAPPLIED_CASH`) 20.00 / Cr `DEPOSIT_LIABILITY` 20.00 |
| End of month 6, receipt | `NOT_A_CONTRACT` | 120.00 | 0.00 | as above |
| End of month 6, `CONTRACT_CRITERIA_MET` | `ACTIVE`, `CATCH_UP_AT_TRANSITION` | 0.00 (transfer 120.00) | C = `cumulative_posted`(720, 72,000, 6/36) = 120.00 | Dr `DEPOSIT_LIABILITY` 120.00 / Cr `CONTRACT_LIABILITY` 120.00; Dr `CONTRACT_LIABILITY` 120.00 / Cr `REVENUE` 120.00 (`STEP1_MET`) |

Closing: deposit liability 0.00, contract liability 0.00, revenue 120.00.

EX-02-B (research 04 S1-COMBINATION-OWN; own example [A]). Contract C-1 (licence at 90,000.00, SSP 100,000) signed 10 January; contract C-2 (implementation services at 60,000.00, SSP 50,000) signed 20 January; negotiated as a package, combined under 25-9(a). Stage 02 places both bookings in one inception fold; stage 05 allocates TP 150,000.00: licence 100,000.00, services 50,000.00. Invoices 90,000.00 (January), 30,000.00 (February), 30,000.00 (March); services performed evenly in February and March.

| Period end | Revenue cumulative | Billed cumulative | Contract asset |
|---|---|---|---|
| January | 100,000.00 | 90,000.00 | 10,000.00 |
| February | 125,000.00 | 120,000.00 | 5,000.00 |
| March | 150,000.00 | 150,000.00 | 0.00 |

Without combination, January revenue would be 90,000.00.

## 3. Stage 03: POB builder

### 3.1 Scope, inputs and outputs

Stage 03 turns booking lines into performance obligations (606-10-25-14 to 25-22, 55-30 to 55-65B): template decision tables, bundle explosion, distinct, non-distinct and series obligations, $0 obligations, material rights, warranties, principal versus agent, licences and scope routing. Package `erev_engine/stages/s03_pob_builder/` (`lines.py`, `templates.py`, `bundles.py`, `distinct.py`, `options.py`, `warranties.py`, `agent.py`, `licences.py`, `scope.py`). Stage 06 calls the entry `build_lines` for lines added by a modification or an option exercise.

| Direction | Content |
|---|---|
| Inputs | Booking lines of every member (`CONTRACT_BOOKED` payload `lines`, API-S-ContractLine); `ProductInput` and bundle components; `TemplateInput` versions; `RuleSetInput` of kind `POB_ASSIGNMENT`; `MaterialRightInput` (T-CON-14); judgements of Table 0.4-A (`POB_DISTINCT_OVERRIDE`, `SERIES_CLASSIFICATION`, `PRINCIPAL_AGENT`, `LICENCE_NATURE`, `WARRANTY_TYPE`, `REPURCHASE_CLASSIFICATION`); the enforceable-term truncations of stage 02; policies of Table 0.10-A row 03 |
| Outputs | `PobState`: `ObligationState` terms for every obligation (without allocation), option records with option SSP, assurance-warranty accrual definitions, agent gross-to-net definitions, routed-out lines, immaterial-promise candidates, time triggers (CV-27), findings |

### 3.2 Pseudocode

```python
def run(ctx: BookContext, st: IdentifiedState, tb: TraceBuilder) -> PobState:
    findings: list[Finding] = []
    drafts = [d for c in members(st) for d in lines.collect(ctx, st, c)]           # S03-R-01, S03-R-04
    drafts = bundles.explode(ctx, st, drafts, tb, findings)                          # S03-R-03
    drafts = [templates.resolve(ctx, st, d, at=d.pricing_date, findings=findings) for d in drafts]  # S03-R-02
    drafts, routed = scope.route(ctx, st, drafts)                                    # S03-R-11, S03-R-12
    drafts = warranties.split(ctx, st, drafts)                                       # S03-R-08
    drafts = options.build(ctx, st, drafts, tb, findings)                            # S03-R-07
    drafts = agent.apply(ctx, st, drafts, tb, findings)                              # S03-R-09
    drafts = licences.apply(ctx, st, drafts)                                         # S03-R-10
    drafts = special.apply(ctx, st, drafts)                                          # S03-R-14 to S03-R-16
    obligations = distinct.group(ctx, st, drafts, findings)                          # S03-R-05, S03-R-06, S03-R-13
    return PobState(obligations=tuple(sorted(obligations, key=lambda o: o.subject_key)),
                    routed_out=routed, time_triggers=options.triggers(obligations), findings=tuple(findings), ...)
```

### 3.3 Rules

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S03-R-01 | **Lines.** Each line of each member's booking payload is a `PobDraft` with subject key `<contract>/<obligation_key>`, product, `stratification`, `quantity` Q, stated price P (`total_price`), `start_date`, `end_date`, performing entity (default: the contracting entity, REQ-ENT-001), `ssp_version_label`, account overrides, scope flag, bundle parent and memos. `pricing_date` = the group inception date for booking lines and the modification effective date for added lines (D-18). Dates follow the template rules: start `LINE_START` = line start; `BOOKING_DATE` = contract inception; `CONTROL_TRANSFER`, `FIRST_USAGE` = unknown until the first matching event (start None); `LICENCE_START_OR_AVAILABLE` per S03-R-10; end `LINE_END` = line end; `START_PLUS_TERM` = start + `term_months` months − 1 day; `NONE` = None. Stage 02 truncations replace end date and stated price (S02-R-06) |
| S03-R-02 | **Template resolution (decision table, most-specific match).** Facts: `product.code`, `product.product_family`, `bundle_parent.code`, `contract.region`, `contract.channel`, `customer.segment`, `contract.contract_type`, `line.term_band`, `contract.currency`, `effective_date` = pricing date (`rules.FIELDS["POB_ASSIGNMENT"]`, T-REF-26). (1) Evaluate the `POB_ASSIGNMENT` rule set version whose effective range contains the pricing date; a rule matches when every condition holds (`eq`, `in`, `range` inclusive lower and exclusive upper, `prefix`, `gte`, `lte`); choose the match with the greatest `specificity`, then greatest `priority`, then ascending `rule_key`; output `pob_template_code`. (2) Otherwise `product.default_template_code`. (3) Otherwise, or when the product is absent, finding `PRODUCT_UNMAPPED` (`ERROR`; CTL-003). The template version is the version effective at the contract's inception for booking lines and at the pricing date for added lines, and is pinned (POLICIES §0.5 rule 3). As built (rev 1.101; supervisor ruling R-113 (b) of 2026-09-30; item CFG-BACKDATE-1): the version is recorded on the obligation version (04 T-CON-11 `pob_template_version_id`) and chosen again by date at every computation — the recorded version is not read back. What keeps a later version from a contract that has posted is the publication rule (PRD ERR-75; 04 §16.5): a version that supersedes a published one is dated after today in every active entity's time zone, and a first version reaches a contract only as the default template of its product, which the product pin holds (dev-guide DG-KRN-REG-02). A contract dated ahead of today takes the version in force on its date when it is next computed. The read-back of the recorded template version is the template half of item PIN-READBACK-1 and is not built. Trace params record `rule_key` and the template `version_key` |
| S03-R-03 | **Bundle explosion (REQ-REF-013).** A line whose product has `is_bundle = true` is replaced by one draft per component valid at the pricing date, in `sequence` order: `obligation_key` = `<bundle line key>.<sequence, two digits>`, product = component, Q = bundle Q × `quantity_per_bundle`, parent = the bundle line. Stated prices split the bundle line price by `largest_remainder(P_minor, weights, component keys)` with weights = the component's point SSP at quantity from `s05_allocation.resolve_ssp(ctx, draft, at=pricing_date, price=None)` (the point value or range midpoint; S05-R-06) for `split_basis = relative_ssp`, or `split_ratio` for `fixed_percentage` (ratios sum to 1, validated at activation). The bundle line itself creates no obligation. The reader takes the version recorded for the component's own pricing as stage 05 does (S05-R-03, rev 1.121) |
| S03-R-04 | **Negative, zero and $0 lines.** A line with Q < 0 or P < 0 is `NEGATIVE_BOOKING_LINE` (`ERROR`; POL-078), except a parity line of kind `VC_LINE` (S01-R-06). Q = 0 is a programming error (the import raises `SETUP_QUANTITY_ZERO`). A distinct line with P = 0 is an obligation and receives an allocation (REQ-POB-002; legacy PAR-01) |
| S03-R-05 | **Distinct and non-distinct (606-10-25-19 to 25-22).** `distinctness` = the reviewed `POB_DISTINCT_OVERRIDE` outcome for the line, else the template version's value. A `distinct` line is its own obligation. A `nondistinct` line with an integration target (outcome `integrates_into_obligation_key`, else `bundle_parent_obligation_key` when the parent is an obligation) merges into the target: the merged obligation keeps the target's key, template and measure; its Q, P and SSP weights are sums over its member lines; start = earliest, end = latest; `member_line_keys` lists the merged keys. A `nondistinct` line with no integration target is its own obligation made of goods and services that are not distinct from each other (25-22), recognised by its measure and treated as class N at modifications (ALG-04). Under POL-211 `SINGLE_POB` (parity) legacy `Nondistinct` lines are never merged |
| S03-R-06 | **Series (606-10-25-14(b), 25-15).** `distinctness = series` (template or reviewed `SERIES_CLASSIFICATION` outcome) requires `series_increment_unit` ∈ {`day`, `month`, `transaction`, `unit`}. Fixed consideration is recognised over the whole series; VC meeting 606-10-32-40 is realised in the increment in which it is earned (component `PERIOD_VC`, ENGINE_SPEC_B S09-R-01); a modification treats the remaining increments as distinct (class D, ALG-04) |
| S03-R-07 | **Material rights (ALG-05 §2.6.1; D-21a; POL-026).** A line of kind `MATERIAL_RIGHT` must have a `MaterialRightInput`. Option SSP at the pricing date: `DISCOUNT_X_LIKELIHOOD` → SSP_opt = `expected_purchase_amount` × `incremental_discount_ratio` × L, with L = `rate` of the `EXERCISE_LIKELIHOOD` pin of `likelihood_estimate_id` at the pricing date; validation 0 ≤ L ≤ 1 and ratio ≥ 0, else `NON_FINITE_AMOUNT`; a ratio of 0 is not a material right (606-10-55-43): no obligation is created and trace node `option_ssp` records 0 with params `reason = NOT_MATERIAL`. `RENEWAL_ALTERNATIVE` → no option obligation; the preparer books the expected renewal lines with their expected consideration (606-10-55-45). `ENTERED_AMOUNT` → SSP_opt = the SSP entry's point (parity: Q dollar-units × list price 1; POL-212 `KEEP_QUANTITY_CONVENTION`), and the obligation is units-delivered. Option obligations with `expiry_date` emit a time trigger (CV-27). POL-020 relief never applies to options (606-10-25-16B). Under POL-029 `EXPECTED_BENEFIT_PERIOD`, an upfront-fee line that accompanies a renewal material right sets the option obligation's end date to start + `amortization_months` of the latest `RENEWAL_EXPECTATION` pin (research 04 §5.8) |
| S03-R-08 | **Warranties (606-10-55-30 to 55-35; POL-022).** `warranty_type` = reviewed `WARRANTY_TYPE` outcome, else template value. `SERVICE`: the line is an obligation of kind `SERVICE_WARRANTY` recognised over its coverage period. `ASSURANCE`: no obligation; when POL-022 is `ENGINE` and the product has `assurance_cost_per_unit` c, stage 04 measures the accrual target round(c × units transferred) on the product obligation (S04-R-19; JET-16). `EXTERNAL`: nothing. A product whose `assurance_cost_per_unit` (T-REF-20 column, 04 rev 1.2; OQ-A-06 resolved by D-76) is NULL under `ENGINE` accrues nothing, and stage 03 emits trace params `reason = NO_COST_RATE` |
| S03-R-09 | **Principal versus agent (606-10-55-36 to 55-40; POL-030).** `principal_agent` = resolved `pob.principal_or_agent` (levels O, P), else the product value. `NOT_ASSESSED` on a line of a contract whose status is not `DRAFT` raises `PRINCIPAL_AGENT_NOT_ASSESSED` (`ERROR`; 04 table 15.4-C, rev 1.2; OQ-A-11 resolved by D-76). `AGENT`: from the reviewed `PRINCIPAL_AGENT` outcome, retained consideration R: `COMMISSION_RATE` ρ → R = `largest_remainder(P_minor, [ρ, 1 − ρ], [retained, supplier])[0]`; `FIXED_FEE` → R = amount; `SUPPLIER_COST` → R = P − amount. The obligation's stated price becomes R; `gross_amount_memo` = P (REQ-POB-009); the supplier share P − R is consideration collected on behalf of another party, excluded from the transaction price (606-10-32-2) and, when billed, posted by JET-02 to `BILLING_CLEARING` with `clearing_purpose = AP_SUPPLIER` |
| S03-R-10 | **Licences (606-10-55-54 to 55-65B; POL-024, POL-025, POL-232).** For an obligation of kind `LICENCE` or a combined obligation containing a licence: ASC606 book (`FUNCTIONAL_SYMBOLIC`) nature = reviewed `LICENCE_NATURE` outcome `nature`, else template `licence_nature`; `SYMBOLIC`, or `FUNCTIONAL` with both `functionality_expected_to_change_substantively = true` and `customer_required_to_use_updated_ip = true` on a reviewed record scoped to `ASC606` or to every book (606-10-55-62; D-91, rev pending ENA-4b: 1.0-rc gives access for `SYMBOLIC` only), → right to access, `OVER_TIME` `TIME_ELAPSED` over the licence period; otherwise (one member, no record, or `activities_significantly_affect_ip` alone, which the ASC606 book never reads) right to use, `POINT_IN_TIME`. IFRS15 book (`ACTIVITIES_SIGNIFICANTLY_AFFECT_IP`) → right to access when outcome `activities_significantly_affect_ip = true`, else right to use. `recognition_start_date` = max(licence period start, availability date), where availability is the first `DELIVERY_RECORDED` of the obligation with trigger `CONTROL_TRANSFER` or `DELIVERY` (55-58C). A renewal licence line (booked by a renewal modification) additionally takes: POL-025 `RENEWAL_PERIOD_START` → max(…, renewal period start = line start); `LATER_OF_AGREEMENT_AND_AVAILABILITY` → max(agreement date = modification effective date, availability). A combined obligation takes the nature of its predominant licence (POL-232) |
| S03-R-11 | **Scope routing (REQ-CON-016; POL-230, POL-231, POL-234).** A line with `scope_flag` ≠ `IN_SCOPE_606`: `LEASE_842` → kept as an allocation target with `routed_out = true` (PT-09: it receives an allocation, which is exported as `LEASE_COMPONENT_ALLOCATION`, and produces no targets or schedules); every other flag → excluded from obligations, and its `out_of_scope_amount` is removed from the transaction price first (S04-R-03). Under POL-231 `ELECTED` with the 842-10-15-42A criteria attested, the combined component is one obligation when the nonlease component predominates, otherwise the whole component is `LEASE_842` |
| S03-R-12 | **Repurchase agreements (POL-233).** The reviewed `REPURCHASE_CLASSIFICATION` outcome `LEASE` sets `scope_flag = LEASE_842` on the obligation; `FINANCING`, `RIGHT_OF_RETURN` and `SALE` are carried on `ObligationState` for stage 09 (ENGINE_SPEC_B S09-R-13) |
| S03-R-13 | **Immaterial promises (POL-020).** Under `APPLY_RELIEF` (ASC606 book only), stage 03 marks non-option lines as candidates; stage 05, after SSP resolution, merges a candidate whose SSP share of the contract total is below `immaterial_threshold_pct` into its bundle parent, else into the obligation of the same contract with the largest resolved SSP (ties by ascending key). Costs of immaterial promises stay in the ERP (POLICIES OQ-15) |
| S03-R-14 | **Shipping (POL-021).** A line of kind `SHIPPING`: `TRUE` (US book) → no obligation; its stated price is added to the stated price of the accompanying product obligation (bundle parent, else the largest SSP of the contract, ties by key), and its SSP weight is 0. `FALSE` (IFRS15 forced) → its own obligation |
| S03-R-15 | **Franchisor expedient (POL-202).** Lines of products mapped to the 952-606-25-2 list: `ELECT_DISTINCT_SERVICES` → each is a distinct obligation; `ELECT_SINGLE_SERVICES_POB` → merged into one services obligation keyed by the first line in key order; `NOT_ELECTED` → S03-R-05 applies |
| S03-R-16 | **Custodial service (POL-094).** Under `CREATE_WHEN_SSP_PROVIDED` a line of kind `CUSTODIAL` with a resolvable SSP entry is an obligation (`TIME_ELAPSED` over the holding period; ENGINE_SPEC_B S09-R-09); under `NEVER`, or without an SSP entry, no obligation |
| S03-R-17 | **Measure and convention.** `recognition_method` = resolved `recognition.measure_of_progress` override (level O) mapped to its E-11 literal, else the template value; one measure per obligation version (REQ-REC-018). `ratable_convention` = resolved `recognition.time_convention` pinned at inception (POL-090, pin K) when the method is `TIME_ELAPSED` |
| S03-R-18 | **Legacy templates.** Under the parity preset the importer assigns the seeded templates by line: stratification `VC` → `LEGACY-VC` (kind `VC_LINE`); a product the migration mapping profile marks as a material right → `LEGACY-MATERIAL-RIGHT` (kind `MATERIAL_RIGHT`, option SSP `ENTERED_AMOUNT`, `is_legacy_quantity_ssp_dollars = true`); SSP entry `distinct` → `LEGACY-DISTINCT`; `nondistinct` → `LEGACY-NONDISTINCT`. All four are `UNITS_DELIVERED` (T-MIG-01; REQ-MIG-006) |

### 3.4 Invariants, findings, trace and references

| Item | Content |
|---|---|
| Invariants | S03-INV-01: every in-scope line belongs to exactly one obligation or is merged by S03-R-05, S03-R-13 or S03-R-14. S03-INV-02: obligation subject keys are unique in the group. S03-INV-03: no stated price is negative except `VC_LINE`. S03-INV-04: every `MATERIAL_RIGHT` obligation has an option record and a non-negative option SSP. S03-INV-05: for an agent obligation 0 ≤ R ≤ P |
| Findings | `PRODUCT_UNMAPPED`, `NEGATIVE_BOOKING_LINE`, `NON_FINITE_AMOUNT`, `PRINCIPAL_AGENT_NOT_ASSESSED` |
| Trace nodes | `stated_price:<ob>:-` (bundle split, agent retained amount, shipping merge); `gross_amount_memo:<ob>:-`; `option_ssp:<ob>:-`; `original_quantity:<ob>:-` |
| Formula ids | `pob.template_match.v1`, `pob.bundle_split.v1`, `pob.option_ssp.v1`, `pob.agent_net.v1`, `pob.merge.v1` |
| POL | POL-020 to POL-022, POL-024 to POL-026, POL-029, POL-030, POL-090, POL-091, POL-094, POL-202, POL-211, POL-212, POL-213, POL-230 to POL-234 |
| ALG, JET, CHK | ALG-05 §2.6.1; JET-02 (agent), JET-08, JET-16; CHK-054, CHK-134 |
| Answer-key families | `POB`, `MR`, `IFRS`, `ONB`, `PAR`; AK:S2-EX11-CASEA-OWNPRICES, AK:S2-EX12A-OWNVOLUMES, AK:S2-EX45-AGENT, AK:S2-EX49, AK:S2-EX51, AK:S2-EX52, AK:S2-EX59, AK:S2-EX61, AK:S2-SHIPPING-OWN, AK:S2-WARRANTY-OWN, AK:S12-FRANCHISOR-OWN |
| DEVIATIONS | DEV-025, DEV-027, DEV-033, DEV-077, DEV-080 |
| Requirements | REQ-POB-001 to -013, REQ-REF-012, -013, REQ-CON-016, REQ-POL-001 |

### 3.5 Worked examples

EX-03-A [A]: bundle `BND-1` (1 unit at 1,000.00) with components HW (SSP 700.00) and SUP (SSP 800.00), `split_basis = relative_ssp`. `largest_remainder(100,000, [700, 800], [BND-1.01, BND-1.02])`: exact quotas 46,666.67 and 53,333.33 minor units; the one remaining minor unit goes to HW (larger fractional remainder). Component stated prices 466.67 and 533.33.

EX-03-B (CHK-054; FASB Example 49): product A sold for 100.00 with a 40% voucher; 10% available without the contract; likelihood 80%; expected purchase 50.00. SSP_opt = 50.00 × (0.40 − 0.10) × 0.80 = 12.00. Stage 05 allocates 100.00 over weights 100 and 12: product 89.29, voucher 10.71 (exact 89.2857… and 10.7142…).

EX-03-C (research 04 S2-EX45): marketplace order of 500.00; reviewed `PRINCIPAL_AGENT` outcome `AGENT`, `COMMISSION_RATE` 0.10. R = 50.00; `gross_amount_memo` 500.00; the supplier share 450.00 is excluded from the transaction price and posts to `BILLING_CLEARING` (`AP_SUPPLIER`) when billed.

EX-03-D (research 04 S2-EX59 Case B): licence of a recording for 20X1 to 20X2, renewed on 31 December 20X1 for 20X3 to 20X4 at 10,000.00 a month; right to use. The renewal line has `recognition_start_date` 1 January 20X3 under POL-025 `RENEWAL_PERIOD_START`, so its revenue before that date is 0.00. With the assumed financing component (0.5% a month; stage 04), revenue at 1 January 20X3 is the present value 225,628.66 and month-1 interest 1,128.14.

EX-03-E (research 04 S2-WARRANTY-OWN; CHK-134): equipment 10,500.00 with a statutory 12-month assurance warranty and an extended warranty for months 13 to 24; SSPs 10,000 and 1,000; assurance cost 200.00. Obligations: equipment (point in time) and `SERVICE_WARRANTY` (months 13 to 24); allocation 9,545.45 and 954.55. Under POL-022 `ENGINE` the accrual target at delivery is 200.00 (JET-16).
