## 4. Stage 04: transaction price

### 4.1 Scope, inputs and outputs

Stage 04 measures the transaction price of the group at inception and at every boundary (606-10-32-2 to 32-27): fixed consideration, variable consideration with the constraint and estimate versions (D-20), expected returns (D-22), implicit price concessions, the significant financing component, noncash consideration, consideration payable to a customer and the sales-tax election. It also produces the specialist targets of EMOD-18, EMOD-21, EMOD-22 and EMOD-23 (ENGINE_SPEC_B §0.1). Package `erev_engine/stages/s04_transaction_price/` (`buildup.py`, `vc.py`, `usage.py`, `returns.py`, `concessions.py`, `financing.py`, `noncash.py`, `customer_consideration.py`, `taxes.py`, `specialist.py`).

| Direction | Content |
|---|---|
| Inputs | `PobState` obligations and stated prices; estimate pins of kinds `VARIABLE_CONSIDERATION`, `IMPLICIT_PRICE_CONCESSION`, `RETURN_RATE`, `EXPECTED_PURCHASES`, `ROYALTY_ACCRUAL`; `USAGE_REPORTED` rated amounts; `BILLING_RECORDED` tax amounts; `PAYMENT_RECEIVED`; `ContractInput.payment_schedule`, `noncash_consideration`, `consideration_payable` (booking and amendment payload members of 04 rev 1.2; OQ-A-07 to OQ-A-09 resolved by D-76); resolved override `sfc.discount_rate_basis`; judgements `SFC_ASSESSMENT`, `OTHER`; `COLLECTIBILITY_ASSESSED.expected_collectible_amount`; policies of Table 0.10-A row 04 |
| Outputs | `PricedState`: `TpBuildUp` at inception; the price function `price_at` used by stages 06 and 08 at every boundary; VC elements with allocation targets for stage 05; `tp_unconstrained` (ENGINE_SPEC_B §11.2.5); `specialist_targets` for financing accretion, warranty accrual, customer incentive asset and payable, noncash asset; findings |

```python
class TpBuildUp:                                # 04 API-S-ContractVersion transaction_price_buildup; exact and posted
    at: date; before_event_key: str | None
    fixed: Quota1; vc_constrained: Quota1; vc_excluded: Quota1          # Quota1 = (exact: Fraction, posted: int)
    expected_returns: Quota1; consideration_payable: Quota1; financing_adjustment: Quota1
    noncash: Quota1; sales_tax_excluded: Quota1; out_of_scope: Quota1
    total: Quota1                               # = fixed + vc_constrained + expected_returns + consideration_payable
                                                #   + financing_adjustment + noncash (memo members excluded)
    allocation_basis: Quota1                    # = total − expected_returns − consideration_payable (S04-R-02)
    elements: tuple[VcElementView, ...]         # estimate_key, pinned version key, amount, allocation target, obligations
```

### 4.2 Pseudocode

```python
def price_at(ctx: BookContext, st: PobState, at: date, before: EventView | None, tb: TraceBuilder) -> TpBuildUp:
    mu = ctx.currencies[ctx.txn_currency].minor_unit
    fixed = sum_minor(ob.stated_price for ob in st.obligations if ob.scope_flag in IN_TP and not ob.is_vc_line)  # S04-R-03
    elements = [vc.measure(ctx, st, e, at, before, tb) for e in vc_elements(st, at, before)]                    # S04-R-04 to R-07
    realised = usage.realised_amounts(ctx, st, at, before)                                                      # S04-R-06
    concession = concessions.implicit(ctx, st, at, before, fixed)                                               # S04-R-09
    vc_constrained = sum(e.constrained for e in elements) + realised + concession
    vc_excluded = sum(e.unconstrained - e.constrained for e in elements)
    returns_memo = returns.expected_reduction(ctx, st, at, before)                                              # S04-R-08
    payable = customer_consideration.reduction(ctx, st, at, before)                                             # S04-R-14 to R-17
    financing = financing.adjustment(ctx, st, at, before, tb)                                                   # S04-R-10 to R-13
    noncash = noncash.measure(ctx, st, at)                                                                      # S04-R-18
    tax = taxes.excluded(ctx, st, at, before)                                                                   # S04-R-20
    total = fixed + vc_constrained + returns_memo + payable + financing + noncash
    basis = total - returns_memo - payable
    for name, q in TP_COMPONENTS(...):                                                                          # trace, CV-51
        tb.node(measure=name, subject_key=st.group_key, period_key=None, value=q.posted, currency=ctx.txn_currency,
                minor_unit=mu, exact=q.exact, formula_id=f"tp.{name}.v1", inputs=q.inputs, narrative_key=f"tp.{name}")
    return TpBuildUp(at=at, before_event_key=before and before.event_key, fixed=fixed, ..., total=total,
                     allocation_basis=basis, elements=tuple(elements))

def run(ctx, st: PobState, tb) -> PricedState:
    tp0 = price_at(ctx, st, at=st.inception_date, before=first_boundary(st), tb=tb)
    targets = specialist.targets(ctx, st, tb)                                                                   # S04-R-13, R-17, R-18, R-19
    return PricedState(pob=st, tp=tp0, price_at=price_at, tp_unconstrained=unconstrained_view(ctx, st), specialist_targets=targets, ...)
```

### 4.3 Rules

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S04-R-01 | **Unit and date.** The price is measured per group in the transaction currency, at a date `at` and an ENG-06 position `before` (the boundary event being applied, or None for the end of the stream). A version pin or event counts only when it precedes `before` (S01-R-18). Every exact component is a `Fraction`; its posted value is `round_half_up(exact, μ)` (CV-35). **Versions in force at inception (rev 1.145; item ENG-INCEPTION-ESTIMATE-1).** A version in force at inception (S01-R-18) counts at every position: the inception price — measured before the first boundary event of the group, an `ESTIMATE_CHANGED` effective on or before the inception not being one — holds it wherever its event stands among the events of the inception date |
| S04-R-02 | **Build-up and allocation basis.** `total` follows REQ-TP-001: fixed + constrained VC (including realised usage and royalty amounts and implicit price concessions) ± expected returns − consideration payable ± financing adjustment + noncash consideration; sales taxes and out-of-scope amounts are excluded and reported as memo members. Stage 05 allocates `allocation_basis` = `total` − `expected_returns` − `consideration_payable`, because expected returns are VC allocated entirely to the returnable obligation and realised through its revenue target (ALG-06 §2.7.2; 606-10-32-40), and consideration payable is released against revenue by JET-14 at the POL-049 rate (606-10-32-27; CHK-133). [J] Both reductions are measured on their own drivers, so spreading them by relative SSP would misstate them. `contract_version.transaction_price` = `total.posted`; `expected_returns_amount` = `expected_returns.posted` ≤ 0 and `consideration_payable_amount` = `consideration_payable.posted` ≤ 0. These memo columns carry the member sign, and answer keys assert the signed figures, for example `RET-CHK-029-S3-EX22` −300.00 and `CPC-CHK-133-S3-EX32` −1,500,000.00 (rev 1.3; ADJUDICATION.md R-SGN-01). Obligation `revenue_cum` targets are gross of consideration payable (ENGINE_SPEC_B Table 14-A, JET-02 row). `contract_version.revenue_cum` is Σ obligation `revenue_cum` less the cumulative JET-14 release `incentive_release_cum` = the posted ordinary release (S04-R-16) plus the sum of the posted, individually rounded share-based element targets (S04-R-17), with no rounding of their aggregate (two elements each exactly 0.005 post E = 0.02) (D-91), taken as the posted target of the contracting entity's period holding d_v (the basis of the CHK-120 keys and of the answer-key oracle; a d_v-dated measurement for a mid-period version date is post-rc): after month 1 of CHK-133 the obligation holds 2,000,000.00 and the contract version 1,800,000.00; CHK-120 380,000.00 after 400,000.00. The persisted identity is therefore 04 DB-17 V1 as published in rev 1.2: Σ `allocated_amount` = `transaction_price` − `consideration_payable_amount`, with the returns-adjusted `allocated_amount` of ENGINE_SPEC_B S09-R-23. The engine invariant is Σ `a_posted` = `allocation_basis.posted` (PROP:P1; DG-ENG-05; OQ-A-04 resolved by D-76) |
| S04-R-03 | **Fixed consideration.** Σ stated prices of in-scope obligations (after the bundle split, agent net and shipping merge of stage 03), including `LEASE_842` targets (PT-09) and excluding `VC_LINE` obligations. `out_of_scope` = Σ `out_of_scope_amount` of routed-out lines (REQ-CON-016) |
| S04-R-04 | **VC elements.** Every estimate element of kind `VARIABLE_CONSIDERATION` of a member contract (or a portfolio containing it) with a pin at `at`. Types `USAGE` and `ROYALTY` that meet 606-10-32-40 or 55-65 are not estimated (table 1.4-A) and enter through S04-R-06. Type `CLAIM` enters only with a reviewed `OTHER` outcome `claim_enforceable = true` for the element (POL-244; 606-10-25-11). Sign convention (D-97 (23); rev 1.10): the amount columns of a `VARIABLE_CONSIDERATION` or `IMPLICIT_PRICE_CONCESSION` version hold magnitudes (04 B3-D16); the signed contribution to the transaction price is magnitude × `direction` (`INCREASE` adds, `DECREASE` reduces); an absent `direction` is read by the B3-DG-17 default (CV-25 hash exception, rev 1.8) |
| S04-R-05 | **Estimation methods (POL-040; 606-10-32-8).** Unconstrained estimate U: `EXPECTED_VALUE` → Σ amount × probability over `scenarios` (probabilities sum to 1, checked at submission); `MOST_LIKELY_AMOUNT` → the amount of the scenario with the greatest probability, ties broken by the amount giving the lower transaction price [J: the more conservative of equally likely outcomes]; `ENTERED_AMOUNT` → `constrained_amount` (parity; POL-040 `ENTERED_AMOUNT`). When `unconstrained_amount` is stored, it must equal the method result exactly (CV-45). The method is locked on version 1 (POL-040; `ESTIMATE_METHOD_LOCKED` at submission) |
| S04-R-06 | **Realised variable amounts.** For an obligation whose component is `PERIOD_VC` or `ROYALTY` (ENGINE_SPEC_B S09-R-01): realised usage fees = Σ `USAGE_REPORTED.rated_amount` of events preceding `before` with `usage_period_end ≤ at`; realised royalties = Σ statements plus, under POL-056 `ACCRUE_ESTIMATE`, the `ROYALTY_ACCRUAL` pins of usage periods ended by `at` without a statement. Under POL-240 `ESTIMATE_MEASUREMENT_PERIOD_TP` a usage obligation is priced instead by its VC element (PT-01): TP component = max(minimum commitment, expected fees), constrained (CHK-110). Under POL-057 `FIXED_ON_LICENCE_PATTERN` the minimum guarantee is fixed consideration and realised royalties count only above the cumulative guarantee. **Right to invoice (rev 1.21, D-98 candidate 28; ENC-6).** For a `RIGHT_TO_INVOICE` obligation the realised amount is the right to invoice R for performance to date (ENGINE_SPEC_B S09-R-18 source order as completed by D-87 L6-5-Q-16, over the events preceding `before`) above the line's stated price P: max(0, R − P), the part the `FIXED` component (allocated from P, measured by R ÷ P) does not carry; P = 0 realises all of R. The transaction price therefore carries R whenever the `FIXED` allocation equals P (REC-FS-08; POS-CHK-010 under candidate 29) |
| S04-R-07 | **Constraint (POL-041; 606-10-32-11 to 32-13).** Constrained amount K: `PREPARER_CONSTRAINED_AMOUNT` → the version's `constrained_amount` (V6 was validated at submission; a violation in an approved version is CV-45); `NOT_APPLIED` → K = U. `vc_excluded` = Σ (U − K), kept for the 606-10-50-15 narrative (REQ-TP-003) |
| S04-R-08 | **Expected returns (D-22; ALG-06; POL-051).** For each returnable obligation p (S04-R-08a), with the `ReturnState` that `returns.py` publishes at `at` (ENGINE_SPEC_B §9.2.7): Y_p = units returned to `at`, E_p = expected further returns (the `expected_quantity` of the `RETURN_RATE` pin less units returned after the pin's effective date, never below 0; portfolio pins give rate × quantity transferred, PT-06), r_p = X_p ÷ Q_p; `expected_returns` = −Σ round(r_p × (Y_p + E_p)) (memo; the revenue target of stage 09 applies the reduction). [J] Measuring the memo on Y + E, as the returns-adjusted `allocated_amount` of S09-R-23 is measured, keeps 04 DB-17 V1 exact after actual returns (rev 1.2; D-76 allocation-basis ruling on OQ-A-04). S04-R-08a: an obligation is returnable when a `RETURN_RATE` element targets it, or when its resolved `returns.model` is `EXPECTED_RETURNS` at level O, C or P (set on the obligation, contract or product template). Such an obligation of a contract whose status is not `DRAFT` with no pin at its first transfer and no reviewed `OTHER` outcome `returns_immaterial = true` raises `RETURN_ESTIMATE_MISSING` (`ERROR`). `ACTUAL_RETURNS_ONLY` gives E = 0 (parity). [J] A framework default alone never makes a service obligation returnable (OQ-A-15) |
| S04-R-08b | **Return paths (published for stage 09).** For every returnable obligation, `AllocatedState.return_paths[subject key]` = `ReturnPath(estimate_key, pins, r, p_ref, policy)`: the `RETURN_RATE` element key; the ordered pins (effective date, version key, `expected_quantity`, `carrying_cost_per_unit`, `recovery_cost_per_unit`, `window_end_date`); r = x_exact ÷ Q of the segment in force at each date (pre-return basis, ALG-06 §2.7.1); p_ref = the billed unit price of transferred units (Σ billed ÷ units billed), else the stated unit price; the resolved POL-051 to POL-053 values. Stage 09 builds `ReturnState` from it (ENGINE_SPEC_B §9.2.7) and reports the obligation's `allocated_amount` net of the expected-return reduction (ENGINE_SPEC_B S09-R-23); the segments keep the gross allocation |
| S04-R-09 | **Implicit price concession (606-10-25-1(e), 32-7; FASB Example 2).** With an `IMPLICIT_PRICE_CONCESSION` pin at `at`: κ = `rate`, or (stated consideration − `expected_total_amount`) ÷ stated consideration; the concession component = −round(κ × Σ fixed consideration of the affected obligations) and is part of `vc_constrained`. A later fall in expected collection caused by credit deterioration is never a change to κ (POL-125) |
| S04-R-10 | **SFC test (POL-046; 606-10-32-15 to 32-18).** At booking and at every boundary, per obligation p with a transfer date T_p (point in time: expected transfer = line start date, else booking date; over time: the exact midpoint of [start, end] as an ordinal day number): W = Σ amount_i × ordinal(date_i) ÷ Σ amount_i over `payment_schedule` points (04 API-S-PaymentPoint, rev 1.2; OQ-A-09 resolved by D-76). The gap is significant when W < ordinal(T_p − 12 months) or W > ordinal(T_p + 12 months) (month arithmetic of `dates.add_months`). With POL-046 `APPLY` and no significant gap, no adjustment. Otherwise an adjustment applies unless a reviewed `SFC_ASSESSMENT` outcome for p (or `""`) has `significant = false` or `exception_32_17` ∈ {`A`, `B`, `C`}; with no such record and status not `DRAFT`, `SFC_REVIEW_REQUIRED` (`ERROR`). Without a payment schedule the test is not run, and trace params record `reason = NO_PAYMENT_SCHEDULE`; a template with `sfc_assessment_required = true` then raises `SFC_REVIEW_REQUIRED` unless the reviewed record exists |
| S04-R-10a | **Judged significant without a payment schedule (D-87 L5-3-Q-8; D-91 C606-05; 606-10-32-15 to 32-20).** When no payment schedule is in force (S04-R-10 `reason = NO_PAYMENT_SCHEDULE`) and the governing reviewed `SFC_ASSESSMENT` for p (or `""`) has `significant = true` and `exception_32_17 = NONE`, an adjustment applies with no gap test (`reason = JUDGEMENT_SIGNIFICANT`, trace param `payment_source`), on payment points derived per member contract from the ENG-06 stream `CanonicalBundle.events` preceding `before`: (a) the contract's cash `PAYMENT_RECEIVED` receipts (`form` `CASH`), summed per the receipt event's `effective_date` (not the payload `receipt_date`; `payment_source = PAYMENT_RECEIVED`); else (b) the contract's billing lines recognised under ENGINE_SPEC_B S10-R-07 at contract scope with identity (contract, `invoice_number`, `line_external_id`): the first-seen event of an identity is the line and enters at the line event's `effective_date` (not `issue_date` or `due_date`) for its amount; a repeated identity whose `is_cancellable` is not `true` (`false` or absent, the 04 default) is a status update and adds no point; a repeated identity with `is_cancellable = true` is another line (stage 10 parity; uniqueness at the API and import channels is a post-rc question, D-91); `CREDIT_MEMO_RECORDED` amounts net by effective date; dates netting to 0 are dropped (`payment_source = BILLING_RECORDED`). A status update with a different amount is `INVOICE_STATUS_UPDATE_MISMATCH` (`ERROR`, stage 10, CV-15); stage 04 neither re-validates nor raises and keeps the first line's amount. The payload `obligation_key` is not part of the identity. Stage 04 never deduplicates a billing record only because it shares an identifier. A missing rate still raises `SFC_RATE_MISSING`. T: over time as S04-R-10; point in time `start_date`, else the delivery at which net delivered reaches the line quantity, else open (`transfer_open`; ADVANCE accretion with no relief and no CSP while open). ADVANCE CSP for every schedule: X = Σ pay_i·v^(n_i) ÷ Σ_k g_k·v^k (v = 1 ÷ (1 + j); g_k the obligation's exact revenue share at the k-th month end; point in time g = 1 at m(start, T)); B_k = B_(k−1)(1 + j) + pay_k − X·g_k settles to 0 at the last revenue month. Trace params `payments` (`<step>:<amount>`), `shares`, `payment_points`, `status_updates_ignored`. [J] `ENGINE` mode (POL-004, POL-123; D-91 C606-05g, implemented by lane ENG-C6; D-97 (4), (5), (15), (18)): a billing point is dated its S10-R-06 unconditional date (a noncancellable line: its effective date; a cancellable line: the effective date of its first same-amount noncancellable status update, or the applied receipt, which then governs under (a)); a line with no unconditional date at `at` is memo-only and contributes no point. Under a `significant = true` judgement the `SFC_REVIEW_REQUIRED` (`ERROR`, CV-15) predicate reads the kept lines, not the netted points, at four boundaries (D-97 (5) v2): (i) no billing line at the position → the S04-R-10 no-schedule outcome, no finding (nothing is known about payment timing before the first document); (ii) billing lines exist and every kept line is memo-only → the finding, nothing accretes; (iii) unconditional lines whose points net to zero by date beside memo-only lines → NOT the finding: the lines were eligible, the netted date is merely dropped, and the trace param `eligible_lines` distinguishes the case; (iv) a receipts-first position (branch (a)) never raises the finding. PROVISIONAL treatment pending AD-8 (D-97 (4) v2 [G12]): under (a) an unpaid remainder of a cancellable line is treated as payable at transfer under the explicit pending PREMISE that a memo-only document creates no unconditional right and that, absent a stated promised payment date before transfer, payment is expected at transfer — a pending accounting judgement, not a fact encoded by the absence of a payment date; the engine's result confers no independent accounting authority. Under the premise the remainder carries no financing component (606-10-32-15/16) and enters the transaction price at its stated amount; applying the receipt to the line (S10-R-06: the line becomes billed) changes billing presentation only, so the applied and unapplied variants carry identical financing; a later receipt of the remainder before transfer is a payment point at its own date; a stated payment-due date on a cancellable line is not read (registered with AD-8). The accountant resolves the premise, any stated or contradictory payment timing in the inputs and the applied / unapplied scope before production acceptance. A credit memo against a still-memo-only line is memo-only with its document and nets only when and where the line becomes unconditional; until that netting is built the engine raises `CREDIT_MEMO_ON_MEMO_ONLY_LINE` (`ERROR`, CV-15; 04 table 15.4-B) instead of entering a negative point at the memo's date — a temporary rejection separate from the pending A19 premise (D-97 (15) [G12]). Pre-netting scope (D-97 (15) v3): the memo is associated by `credited_invoice_number` with the document's kept lines; the rejection covers a credited document ANY of whose kept lines is memo-only at the position, and a memo dated before the document's earliest S10-R-06 date (the memo-only → unconditional transition: a later status update alone is not sufficient remediation for a memo dated before it; the document must be unconditional on or before the memo's date, or the memo re-issued at that date); repeated status updates do not move the association; under (a) credit memos enter no point and the finding never raises; a memo against a document unconditional at or before the memo's date nets at its own date (the ERP 15.00 / 3,381.48 control unchanged, not `ENGINE` acceptance). The unconditional date is the project's payment-timing proxy where no expected payment date exists (606-10-32-15/16 assess timing and the cash selling price; 32-20 limits interest to a recognised balance); `ERP` mode counts the effective date. Check figures: SFC-FS-11-CASEB X 295,701.23; SFC-S3-EX29 in `ERP` mode 4,020.00 / 4,246.71 / 4,508.64 with or without a matching status update; the same world in `ENGINE` mode with a cancellable 1 January line made noncancellable on 20 February 4,020.00 / 4,225.58 / 4,486.21 (SFC-CHK-136-S3-EX29-ENGINE-CANCELLABLE-UPDATE; 23 month ends); the same world in `ENGINE` mode with a 1,000.00 receipt on 1 January against the cancellable 4,000.00 line: 5.00 / 1,005.00 in January, cash selling price of the receipt 1,127.16, transaction price and revenue 4,127.16, applied and unapplied alike (A18; 4,508.64 rejected for `ENGINE` mode); a 1,000.00 credit memo against a noncancellable or `ERP` line nets the points to 3,000.00 (15.00 / 3,381.48); SFC-CHK-136 4,494.40; JE-CHK-136 4,508.64; SFC-FS-11-CASEA (not significant) 0 |
| S04-R-11 | **Rate (POL-047; 606-10-32-19).** The approved contract-level override `sfc.discount_rate_basis` with value `{basis, annual_rate, compounding}` locked at inception (05 §3.6.3; `compounding` ∈ {`MONTHLY`, `ANNUAL`}, default `MONTHLY`: POLICIES POL-047 rev 1.2 and the 04 T-CON-23 value schema; D-76 ruling on the significant financing component rate; OQ-A-16 resolved by D-76). Missing → `SFC_RATE_MISSING` (`ERROR`). `MONTHLY`: monthly rate j = annual_rate ÷ 12. `ANNUAL`: yearly rate i = annual_rate |
| S04-R-12 | **Cash selling price and accretion (Subtopic 835-30; CV-31).** Month count (rev 1.3; ADJUDICATION.md R-SFC-02, R-SFC-04): m(a, b) = the number of calendar month ends e with a < e ≤ b (`dates.month_ends_between`). [J] The accretion schedule below steps once per month end, so discounting on the same grid settles the schedule exactly: a payment at the k-th month end after a transfer on the first day of a month is k months away (CHK-136, CHK-137). Deferred payment (payments after T): CSP = Σ payment_i ÷ (1 + j)^n_i with n_i = m(T, date_i), or with `ANNUAL` n_i = ⌊m(T, date_i) ÷ 12⌋ whole years and rate i; `financing_adjustment` = round(CSP) − Σ payments (negative). Advance payment (payments before T): the accreted balance at T = Σ payment_i × (1 + j)^n_i with n_i = m(date_i, T), or with `ANNUAL` compounding over ⌊m(date_i, T) ÷ 12⌋ whole years; `financing_adjustment` = round(accreted) − Σ payments (positive). Accretion schedule: the exact balance B opens at CSP (deferred) or at the first payment (advance); each month B ← B × (1 + j) − payments of the month (`MONTHLY`); with `ANNUAL`, interest of contract year y is B_y × i on the opening balance of the year and is spread over the months of the year by `cumulative_posted(interest_y, round(interest_y), k ÷ 12)`. Target `financing_interest_cum(t)` = round(Σ exact interest through t) (JET-11a for deferred, JET-11b for advance). Payments beyond the balance by more than one minor unit raise `SFC_SCHEDULE_UNSETTLED` (`ERROR`). A payment event or a changed schedule reruns the schedule from inception (05 §3.6.3) |
| S04-R-12a | **Accretion while expected returns exclude the whole consideration (rev 1.3; 606-10-55-229 to 55-232; ADJUDICATION.md R-SFC-05).** For a deferred payment (JET-11a), L is the first date on or after T on which the revenue target of an obligation that carries the financing adjustment is positive (ENGINE_SPEC_B §9.2.7: N − Y − E > 0, for example `window_end_date` itself, on which the period-end measurement has E = 0: ENGINE_SPEC_B S09-R-26; rev 1.57). While that target is 0 because expected returns exclude the whole consideration, no receivable or contract asset exists and nothing accretes. When at least one month end e_m of the S04-R-12 inception schedule satisfies e_m ≤ L: `financing_interest_cum(t)` = 0 for t ≤ L, and afterwards `cumulative_posted(X_int, X_int, g_t)`, with X_int = Σ payments − round(CSP) and g_t = Σ w_m over L < e_m ≤ t ÷ Σ w_m over e_m > L. w_m is the exact interest of month m of the inception schedule: B_(m−1) × j under `MONTHLY`, interest_y ÷ 12 for the months of year y under `ANNUAL`. [J] The suspended interest is recognised over the remaining months with no catch-up, and the balance still settles at the last payment. A constant-yield rate over the remaining term would need a fractional power (CV-31). When no month end falls on or before L, S04-R-12 applies unchanged. CSP, `financing_adjustment` and the POL-047 rate do not change. An advance payment (JET-11b) is never suspended, because the contract liability exists from receipt. Trace params of `financing_interest_cum` record `suspended_until` = L (EX-04-H) |
| S04-R-13 | **Financing targets.** Per (contract, contracting entity, book) and period end: `financing_interest_cum`, `financed_balance` (exact and posted). The financed balance is a separate measure. Accretion posted by JET-11 is part of the net position, and for the presentation split it is attributed to the obligations for which S04-R-10 requires an adjustment (D-76 ruling on financing interest; ENGINE_SPEC_B S10-R-10, S10-R-20; CHK-137) |
| S04-R-14 | **Consideration payable (606-10-32-25, 32-26).** For each payable promise (04 API-S-ConsiderationPayable, rev 1.2; OQ-A-08 resolved by D-76) {amount, promise_date, related_obligation_keys, distinct_good_fair_value, committed_purchases, share_based}: reduction R = amount when there is no distinct good; else max(0, amount − distinct_good_fair_value), with the whole amount when the fair value is not reasonably estimable (`distinct_good_fair_value` None). `consideration_payable` = −Σ R of promises dated ≤ `at` (memo, S04-R-02) |
| S04-R-15 | **Timing (606-10-32-27).** A promise made after revenue for the related purchases was recognised is released immediately at the promise date (JET-14 Dr `REVENUE` / Cr `CONSIDERATION_PAYABLE`). A promise made before related revenue creates the incentive asset R at the promise date (JET-14 Dr `CUSTOMER_INCENTIVE_ASSET` / Cr `CONSIDERATION_PAYABLE`) |
| S04-R-16 | **Release rate (POL-049).** ρ = R ÷ purchases, with purchases = `expected_total_amount` of the `EXPECTED_PURCHASES` pin (`EXPECTED_PURCHASES`) or `committed_purchases` (`COMMITTED_PURCHASES`). Release target at t = min(R, round(R_released_before + ρ′ × invoiced related purchases since the latest rate change)), where a new pin changes the rate prospectively: ρ′ = (R − released) ÷ (purchases′ − invoiced to date). The posted target `incentive_release_ordinary_cum` is the JET-14 release part on its own (ENGINE_SPEC_B S14-R-01; D-91): the share-based reduction is never netted into or out of it, and `customer_incentive_asset`(t) = posted `consideration_payable`(t) − posted `incentive_release_ordinary_cum`(t), so the T-CON-09 asset equals promises less releases at every period end (no independent rounding of the asset; ENGINE_SPEC_B S14-R-11). The invoice-driven release under advance billing is post-rc question L9-CPC-Q-1. CHK-133 |
| S04-R-17 | **Share-based consideration payable (PT-10; POL-246; 606-10-32-25A, 32-27; ASU 2025-04; D-91).** Per `SHARE_BASED_CONSIDERATION` element (04 E-09, T-CON-13): cumulative reduction Red(t) = grant-date fair value × (1 when `vesting_probable` under the version pinned at t, else 0) × R(t) ÷ `expected_total_amount`, capped at the fair value and 0 while t < `grant_date`, where R(t) is the cumulative revenue recognised through the contracting entity's period end t for the related obligations: the posted stage 09 `revenue_cum` node of each related obligation at its latest performing-entity period end c ≤ t (S09-R-23, S09-R-41; mapped as S10-R-09 relief) less the posted JET-05c concession portions of those obligations dated after c and on or before t, which that node cannot yet carry; a portion dated on or before c is already in the node and is not subtracted again, so each concession counts once (ENGINE_SPEC_B S10-R-26 rev 1.8; D-91 C606-03 (1) erratum). The related obligations are the union of `related_obligation_keys` of the contract's promises with `share_based = true` (empty = every obligation); when the contract has no share-based promise, the element's `target_obligation_keys` / `obligation_key`, else every obligation; element keys, when set, must equal the promise scope, and every key must name an obligation of the contract, else `CPC_RELATED_SCOPE_INVALID` (`ERROR`, 04 table 15.4-C). R(t) counts related revenue recognised before the grant date once the grant exists (POL-246 LATER_OF; 606-10-32-27). Invoices never drive the measure. Exact fractions; posted = `round_half_up` once per element and period (CV-35). `expected_total_amount` ≤ 0 with R(t) ≠ 0 raises `NON_FINITE_AMOUNT` (CV-32); E ≤ 0 with R(t) = 0 gives 0. The measurement is produced by stage 10 (ENGINE_SPEC_B §10.1, S10-R-26) and posts by JET-14 to `BILLING_CLEARING` (`EQUITY`) as its own part (ENGINE_SPEC_B S14-R-01); stage 04 publishes only the ordinary release (`incentive_release_ordinary_cum`). Supersedes lane note L2-2-Q-27 |
| S04-R-18 | **Noncash consideration (POL-048; 606-10-32-21 to 32-24; ASU 2025-07).** For each noncash promise (04 API-S-NoncashConsideration, rev 1.2; OQ-A-07 resolved by D-76) {units, fair_value_per_unit, measurement_date, variability, asset_type}: measured at contract inception in the ASC606 book (FORCED) and at the resolved date in the IFRS15 book; `noncash` = Σ round(units × fair value). Variability other than the form of consideration is a VC element (32-23). Target `noncash_asset_recognised_cum(t)` = round(fair value × units whose right is unconditional by t: increments completed) (JET-17; share-based noncash consideration stays in Topic 606 until unconditional, 606-10-15-3A) |
| S04-R-19 | **Assurance-warranty accrual (EMOD-21; POL-022 `ENGINE`).** Per product obligation p with `assurance_cost_per_unit` c: `warranty_accrual_cum(t)` = round(c × (transferred units through t)), where transferred units follow the ledger (deliveries less returns). JET-16 in stage 14 |
| S04-R-20 | **Sales taxes (POL-045; 606-10-32-2A).** ASC606 book `EXCLUDE_ALL_IN_SCOPE`: invoice `tax_amount` never enters the price; `sales_tax_excluded` = Σ `tax_amount` of included invoices dated ≤ `at` (memo); under POL-004 `ENGINE`, JET-03 credits `SALES_TAX_PAYABLE`. IFRS15 book `ASSESS_EACH_TAX` (FORCED): every tax line is treated as collected on behalf of a third party (`AGENT`) and excluded, until a tax-type principal mapping exists (OQ-A-14). Identity (D-97 (32); rev 1.10): tax lines follow the S10-R-07 identity through the kernel helper `erev_engine/billing_identity.py` — a status update (same identity, `is_cancellable` false, same amount) adds no tax, a repeated cancellable identity is a new line (aligned by lane ENG-B4, 790a93e) |
| S04-R-21 | **Unconstrained, credit-adjusted view (EMOD-17).** `tp_unconstrained[contract].at(d)` = fixed + Σ U + realised + concession + financing adjustment + noncash − Σ R + expected returns memo; replaced by the latest `COLLECTIBILITY_ASSESSED.expected_collectible_amount` of the book when lower (605-35-25-46A; ENGINE_SPEC_B S11-R-11a) |

### 4.4 Invariants, findings, trace and references

| Item | Content |
|---|---|
| Invariants | S04-INV-01: `total.exact` = sum of its members' exact values. S04-INV-02: for every element, K lies between `most_conservative_amount` and U inclusive, when both are stored (V6). S04-INV-03: `allocation_basis.exact` = `total.exact` − `expected_returns.exact` − `consideration_payable.exact`. S04-INV-04: released incentive ≤ R per promise; `financed_balance` at settlement is within one minor unit of 0 |
| Findings | `RETURN_ESTIMATE_MISSING`, `SFC_REVIEW_REQUIRED`, `SFC_RATE_MISSING`, `SFC_SCHEDULE_UNSETTLED`, `NON_FINITE_AMOUNT` |
| Trace nodes | Version-state nodes named as T-CON-08 columns on the group key: `transaction_price`, `fixed_consideration`, `vc_constrained_amount`, `vc_excluded_amount`, `expected_returns_amount`, `consideration_payable_amount`, `financing_adjustment_amount`, `noncash_consideration_amount`, `sales_tax_excluded_amount`, `out_of_scope_amount`; `tp_allocation_basis`; boundary nodes `transaction_price@<event key>`; per element `vc_unconstrained:<estimate key>:-` and `vc_constrained:<estimate key>:-` with the pinned version in params; `financing_interest_cum:<contract>@<entity>:<period>` (params `suspended_until` under S04-R-12a, rev 1.3); `cash_selling_price:<ob>:-`; `consideration_payable:<contract>@<entity>:<period>`; `incentive_release_ordinary_cum:<contract>@<entity>:<period>`; `customer_incentive_asset:<contract>@<entity>:<period>` (the share-based and composed release nodes are stage 10 nodes, ENGINE_SPEC_B §10.5; D-91); `warranty_accrual_cum:<ob>:<period>`; `noncash_asset_recognised_cum:<contract>@<entity>:<period>` |
| Formula ids | `tp.buildup.v1`, `tp.fixed.v1`, `tp.vc_expected_value.v1`, `tp.vc_most_likely.v1`, `tp.vc_entered.v1`, `tp.vc_constrained.v1`, `tp.realised_usage.v1`, `tp.returns_expected.v1`, `tp.concession_implicit.v1`, `tp.cpc_reduction.v1`, `tp.cpc_release.v1`, `tp.noncash.v1`, `tp.tax_excluded.v1`, `tp.warranty_accrual.v1`, `sfc.gap_test.v1`, `sfc.cash_selling_price.v1`, `sfc.effective_interest.monthly.v1`, `sfc.effective_interest.annual.v1`, `sfc.accretion.v1`. `tp.cpc_release.v1` is also emitted by stage 10 and `tp.cpc_share_based.v1` is a stage 10 formula (ENGINE_SPEC_B §10.5; D-91) |

### 4.5 Worked examples

EX-04-A (research 04 S3-EX21-EXTENDED; CHK-100 inputs): base 2,500,000.00; daily incentive or penalty by `EXPECTED_VALUE` over scenarios (0.2, +100,000), (0.5, 0), (0.3, −50,000) → U = 5,000.00; bonus 150,000.00 by `MOST_LIKELY_AMOUNT` → U = 150,000.00, version 1 constrained to 0.00. Year 1 price: `fixed` 2,500,000.00; `vc_constrained` 5,000.00; `vc_excluded` 150,000.00; `total` = `allocation_basis` = 2,505,000.00. With progress 40%, the revenue target is `cumulative_posted`(2,505,000, 250,500,000, 0.40) = 1,002,000.00. Stage 08 applies version 2 (EX-08-B).

EX-04-B (CHK-136; research 04 S3-EX29; re-baselined in rev 1.2 under the D-76 ruling on the significant financing component rate): 4,000.00 paid on 1 January 2026 for an asset transferred on 1 January 2028; POL-046 gap 24 months > 12; rate override {`ENTITY_BORROWING_RATE`, 0.06, `MONTHLY`} (POL-047 default compounding), j = 0.005. The exact cumulative interest through month m is 4,000 × (1.005^m − 1), and `financing_interest_cum` = round of it (JET-11b): month 1 20.00; month 12 246.71 (month-12 interest 21.13); month 24 508.64 (month-24 interest 22.43). Year 1 246.71; year 2 261.93; contract liability 4,246.71 at the end of year 1 and 4,508.64 at the end of year 2; `financing_adjustment` = round(4,000 × 1.005^24) − 4,000.00 = +508.64; `total` 4,508.64 (revenue at transfer). `ANNUAL` variant (CHK-136 second row): interest year 1 = 4,000 × 0.06 = 240.00; year 2 = 4,240 × 0.06 = 254.40; `financing_adjustment` +494.40; `total` 4,494.40; year-1 monthly targets by cumulative rounding of 240.00 over 12 months: 20.00 a month.

EX-04-B2 (CHK-137; research 04 S3-EX28 Case B): 60 monthly instalments of 18,871.00 after transfer; override {`CUSTOMER_CREDIT_RATE`, 0.12, `MONTHLY`}, j = 0.01. CSP = Σ 18,871 ÷ 1.01^n, n = 1 to 60 → 848,346.53; `financing_adjustment` = 848,346.53 − 1,132,260.00 = −283,913.47; month-1 interest round(848,346.5298… × 0.01) = 8,483.47 (JET-11a). Instalment k falls at the k-th month end after the transfer on 1 January 2026, so n = m(T, date_k) = k (S04-R-12 rev 1.3). A floor of whole months would give n = k − 1, a CSP of 856,830.00 and a schedule that does not settle.

EX-04-C (CHK-135; research 04 S3-EX31): 100 shares of the customer per completed week for 52 weeks; fair value 10.00 per share at inception → `noncash` 52,000.00; week-1 target of `noncash_asset_recognised_cum` 1,000.00; the 200.00 gain at receipt is outside revenue.

EX-04-D (CHK-133; research 04 S3-EX32): 1,500,000.00 paid at inception for shelving (no distinct good); committed purchases 15,000,000.00; POL-049 `COMMITTED_PURCHASES`. ρ = 1/10; month-1 invoice 2,000,000.00 → release 200,000.00 (JET-14); net revenue 1,800,000.00; incentive asset 1,300,000.00. `consideration_payable` −1,500,000.00 (memo), `allocation_basis` unchanged. Rev 1.3 (R-SGN-01): `consideration_payable_amount` −1,500,000.00; `transaction_price` 13,500,000.00; Σ `allocated_amount` 15,000,000.00 = 13,500,000.00 − (−1,500,000.00) (04 DB-17 V1). After month 1 the obligation's `revenue_cum` is 2,000,000.00 (gross target) and `contract_version.revenue_cum` is 1,800,000.00.

EX-04-E (CHK-023): invoice 1,080.00 including 80.00 sales tax; `billing.posting = ENGINE`. The price excludes the tax (`total` 1,000.00; `sales_tax_excluded` 80.00); JET-03 Dr AR 1,080.00 / Cr CL 1,000.00, Cr `SALES_TAX_PAYABLE` 80.00.

EX-04-F (CHK-138; FASB Example 2): 1,000 units invoiced 1,000,000.00; expected entitlement 400,000.00 → κ = 3/5; concession component −600,000.00; `total` 400,000.00.

EX-04-G (CHK-029; FASB Example 22): 100 units at 100.00 with a return right; `RETURN_RATE` pin E = 3. `expected_returns` = −round(100 × 3) = −300.00; `total` 9,700.00; `allocation_basis` 10,000.00, all targeted at the returnable obligation; the revenue target at transfer is round(100 × (100 − 0 − 3)) = 9,700.00 (ENGINE_SPEC_B §9.2.7).

EX-04-G2 (rev 1.3; S04-R-08 with Y > 0; CHK-029 and CHK-060 inputs; keys `RET-CHK-029-S3-EX22` and `RET-CHK-060-S3-EX22-REVISED`; ADJUDICATION.md R-RET-01): r = 100.00. At the end of February 2026 two units have been returned (Y = 2) and E has fallen to 1: `expected_returns` = −round(100 × (2 + 1)) = −300.00; `total` 9,700.00. The obligation's `allocated_amount` is 10,000.00 − 300.00 = 9,700.00, equal to the revenue target round(100 × (100 − 2 − 1)) = 9,700.00, so DB-17 and V1 hold. At window expiry (Y = 2, E = 0): memo −200.00; `total` 9,800.00; revenue 9,800.00. CHK-060 (E revised to 4 before any return): after the two returns E = 2, memo −400.00 and `total` 9,600.00; at expiry −200.00 and 9,800.00. A memo measured on E alone would be −100.00 after the returns and would break V1 by 200.00.

EX-04-H (rev 1.3, L and the month of recognition amended at rev 1.57; S04-R-12a; FASB Example 26 with its 90-day return right; research 04 S3-EX26; key `SFC-S3-EX26-RETURN-RIGHT`; ADJUDICATION.md R-SFC-05): a product costing 80.00 transfers on 1 January 2026 for 121.00 payable on 31 December 2027; override {`CUSTOMER_CREDIT_RATE`, 0.10, `ANNUAL`}. m = 24, so CSP = 121 ÷ 1.1² = 100.00 and `financing_adjustment` −21.00. The `RETURN_RATE` pin expects the one unit back until the right lapses at the end of `window_end_date` 31 March 2026: at every measurement before that date E = 1, revenue target 0.00, `expected_returns` −100.00, `total` 0.00, refund liability 0.00 (nothing is billed or paid) and return asset 80.00, and `financing_interest_cum` is 0.00 through 31 March 2026. The measurement dated 31 March 2026 has E = 0 (ENGINE_SPEC_B S09-R-26; D-87 L6-5-Q-25), so L = 31 March 2026. The inception weights are w_m = 10 ÷ 12 for months 1 to 12 and 11 ÷ 12 for months 13 to 24; Σ over months 4 to 24 = 18.5; X_int = 21.00. Targets: April 2026 round(21 × (10 ÷ 12) ÷ 18.5) = 0.95; end of 2026 round(21 × 7.5 ÷ 18.5) = 8.51 (months 4 to 12); through November 2027 19.96; December 2027 1.04; months 13 to 24 in total 12.49. Revenue 100.00 posts in March 2026, and the return asset of 80.00 is released in the same month. The unbilled receivable is 100.00 at 31 March 2026, 100.95 at 30 April 2026, 108.51 at 31 December 2026 and 121.00 before the invoice of 31 December 2027. The figures equal research 04 S3-EX26 (8.51 and 12.49), which used a constant monthly rate of 0.9117%.

## 5. Stage 05: SSP resolution and allocation

### 5.1 Scope, inputs and outputs

Stage 05 resolves the SSP of every obligation from the pinned SSP book versions and allocates the allocation basis (606-10-32-28 to 32-41): VC carve-outs, the discount exception, the residual approach, relative SSP and largest remainder, in that order (REQ-ALC-007). Package `erev_engine/stages/s05_allocation/` (`ssp_books.py`, `points.py`, `conversion.py`, `pipeline.py`, `targeted.py`, `discount.py`, `residual.py`, `relative.py`, `snapshot.py`). Stages 03, 06 and 08 call the entries `resolve_ssp` and `allocate`.

| Direction | Content |
|---|---|
| Inputs | `PricedState` (obligations, `TpBuildUp.allocation_basis`, VC elements with targets); `SspVersionInput`; `RuleSetInput` of kind `SSP_ASSIGNMENT`; `FxRateInput` spot rates; bundle products; judgements `OTHER`; policies of Table 0.10-A row 05 |
| Outputs | `AllocatedState`: per obligation `SspResolution`, `original_*` values, first `AllocationSegment` (component `FIXED`, cause `INCEPTION`), targeted-VC segments; `proposals` of kind `DISCOUNT_EXCEPTION`; findings |

```python
class SspResolution:                            # snapshot feeding T-CON-11 ssp_* and original_ssp_* columns
    book_code: str; version_key: str; version_label: str | None; entry_key: str; method: str   # E-47
    rate_key: str | None                        # POL-079 conversion rate, never re-converted (32-43)
    unit_list_price: Fraction | None; midpoint_discount_ratio: Fraction | None; range_ratio: Fraction | None
    low: Fraction | None; mid: Fraction | None; high: Fraction | None                        # extended (× Q)
    selected: Fraction                          # original_ssp_selected
    in_range: bool | None; point_policy: str    # POL-071 or POL-072 literal applied, or "POINT"
    residual_candidate: bool
```

### 5.2 Pseudocode

```python
def resolve_ssp(ctx, draft, at: date, price: Fraction | None, tb=None, findings=None) -> SspResolution:
    book = ssp_books.select_book(ctx, draft, at)                        # S05-R-02
    version = ssp_books.select_version(ctx, book, draft, at)            # S05-R-03 (POL-070)
    entry = ssp_books.select_entry(version, draft, ctx.txn_currency)    # S05-R-04
    entry, rate_key = conversion.to_txn_currency(ctx, entry, at)        # S05-R-05 (POL-079)
    lo, mid, hi, point = points.extend(entry, draft)                    # S05-R-06: values × quantity
    return points.select(ctx, entry, draft, price, lo, mid, hi, point)  # S05-R-07 (POL-071, POL-072)

def run(ctx: BookContext, st: PricedState, tb: TraceBuilder) -> AllocatedState:
    findings = []
    obs = [with_ssp(ob, resolve_ssp(ctx, ob, ob.pricing_date, ob.stated_price, tb, findings)) for ob in st.obligations]
    obs = merge_immaterial(ctx, obs)                                    # S03-R-13
    quotas = allocate(ctx, st.tp.allocation_basis.posted, obs, st.tp.elements, tb, findings)   # S05-R-09 to S05-R-15
    for ob in obs:
        seg = AllocationSegment(component="FIXED", effective_date=st.inception_date, event_key=None,
                                cause="INCEPTION", basis=initial_basis(st, ob),       # S02-R-04
                                x_exact=quotas[ob].x_exact, a_posted=quotas[ob].a_posted,
                                base_revenue_posted=0, base_revenue_exact=Fraction(0),
                                base_progress=ProgressBase.zero(), totals=ProgressTotals.of(ob),
                                progress_measure=ob.recognition_method, unit_ssp=ob.ssp.selected / ob.quantity,
                                remaining_ssp=ob.ssp.selected, remaining_billing_plan=ob.stated_price,
                                estimate_pair=(None, None), modification_boundary_no=0)
        snapshot.original_columns(ctx, ob, seg, st.tp, tb)                 # S05-R-16
    return AllocatedState(obligations=..., findings=tuple(findings), ...)
```

### 5.3 SSP resolution rules

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S05-R-01 | **Pricing date.** Booking lines are priced at the group inception date (606-10-32-31); lines added by a modification at its effective date (D-18; stage 06); under POL-217 (acquired contracts) at the acquisition date (§7). SSP changes after the pricing date never re-price an obligation (606-10-32-43) |
| S05-R-02 | **Book selection.** (1) The `SSP_ASSIGNMENT` rule set version effective at the pricing date, evaluated like S03-R-02, outputs `ssp_book_code`. (2) Otherwise the book whose non-null scope members (entity, currency, channel, segment) all equal the line's facts and whose count of non-null scope members is greatest, ties by ascending book code. (3) Under the parity preset, book `LEGACY-SKU-SSP`. (4) Otherwise `SSP_KEY_NOT_FOUND`. **A recorded version names its book (rev 1.121; item PIN-READBACK-1).** A pricing for which the bundle holds a recorded version (S05-R-03) takes the book that holds that version; steps (1) to (3) are not evaluated again for it, so a rule set version, a narrower book or a book of equal scope that came later does not move a line already priced. A recorded version the bundle does not carry → `SSP_KEY_NOT_FOUND` |
| S05-R-03 | **Version selection (POL-070; V7).** `LATEST_APPROVED_EFFECTIVE_AT_INCEPTION`: the version of the book with `effective_from_date ≤ pricing date ≤ effective_to_date` (open end = unbounded) and the greatest `version_no` among versions approved at or before `known_at`. `NAMED_VERSION`, or an approved per-obligation override (POL-070 level O; POL-080 level O for modifications): the version whose `legacy_version_label` (book `BY_LABEL`) or `version_key` equals the named value. No version → `SSP_KEY_NOT_FOUND` (`ERROR`) naming product, stratification and label. **Recorded versions (rev 1.121; item PIN-READBACK-1; ASC 606-10-32-43, IFRS 15.88).** The orchestrator hands back the versions an earlier computation priced an obligation from, as POL-070 at `OBLIGATION` scope with the read-back value `{recorded: <version key>, recorded@<event key>: <version key>, …}` (05 RCP-15). A pricing names the record it reads. The obligation's own pricing — stage 05 at the booking, the stage 03 reads of the same line at its pricing date (the bundle weights of S03-R-03 and the option, shipping and custodial reads) and the pricing of an obligation in the event that adds it (S06-R-11, class D new) — reads `recorded`; the weight of an existing obligation in a modification reads `recorded@<key of that event>` (S06-R-11). The version of the recorded key is selected whatever its dates and whatever was approved since, and it names its book (S05-R-02). A pricing without a record — a first pricing, the weights of a modification computed for the first time (01-DECISIONS D-18), the correction of S06-R-26, the modification SSP of a legacy template (S06-R-28) — passes a read-back value over and resolves POL-070 at `CONTRACT`, `ENTITY` and `GROUP` scope as if the row were absent. An approved override replaces the obligation's row for the pricing it governs and therefore wins. A bundle without a read-back row computes as before: no canonical member is added (`ResolvedPolicyInput.value` already admits an object), and the output of every bundle of the corpus is unchanged |
| S05-R-04 | **Entry selection.** Among the version's entries with `product_code` = the line's product and `stratification` = the line's stratification (or `""`): keep entries whose non-null dimensions (`region`, `channel`, `segment`, `deal_size_band`, `term_band`) equal the contract or line facts; choose the greatest count of non-null dimensions, then the entry in the transaction currency, then ascending `entry_key`. None → `SSP_KEY_NOT_FOUND`. E-47 `formula` never resolves (1.0) |
| S05-R-05 | **Currency (POL-079).** An entry in another currency, when none exists in the transaction currency: `CONVERT_AT_INCEPTION_SPOT` → values × the `spot` rate at the pricing date (base = entry currency, quote = transaction currency; an inverse rate is used as 1 ÷ rate when only the inverse exists); the rate key is recorded and never re-converted; no rate → `FX_RATE_MISSING` (`ERROR`). `CURRENCY_SPECIFIC_BOOK_REQUIRED` → `SSP_KEY_NOT_FOUND` |
| S05-R-06 | **Extended values.** With quantity Q and the band row selected by `band_dimension` (`NONE`; `QUANTITY` by Q; `DEAL_SIZE` by `allocation_basis`; `TERM_MONTHS` by the line's term in whole months; `band_from ≤ value < band_to`): `AMOUNT` basis → point = Q × `point_value`; low, mid, high = Q × row values. `PERCENT_OF_LIST` → the same times `unit_list_price`. `legacy_range` → mid = Q × L × (1 − d), low = mid × (1 − r), high = mid × (1 + r) (legacy 01 §3.3; LM-SSP-06, 07). `cost_plus_margin` without a band → point = Q × `cost_basis` × (1 + `margin_ratio`). `residual` → no value; the obligation is a residual candidate (S05-R-12). A call with `price = None` (bundle split weights, S03-R-03) returns the point or the midpoint |
| S05-R-07 | **SSP point for a stated price P (POLICIES §3.4; POL-071, POL-072).** Point entries: SSP = point whatever P. Range entries: when low ≤ P ≤ high (inclusive), `CONTRACT_PRICE` → P, `MIDPOINT` → mid; when P < low or P > high: `NEAREST_BOUND` → the nearer bound (low for P < low, high for P > high); `MIDPOINT` → mid; `LOW_POINT` → low; `HIGH_POINT` → high; `OBSERVABLE_POINT` → Q × `observable_point` (T-REF-30 column, 04 rev 1.2; OQ-A-10 resolved by D-76), or the nearer bound with `OBSERVABLE_POINT_MISSING` (`WARNING`) when the column is NULL. P = 0 is below low, so a free obligation still receives an allocation (legacy OK-03). `in_range` = (low ≤ P ≤ high) |
| S05-R-08 | **Bypasses.** Option obligations under `ENTERED_AMOUNT` and `DISCOUNT_X_LIKELIHOOD` take SSP_opt from stage 03; `VC_LINE` obligations take SSP 0; neither is range-tested (POLICIES §3.4) |

### 5.4 Allocation rules

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S05-R-09 | **Order (REQ-ALC-007).** (1) targeted VC (S05-R-14), (2) discount exception (S05-R-11), (3) residual (S05-R-12), (4) relative SSP (S05-R-10), (5) largest remainder at the minor unit for every posted amount (CV-34, CV-36). Every obligation of every member contract is in scope, whatever its entity or upload batch (POL-214; REQ-ALC-001). `LEASE_842` targets take part (PT-09) |
| S05-R-10 | **Relative SSP.** T = `allocation_basis.posted` less targeted VC; weights w_p = selected SSP; keys = obligation subject keys. Σw = 0 → `TOTAL_SSP_ZERO` (`ERROR`; POL-077). **Nothing to allocate (D-88 L7-5-Q-10 (1); D-98 78; rev 1.22).** Stage 05 skips allocation whenever the member set has no obligation AND `allocation_basis.posted` = 0 — the general form of the D-88 guard, whose stated intent contrasted "T ≠ 0 with no obligation still raises" with "nothing is priced", not with routed-out lines; the routed-out clause described the D-88 case (every line excluded by S03-R-11), and a member whose contract is `VOIDED` (S01-R-13: `CONTRACT_VOIDED` removed every other event, so stage 03 yields no obligation and the basis is 0) is the second instance. Every target of a voided contract is 0 and stage 14's reversal path runs (S14-R-08). The skip is never keyed on routed-out lines nor on the void status; `TOTAL_SSP_ZERO` remains the refusal whenever allocation is reached with Σw = 0 — a non-voided member with no SSP, and any member set with no obligation but T ≠ 0 (explicit test). `a_p = largest_remainder(T, w, keys)[p]`; `x_p = (T ÷ 10^μ) × w_p ÷ Σw` (CV-34). A negative T is apportioned symmetrically (PROP:P3) |
| S05-R-11 | **Discount exception (POL-076; 606-10-32-36 to 32-38).** Under `PROPOSE_WITH_APPROVAL`, for each bundle product b whose component products are all products of the contract's obligations: (a) every component obligation's SSP entry has method `observable`; (b) b has an `observable` SSP entry (the regularly sold bundle price S_b); (c) \|(1 − S_b ÷ Σ component SSP) − (1 − T ÷ Σ all SSP)\| ≤ `bundle_discount_tolerance_pp`. A bundle meeting (a) to (c) is returned as a `DISCOUNT_EXCEPTION` proposal. It is applied only when a reviewed `OTHER` judgement for the contract has outcome `discount_exception_bundle` = b: obligations outside the bundle receive their SSPs; the bundle's obligations share T − Σ SSP of the others by relative SSP within the bundle. `DISABLED` (parity) → never proposed |
| S05-R-12 | **Residual approach (POL-075; 606-10-32-34(c), 32-35, 32-38).** A residual candidate is allocated R = T − Σ SSP of the other obligations, where obligations under an applied discount exception count at their discounted allocation. Eligibility: every other obligation has a non-residual SSP; the discount exception, if any, is applied first; several candidates share one residual R, split by an approved non-residual SSP of each (otherwise ineligible). Checks: R > 0; when the candidate's entry has a range, low ≤ R ≤ high. A failure raises `RESIDUAL_REJECTED` (`ERROR`) under both `REQUIRE_ESTIMATED_SSP` and `BLOCK`; under `REQUIRE_ESTIMATED_SSP` the preparer's approved per-obligation SSP override to an `adjusted_market` or `cost_plus_margin` entry makes the obligation a normal S05-R-10 obligation on the next computation (FASB Example 34 Case C) |
| S05-R-13 | **Posting exceptions paths.** When S05-R-11 or S05-R-12 fixes exact amounts x_p that sum exactly to T, the posted amounts are `largest_remainder(T, [x_p], keys)`, so CV-34 holds on every path |
| S05-R-14 | **Targeted VC (POL-044; 606-10-32-39 to 32-41).** An element with `allocation_target = OBLIGATIONS` and a present and non-empty `allocation_criteria_evidence` (the 32-40(a) and (b) attestation; `None` or an empty string is unevidenced, the shared S05/S08 predicate, D-91) allocates its constrained amount K only to its target obligations, by relative SSP within the targets. `INCREMENTS` elements are realised in the increment in which they are earned (component `PERIOD_VC`) and are not allocated at inception. Test: r_p = allocation_p ÷ SSP_p for each target and r_c = basis ÷ ΣSSP; if \|r_p − r_c\| > tolerance × r_c, `VC_TARGET_TOLERANCE_EXCEEDED` (`ERROR`; 04 table 15.4-C, rev 1.2; OQ-A-12 resolved by D-76) unless a reviewed `OTHER` outcome `pol_044_override = true` names the element; in both cases trace params record `tolerance_exceeded = true`. `NOT_ENFORCED` (parity) skips the test. Each targeted element produces its own segments on the targets with component `FIXED`, and stage 05 records `AllocatedState.targeted_vc_quotas[estimate key][obligation subject key]` = the element's `Quota` on that obligation and seeds `targeted_vc_quota_history[estimate key][obligation subject key]` = ((inception date, `Quota`),); stage 08 appends (effective date, the quota after the change) after every reallocating version of an evidenced `OBLIGATIONS` element whose segments were added (S08-R-06) and updates the latest quota, so that RPO exemption POL-200 can exclude exactly the targeted share in force at any measured date (ENGINE_SPEC_B S15-R-09; OQ-B-21 resolved by D-76; rev 1.6, D-91) |
| S05-R-15 | **Negative allocation.** A targeted or changed amount that makes an obligation's total allocation negative raises `VC_ALLOCATION_NEGATIVE` (`ERROR`; 606-10-32-44; DEV-040) |
| S05-R-16 | **Original columns (REQ-ALC-008, -009; LM-CL-23 to 31).** `original_ssp_mid`, `original_ssp_high`, `original_ssp_low` = extended values; `original_ssp_selected` = selected SSP; `original_ssp_in_range`; `original_total_contract_price` = `total.posted` of the inception build-up; `original_total_contract_ssp` = Σ selected SSP; `original_allocated_exact` = x_p; `original_allocated_amount` = a_p; `original_unit_ssp` = SSP ÷ Q; `original_unit_revenue_rate` = x_p ÷ Q; `allocation_weight` = w_p ÷ Σw; `allocation_adjustment` = a_p − stated price (Σ over the contract = 0 when no VC or expected return exists). A revenue account on the SSP entry is snapshotted as the obligation's `REVENUE` override (REQ-SSP-014) |
| S05-R-17 | **Parity preset.** POL-070 `NAMED_VERSION`, POL-071 `CONTRACT_PRICE`, POL-072 `NEAREST_BOUND`, POL-073 `NOT_ENFORCED`, POL-076 `DISABLED`, POL-044 `NOT_ENFORCED` reproduce legacy L682 to L717 exactly in rationals: SSP = stated price clamped into [low, high]; allocation = SSP ÷ contract SSP × contract price; VC rows SSP 0 and allocation 0, their negative consideration spread by relative SSP through `vc_constrained` (REQ-TP-016; DEV-073, DEV-077). Posted amounts differ from legacy report rounding only within DEV-002 |
