## 6. Stage 06: modifications

### 6.1 Scope, inputs and outputs

Stage 06 classifies and applies contract modifications (606-10-25-10 to 25-13, 32-42 to 32-45): separate contract, prospective, cumulative catch-up and mixed routes with the D-18 SSP basis; price changes on satisfied performance; POB-specific VC changes; subscription changes; terminations; unpriced change orders and claims; material-right exercise; the legacy templates; and catch-up measurement. Package `erev_engine/stages/s06_modifications/` (`classify.py`, `price_test.py`, `satisfied.py`, `weights.py`, `pools.py`, `segments.py`, `catch_up.py`, `subscriptions.py`, `terminations.py`, `exercise.py`, `parity.py`, `attributes.py`, `quantities.py`).

| Direction | Content |
|---|---|
| Inputs | `AllocatedState` before the event; the boundary event (`CONTRACT_AMENDED` payload `modification_id`, `treatments`, `lines`, `ssp_basis`; `CONTRACT_TERMINATED` payload `termination_kind`, `obligation_keys`, `refund_amount`; `MATERIAL_RIGHT_EXERCISED` payload `obligation_key`, `exercised_quantity`, `additional_consideration`, `new_lines`; `REGROUPED`; `LINE_ATTRIBUTES_CHANGED`); `ModificationInput` (T-CON-06: `kind`, `template_mode`, `questionnaire`, `lines`, `price_change_amount`); `QuantityLedger` at the boundary; VC pins at the effective date; `resolve_ssp` and `price_at`; policies of Table 0.10-A row 06 |
| Outputs | `AllocatedState` after the event: new segments (causes `MODIFICATION`, `TERMINATION`, `MATERIAL_RIGHT_EXERCISE`), added obligations, `lineage_pre_modification`, `terminated_on`, status updates, refund-liability components `TERMINATION` and `CONCESSION` (ENGINE_SPEC_B §10.2.4), catch-up nodes, `proposals` of kind `MODIFICATION_TREATMENT`, findings |

State before the event, per obligation p at effective date d (ENG-06 position before the event; CV-63): segment k in force with X_p = x_k and A_p = a_k; posted cumulative revenue R_p = C_before(d); exact E_p = E_before(d); progress f_p(d); contracted quantity Q_p and net delivered q_p; inception extended SSP SSP0_p and u0_p = SSP0_p ÷ Q_p; original stated unit price ū_p = original stated price ÷ original quantity; remaining SSP, SSP delivered, billing plan and billed amount from the segment and the ledger.

### 6.2 Proposal (REQ-MOD-002; ALG-04 §2.5.2; POL-100, POL-101)

```python
def propose(ctx, st: AllocatedState, mod: ModificationView, tb) -> Proposal:
    d = mod.effective_date
    if ctx.policies.value("mod.route_selection") == "USER_SELECTED_TEMPLATE" and mod.template_mode:
        t = {"prospective": "LEGACY_PROSPECTIVE", "retrospective": "LEGACY_RETROSPECTIVE",
             "pob_price_change": "LEGACY_POB_VC"}[mod.template_mode]
        return Proposal(treatments={ob: t for ob in contract_obligations(st, mod.contract_key)}, summary=t)
    cls = classify.obligations(ctx, st, mod, d)                    # S06-R-05: S | D | N per obligation, new lines
    if classify.separate_contract(ctx, st, mod, d, cls):           # S06-R-04
        return Proposal(treatments={line.obligation_key: "SEPARATE_CONTRACT" for line in mod.lines}, summary="SEPARATE_CONTRACT")
    treatments = {p: {"D": "PROSPECTIVE", "N": "CUMULATIVE_CATCH_UP"}[c] for p, c in cls.items() if c != "S"}
    kinds = set(treatments.values())
    summary = kinds.pop() if len(kinds) == 1 else "MIXED"
    return Proposal(treatments=treatments, summary=summary, classification=cls)
```

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S06-R-01 | **Proposal and choice.** The engine proposes per obligation (E-23 literals) and returns the proposal in `proposals` (CV-16). `CONTRACT_AMENDED.treatments` carries the approved chosen treatments; the engine applies the chosen treatments, and a departure from the proposal requires a reviewed judgement record of topic `MODIFICATION_TREATMENT_OVERRIDE` before approval (REQ-MOD-002; platform). Rev 1.32 (D-98 140 AMENDMENT 16): the `MODIFICATION_TREATMENT` proposal's `detail` carries `modification_key`, `class[<key>]` (S / D / N per obligation) and, for every distinct added line whose S06-R-04 price test resolved an SSP entry, `ssp_version[<key>]` = the resolved `<book>@v<n>` (`SspValues.version_key`, the value the `mod_price_test@…` node traces as `version_key`) — an additive output-detail member the platform reads for the T-CON-06 classification default (04 rev 1.78); no figure, rule or allocation change |
| S06-R-02 | **Impact preview.** The platform previews a modification by running `compute` in mode `DRY_RUN` with the candidate `CONTRACT_AMENDED` appended (ENG-12; REQ-MOD-013) |
| S06-R-03 | **Separate contract is not applied here.** A chosen `SEPARATE_CONTRACT` treatment makes the platform book a new contract (own group, `origin_modification_id`) and append nothing to the original stream; a `CONTRACT_AMENDED` event carrying `SEPARATE_CONTRACT` is a programming error (CV-45) |
| S06-R-04 | **25-12 test.** Separate contract when every line has `action = ADD` for goods that are distinct (questionnaire `added_goods_distinct`, default: the resolved template distinctness is not `nondistinct`), no existing obligation is changed, and every line passes POL-101 `WITHIN_MOD_DATE_RANGE`: `resolve_ssp(at = d, quantity ΔQ_m, price ΔC_m)` gives a range with low ≤ ΔC_m ≤ high, or a point with \|ΔC_m − point\| ≤ `point_tolerance_pct` × point, or questionnaire `priced_at_ssp = true` (the preparer's approved attestation of an SSP adjusted for the circumstances of the contract) [F, 606-10-25-12; FASB Example 5 Case A] |
| S06-R-05 | **Classes S, D, N.** For each existing obligation after the modification: **S** when f_p(d) = 1 and no line changes its quantity (a quantity line on S is `MOD_QTY_ON_SATISFIED_POB`, `ERROR`); **D** when its remaining goods are distinct from those transferred: questionnaire `remaining_goods_distinct_from_transferred`, default true for f_p(d) = 0, for `series` obligations, and for `distinct` obligations measured by units; **N** otherwise (a partially satisfied single obligation whose remaining goods are not distinct). A new line is class D when distinct, else it joins the N obligation named by its integration target (S03-R-05). Route label: all D `PROSPECTIVE`; all N `CUMULATIVE_CATCH_UP`; otherwise `MIXED` |

### 6.3 Application of a native modification (ALG-04 steps 2 to 7)

```python
def apply_native(ctx, st, ev, mod, tb) -> AllocatedState:
    d = ev.effective_date
    pre = state_before(st, ev)                                          # §6.1 values per obligation
    lines = [with_ssp(ctx, l, at=d, basis=mod.ssp_basis.get(l.obligation_key)) for l in mod.lines]   # S06-R-06
    new_obs = s03_pob_builder.build_lines(ctx, st, [l for l in lines if l.action == "ADD"], at=d, tb=tb)
    quantities.validate(ctx, pre, lines)                                # S06-R-08
    sat = satisfied.apply(ctx, pre, mod, lines, tb)                     # S06-R-09, S06-R-10
    w = weights.compute(ctx, pre, lines, new_obs, d)                    # S06-R-11 (POL-080, POL-081)
    pool = pools.build(ctx, pre, lines, sat, st.price_at(ctx, st, d, ev), mod)   # S06-R-12 (POL-103)
    quotas = pools.apportion(ctx, pool, w, pre)                         # S06-R-13
    segs = segments.after_boundary(ctx, pre, quotas, mod.treatments, d, ev)      # S06-R-14 to S06-R-16
    cu = catch_up.measure(ctx, pre, segs, ev, tb)                       # S06-R-17
    return st.with_segments(segs).with_obligations(new_obs).with_lineage(pre, mod).with_findings(...)
```

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S06-R-06 | **SSP basis (D-18; POL-080).** `D18_DEFAULT`: lines and remaining goods of class D are priced with `resolve_ssp(at = d)` from the version effective at d, or the approved per-obligation override named in `ssp_basis` (with justification); class N and satisfied obligations keep inception SSPs (606-10-32-43), and goods added to an N obligation use the d SSP for their increment. `INCEPTION_ALL`: every existing obligation keeps its inception SSP. `LEGACY_CARRIED_PLUS_FILE_VERSION` (parity): §6.5 |
| S06-R-07 | **Lines.** Each line has target obligation key, signed ΔQ_m, signed ΔC_m (`consideration_delta`), new start and end dates, SSP basis. `price_change_amount` is the amount attributed to satisfied performance, with the contract-level questionnaire member `price_change_settlement` ∈ {`FUTURE_PRICING`, `CREDIT_OR_REFUND`} (default `FUTURE_PRICING`; T-CON-06 and the `CONTRACT_AMENDED` payload, 04 rev 1.2; OQ-A-18 resolved by D-76): `FUTURE_PRICING` settles through JET-05b, `CREDIT_OR_REFUND` through JET-05c. Attribute members that differ from stored values are not applied (REQ-MOD-021; the import raises `MOD_ATTRIBUTE_CONFLICT`, `ERROR`, which blocks the upload; OQ-A-20 resolved by D-76) |
| S06-R-08 | **Quantity checks.** RQ_p = Q_p − q_p + ΔQ_p; RQ_p < 0, or a non-VC remaining billing plan below 0 under the parity preset, raises `MOD_REMAINING_NEGATIVE` (`ERROR`; DEV-041). After the fold, every measure event must leave net delivered units ≤ the quantity of the segment in force, else `PROGRESS_OVER_DELIVERY` (`ERROR`). **Unit history (D-90b).** An eligible obligation is one that is not a VC line and not `series`, whose `FIXED` segment in force measures `TIME_ELAPSED` with a cause other than `TERMINATION`, whatever its class D or N treatment. When the service of an eligible obligation has started (d > s_p, with [s_p, e_p] the term in force of S06-R-11), `MOD_UNIT_HISTORY_AMBIGUOUS` (`ERROR`) is raised when (a) the lines of one modification on that obligation carry both a negative and a positive `quantity_delta`, or (b) a line adding units to it carries a start date other than that of the term in force (rev 1.5; D-90c). Modifications effective on the same date are applied in event order and each is a boundary of its own: a removal followed by an addition keeps the remaining booked units at their start and adds a layer at d; an addition followed by a removal reduces every layer pro rata (S06-R-11; D-90b). The finding is emitted by these checks over every pooled obligation before any weight (S06-R-11); it blocks the event and appends nothing, so no `mod_weight` node exists for the event. The S06-R-11 unit layers are reconstructed from net quantity changes and cannot represent these histories. A mixed event before the service start (d ≤ s_p) reconstructs exactly and is accepted |
| S06-R-09 | **Satisfied performance (POL-104 `RECOGNISE_IN_PERIOD_ON_INCEPTION_BASIS`; 606-10-32-42 to 32-44).** ΔC_sat = `price_change_amount`. It is apportioned over the class S obligations (and the delivered portions of D obligations the line names) by `largest_remainder(ΔC_sat, weights = original posted allocations, keys)`. Each receiving obligation gets a segment with basis `INCEPTION`, x′ = X_p + share exact, a′ = A_p + share; with f = 1 its catch-up equals the share (JET-05b under `FUTURE_PRICING`; JET-05c and a `CONCESSION` refund component under `CREDIT_OR_REFUND`, POL-243). When no obligation remains unsatisfied, every untagged ΔC_m is also satisfied performance (DEV-054). `POOL_WITH_REMAINING` (parity) puts ΔC_sat into the pool |
| S06-R-10 | **Concessions targeted at one obligation (POL-243).** `TARGETED_WHEN_32_40_ATTESTED` with questionnaire `pol_243_attested = true`: the whole ΔC_sat goes to the named obligation; `INCEPTION_BASIS`: S06-R-09 apportionment (CHK-113) |
| S06-R-11 | **Weights (ALG-04 §2.5.4; POL-081).** All ≥ 0 (`NEGATIVE_WEIGHT` otherwise). `D18_DEFAULT`: class D existing → ρ_p × `resolve_ssp(at = d, quantity = RQ⁰_p, price = RQ⁰_p × ū_p).selected` with RQ⁰_p = remaining existing units after removals (`CARRIED_UNIT_SSP`: removed units simply reduce RQ⁰_p), plus Σ over added-unit lines `resolve_ssp(at = d, quantity = ΔQ_m, price = ΔC_m).selected`, not scaled; class D new → `resolve_ssp(at = d, ΔQ_m, ΔC_m).selected`; class D series → the d SSP of the remaining increments, priced from the SSP entry's declared basis (D-93 (4); 04 E-49 `ssp_value_basis`: `PER_INCREMENT` × the remaining increments in [d, e_p] × the remaining quantity as T-REF-30 `quantity_unit` declares it — `SERVICE_UNITS`: each unit spans the term, so value × the remaining increments (the term's increments under the pinned convention × ρ, entering once) × quantity; `INCREMENTS`: the quantity counts increments over the full term, so value × ρ × quantity — the supported meaning is the declared one: a `PER_INCREMENT` entry without `quantity_unit` fails closed, CV-45, a `quantity_unit` with any other basis is refused, the unit is never inferred from a quantity, and all dated entries of one product in one SSP book agree (checked at bundle assembly and again when a dated entry is selected) (D-97 (3)); `PER_BOOKED_TERM` ÷ the increments of the term the entry prices × the remaining increments; an entry annotated as pricing the remaining increments at d is taken as is; a series entry without a declared basis fails closed, CV-45, once the corpus entries are annotated; increments are counted as the pinned E-21 convention counts the term's periods — MONTHLY_EVEN whole periods plus day-prorated partial periods, MID_MONTH the counted periods, DAILY the days — as in the D-90b paragraph below); class N → (1 − f_p(d)) × SSP0_p + Σ added goods `resolve_ssp(at = d, ΔQ_m, ΔC_m).selected` − removed goods × u0_p. `INCEPTION_ALL`: class D existing → ρ_p × RQ⁰_p × u0_p plus added units at d SSP, not scaled; series → inception SSP of the remaining increments, priced from the entry's basis in the same way (D-93 (4)); N as `D18_DEFAULT`. **Remaining service of an existing time-elapsed obligation (D-90b).** ρ_p = 1, so the weight is unchanged, for series (the d SSP of the remaining increments, from the entry's declared basis, D-93 (4)), obligations measured by units, cost or ratio, class N, added obligations and terminations. For a class D existing obligation that is not a VC line and not `series`, whose `FIXED` segment in force before the event measures `TIME_ELAPSED` with a cause other than `TERMINATION`, ρ_p = Σ_L q_L × (1 − f_L(d − 1)) ÷ Σ_L q_L, exact. [s_p, e_p] is the term in force of that segment, its start clamped by `recognition_start_date`. f_L is ALG-11 progress under the pinned E-21 convention (ENGINE_SPEC_B S09-R-08; else POL-090), measured as on the `INCEPTION` basis at the close of d − 1 over [s_L, e_p]. Unit layers L: the booked units start at s_p; units added at an earlier boundary k start at max(d_k, s_p); an earlier decrease reduces every layer pro rata; removals at d reduce RQ⁰_p before scaling. The layers are reconstructed from the `FIXED` segment history of `totals.quantity`, which carries net changes only; histories that net reconstruction cannot represent raise `MOD_UNIT_HISTORY_AMBIGUOUS` (S06-R-08). ρ_p is never read from the progress g of a `PROSPECTIVE` segment and is never compounded across boundaries. Inside a period under `MONTHLY_EVEN` or `MID_MONTH` the current period counts as remaining (1 − f(d − 1)), so a modification priced at SSP moves no consideration between obligations. For such an obligation ΔQ_m of an added-unit line counts units over [d, e_p], and the SSP entry prices that unit over [d, e_p]. Fail closed: `ENGINE_INVARIANT_VIOLATED` when the unit layers do not reconcile with the segment history, or when ρ_p = 0 for a class D obligation. A `FIXED` boundary that changes `totals.quantity` without a modification or a material-right exercise also raises `ENGINE_INVARIANT_VIOLATED`, except `OPENING_BALANCE`, which resets the layers at the opening start (D-90b: this invariant takes effect once prototyped and measured over the corpus, else it is recorded post-rc). Trace: `mod.weights.d18.v1` and `mod.weights.inception_all.v1` keep their ids with additive params `measure`, `convention`, `term_start`, `term_end`, `progress_as_of`, the layers (`layer_quantities`, `layer_starts`, `layer_progress`) and `remaining_scale`; the params tie Σ layer quantities to the remaining quantity plus removed units; a node without `measure` evaluates as before, and reevaluate(trace) stays exact. Worked example (MOD-JS-06): L1-STORE1, 40,000.00 over 1 Jul 2026 to 30 Jun 2036 (`MONTHLY_EVEN`), d = 1 Oct 2026: f_L1(30 Sep 2026) = 3 ÷ 120 = 1 ÷ 40, ρ = 39 ÷ 40, weight 39,000.00; L2-STORE2 unstarted (from 1 Jul 2027), ρ = 1, weight 40,000.00; Pool 109,000.00; shares 53,810.13 / 55,189.87. **Recorded weights (rev 1.121; item PIN-READBACK-1).** The version a weight is priced from is selected at d when the modification is computed for the first time (01-DECISIONS D-18) and is then fixed: at a later computation of the same event the `resolve_ssp` calls of a class D existing obligation, and of the added-unit lines on a class D or class N obligation, read the version recorded for that obligation and event (S05-R-03 `recorded@<event key>`), so an SSP book version approved after the modification was applied does not weigh it a second time. A class D new obligation reads its own record (`recorded`). An approved `ssp_basis` override names its version itself and wins. The orchestrator records the versions from the `ssp_entry` sources of the event's `mod_weight@<event key>` nodes, members `remaining` and `added:<n>` (04 T-CON-07 `pinned_refs.ssp_weights`); a weight priced from entries of more than one version has no record. The record is machine state of the computation and never an authored member: it is no part of `ssp_basis`, which holds what the author and the approver of the modification decided. **An approved override is read by version key (item MOD-SSP-OVERRIDE-PREVIEW-1).** For an `ssp_basis` entry with `is_override` true the engine reads `ssp_version_key`; the orchestrator sets it beside the stored `ssp_book_version_id` of the `CONTRACT_AMENDED` payload when the id names an approved version, as the `ModificationInput` projection does. An override without it is a bundle-assembly error (`ValueError`), which the platform refuses by name before the dry run (04 T-CON-06) |
| S06-R-12 | **Pool (ALG-04 §2.5.5; POL-103).** `REMAINING_TP`: Pool = Σ_{D, N existing} (A_p − R_p) + Σ ΔC_m − ΔC_sat + ΔVC, in minor units, where ΔVC is the change of constrained VC caused by versions effective at d that the modification makes includable (for example `UNPRICED_CHANGE_ORDER`, PT-05). `ATTRIBUTE_BY_LINE`: per obligation Pool_p = (A_p − R_p) + Σ ΔC_m of its lines, no apportionment. `TOTAL_TP`: TP′ = TP + Σ ΔC_m allocated over every obligation, class S included, with weights = delivered SSP + remaining SSP (the parity retrospective template, §6.5). If Σw = 0 while Pool ≠ 0, the pool is satisfied performance (S06-R-09) |
| S06-R-13 | **Apportionment.** `REMAINING_TP`: s_p = `largest_remainder(Pool, w, keys)[p]`; existing obligations A′_p = R_p + s_p and X′_p = R_p + (Pool ÷ 10^μ) × w_p ÷ Σw; new obligations A′_p = s_p and X′_p = (Pool ÷ 10^μ) × w_p ÷ Σw. `TOTAL_TP`: A′ = `largest_remainder(TP′, w, keys)`, X′ per CV-34 |
| S06-R-14 | **Segments of class D (25-13(a)).** Basis `PROSPECTIVE`; `x_exact` X′_p; `a_posted` A′_p; `base_revenue_posted` R_p; `base_revenue_exact` R_p as a Fraction (native; CV-62); `base_progress` = ledger quantities at the boundary; `totals` = RQ_p, updated start and end dates, EAC remaining; `modification_boundary_no` + 1 (REQ-MOD-020). E after the boundary starts at R_p, so there is no catch-up (ENGINE_SPEC_B S09-R-05) |
| S06-R-15 | **Segments of class N (25-13(b)) under POL-102 `PARTIALLY_SATISFIED_NONDISTINCT_ONLY`.** Basis `INCEPTION`; `x_exact` X′_p; `a_posted` A′_p; `totals` updated: quantity Q_p + ΔQ_p, the `EAC` pin effective at d — including a same-date `ESTIMATE_CHANGED` version recorded before the `CONTRACT_AMENDED` that the amendment's lines change or this re-measurement reads (D-92 (1)/(1a); it applies AT the boundary, so post-change recognition uses it, D18: EAC 75,000, f = 0.4 going forward) —, updated term; f′_p(d) over updated totals (units q_p ÷ (Q_p + ΔQ_p); cost costs to date ÷ updated EAC; time elapsed ÷ updated term). `ALL_POBS_FULL_REALLOCATION`: every obligation takes an `INCEPTION` segment with the POL-107 progress basis |
| S06-R-16 | **Record and lineage (POL-106).** Every obligation of the contract receives a version (REQ-MOD-022). After a 25-13(a) boundary, `lineage_pre_modification` of each post-modification obligation lists the obligations identified before the modification, which stage 08 uses to route later changes of VC promised before the modification (606-10-32-45; FASB Example 6) |
| S06-R-17 | **Catch-up measurement.** For each obligation with a new segment: CU_exact = E_after(d) − E_before(d); CU_posted = C_after(d) − C_before(d), with C and E per CV-63 at the same ENG-06 position. C_before(d), E_before(d), the pool and the S06-R-11 weights are measured WITHOUT a same-date `ESTIMATE_CHANGED` version that D-92 (1)/(1a) assigns to the modification (the estimates in force before it), C_after(d) and E_after(d) WITH it, so the combined effect carries the `MODIFICATION` cause and no separate `CATCH_UP` / `TP_CHANGE` arises on d (ENGINE_SPEC_B S09-R-04, S09-R-35; D18: EAC 60,000, f = 0.5, weights 62,500 : 30,000, shares 47,297.30 / 22,702.70, then f = 0.4 post-change). Node `catch_up@<event key>:<ob>:-` (formula `mod.catch_up.v1`, inputs the before and after nodes, params cause `MODIFICATION`). A zero progress divisor with E_p ≠ 0 raises `MOD_PROGRESS_UNDEFINED` (`ERROR`); with E_p = 0 the catch-up is 0 (DEV-059). Stage 09 sums these nodes into `catch_up_amount` and `catch_up_cum` (Table 0.9-A) |
| S06-R-18 | **Transaction price after the event.** `price_at(ctx, st, d, before = the next event)` must give `allocation_basis.posted` = Σ A′ over every obligation (class S keeping A_p); otherwise `ENGINE_INVARIANT_VIOLATED` (S06-INV-01) |

### 6.4 Subscription changes, terminations, change orders, exercise and attribute events

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S06-R-19 | **Subscription commands (REQ-MOD-012; E-25).** The platform builds the modification lines; stage 06 then proposes and applies as §6.2 and §6.3. `UPGRADE`: `CHANGE` line on the subscription obligation for the remaining term with ΔQ > 0 or ΔC > 0. `DOWNGRADE`: `CHANGE` line with ΔQ < 0 or ΔC < 0 for the remaining term (removed units at the carried unit SSP, POL-081). `CO_TERM`: `ADD` line starting on d and ending on the original end date. `RENEWAL`: `ADD` line starting on the day after the original end date for the renewal term. `EARLY_RENEWAL`: `ADD` renewal line as `RENEWAL`, effective d before the original end, with any price change of the current term as a `CHANGE` line. `CANCELLATION`: a termination (S06-R-21). Each receives the S06-R-04 test first; a renewal priced at its d SSP is proposed as a separate contract, and a licence renewal takes the POL-025 start guard (S03-R-10) |
| S06-R-20 | **Unpriced change orders and claims (POL-105, POL-244; PT-05; 606-10-25-10, 25-11).** An approved scope change without an agreed price is a modification whose lines carry ΔC = 0 and whose price enters as a VC element of type `UNPRICED_CHANGE_ORDER` (`MOST_LIKELY_AMOUNT`, constrained) effective at d, contributing ΔVC to the pool. A claim enters only with a reviewed `OTHER` outcome `claim_enforceable = true` (S04-R-04). Pricing the change later is an estimate change applied by stage 08 (ALG-10) |
| S06-R-21 | **Terminations (REQ-MOD-011; PT-03; POL-242).** `CONTRACT_TERMINATED` with `termination_kind = FULL` applies to every unsatisfied obligation of the contract, `PARTIAL` to `obligation_keys`. The approved modification carries `REMOVE` lines for the remaining quantity or term and ΔC = −(consideration forgone: amounts no longer due plus consideration refunded). Classes and pools follow §6.3 (a scope reduction is never a separate contract; DART 9.2.2.7). Each ended obligation receives a `TERMINATION` segment with basis `INCEPTION`, `x_exact` = E_before(d_T) + its pool share, `a_posted` = C_before(d_T) + its posted share, and totals that give f = 1 from d_T (ENGINE_SPEC_B S09-R-06); `terminated_on` = d_T; a non-zero pool with no remaining obligation is satisfied performance (S06-R-09), which recognises the remaining nonrefundable consideration. `refund_amount` creates a `TERMINATION` refund-liability component dated d_T (JET-04b; released by credit memos, ENGINE_SPEC_B §10.2.4). A `FULL` termination sets the contract status `TERMINATED` in every book (Table 2.2-A). Cost-asset acceleration is stage 11 (POL-145) |
| S06-R-22 | **Negative allocation.** A pool share or price change that makes an obligation's total allocation negative raises `VC_ALLOCATION_NEGATIVE` (`ERROR`) |
| S06-R-23 | **Material-right exercise, `CONTINUATION` (ALG-05 §2.6.2; POL-028).** On `MATERIAL_RIGHT_EXERCISED` at x for an option obligation o that is not `REDEMPTION_PATTERN`: M = A_o − R_o (minor units) and M_x = X_o − E_o. The option receives a `TERMINATION`-shaped segment (x′ = E_o(x), a′ = C_o(x), f = 1; cause `MATERIAL_RIGHT_EXERCISE`). `build_lines(new_lines, at = x)` creates the optioned goods G; their total T_G = M + C_add (C_add = `additional_consideration`) is apportioned over G by `largest_remainder(T_G, SSP at x, keys)` with quotas per CV-34; other obligations keep their segments and no catch-up arises. For an option recognised by redemption pattern, the event only records the redemption quantity for stage 09 (FASB Example 52) |
| S06-R-24 | **Material-right exercise, `MODIFICATION` (ALG-05 §2.6.3).** The exercise is a modification effective at x with two lines: `REMOVE` the option obligation (its remaining allocation joins the pool) and `ADD` G with ΔC = C_add. Classification and pools per §6.2 and §6.3; the route is never `SEPARATE_CONTRACT` because the consideration includes M. Under the parity preset the approved modification uses `LEGACY_PROSPECTIVE` with the dollar-quantity convention (POL-212; GT-15) |
| S06-R-25 | **Expiry.** `MATERIAL_RIGHT_EXPIRED` is not a boundary; stage 09 recognises the option's remaining allocation (JET-08) |
| S06-R-26 | **`LINE_ATTRIBUTES_CHANGED` (REQ-MOD-021).** Account overrides and performing entity apply to targets dated on or after the effective date and create no segment. A change of the SSP version pin corrects the inception SSP: the group is re-allocated from inception with the corrected resolution (stage 05) and the differences post as deltas (RCP-06) |
| S06-R-27 | **`REGROUPED` (REQ-CON-012).** Before any line of either contract has posted, the moved obligations are treated as booked in the target contract from its inception (bundle assembly moves them; no boundary). After posting, the paired events are a modification of both contracts: `REMOVE` lines on the `OUT` contract with ΔC = −(moved remaining consideration) and `ADD` lines on the `IN` contract at d with ΔC = +(moved remaining consideration), each applied by §6.3. Rev 1.148 (items REGROUP-BEFORE-POSTING-1 and REGROUP-AFTER-POSTING-AMOUNT-1; 04 §16.1 rev 1.234): in release 1.0 the platform takes the command only between two DRAFT contracts — the before-posting case, where nothing can post — and refuses it otherwise; the after-posting case of this rule is reached by no command until the road is rebuilt. The engine's reading of a `REGROUPED` pair is unchanged |

### 6.5 Legacy templates (POL-100 `USER_SELECTED_TEMPLATE`; ALG-04 §2.5.7; D-17)

The legacy templates run on exact state, cents only at posting (ALG-04 §2.5.7). For each obligation i of the contract before the event: E_i = E_before(d) (legacy `Rev Rec - Cumulative`); RemAlloc_i = X_i − E_i; RemQty_i = Q_i − net delivered (returns restore quantity under POL-053 `RESTORE_REMAINING_QUANTITY`); RemSSP_i = segment `remaining_ssp` − Σ delivered units since the boundary × segment `unit_ssp`; SSPDel_i = cumulative SSP delivered (legacy `SSP Delivered - Cumulative`); Plan_i = `remaining_billing_plan` − billed since the boundary.

```python
def mod_ssp(line) -> Fraction:                                  # legacy 03 §3.1, 04 §3.3; POL-081 CLAMPED_MOD_PRICE
    L, d_, r = file_version_entry(line)                         # the upload's SSP Version (POL-080 parity)
    mid = line.qty * L * (1 - d_); lo = mid * (1 - r); hi = mid * (1 + r)
    b = line.billing
    if line.qty > 0:
        return hi if b > hi else lo if b < lo else b
    return hi if b < hi else lo if b > lo else b                # qty <= 0: interval [hi, lo]; qty 0 gives 0

def legacy_prospective(st, lines):                              # legacy 03 §3.4 to §3.6
    for i in obligations: RemQty_i += ModQty_i; RemSSP_i = max(0, RemSSP_i + mod_ssp_i); Plan_i += ModBilling_i
    for i in obligations:
        if RemQty_i == 0: RemSSP_i = 0                          # DEV-055: stranded SSP joins the pool with weight 0
    Pool = sum(RemAlloc) + sum(ModBilling)
    W = sum(RemSSP)
    if W == 0: return satisfied_performance(Pool)               # DEV-054
    RemAlloc_i = Pool * RemSSP_i / W
    for i in nondistinct obligations:                           # distinct obligations: no catch-up
        den = CumDel_i + RemQty_i
        if den == 0:
            if E_i != 0: finding("MOD_PROGRESS_UNDEFINED")      # DEV-059
            CU_i = 0
        else:
            CU_i = (CumDel_i / den) * (E_i + RemAlloc_i) - E_i
        E_i += CU_i; RemAlloc_i -= CU_i

def legacy_retrospective(st, lines):                            # legacy 04 §3.4 to §3.6
    CRQ_i += ModQty_i; CRS_i = max(0, RemSSP_i + mod_ssp_i); Plan_i += ModBilling_i
    TP_c = sum(RemAlloc) + sum(ModBilling) + sum(E)
    SSP_c = sum(CRS) + sum(SSPDel)
    if SSP_c == 0: finding("NON_FINITE_AMOUNT")                 # legacy loses the price (04 §3.5)
    r_c = TP_c / SSP_c
    for i in obligations:                                       # every obligation, distinct or not (DEV-071)
        CU_i = r_c * SSPDel_i - E_i; E_i = r_c * SSPDel_i; RemAlloc_i = r_c * CRS_i

def legacy_pob_vc(st, line):                                    # legacy 05 §3.4, §3.5; targeted line only (DEV-056)
    if line.qty != 0: finding("VC_QUANTITY_NOT_ALLOWED")
    if target_is_vc_line(line): finding("VC_TARGET_INVALID")
    A1 = RemAlloc_t + line.billing; Plan_t += line.billing      # DEV-083: billing plan moves with the price
    if A1 + E_t < 0: finding("VC_ALLOCATION_NEGATIVE")          # DEV-040
    W = RemSSP_t + SSPDel_t
    CU_t = 0 if W == 0 else (A1 + E_t) / W * SSPDel_t - E_t
    E_t += CU_t; RemAlloc_t = A1 - CU_t
```

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S06-R-28 | **Template selection.** `CONTRACT_AMENDED.treatments` = `LEGACY_PROSPECTIVE`, `LEGACY_RETROSPECTIVE` or `LEGACY_POB_VC` for every obligation of the contract (S01-R-09). Every obligation of the contract is in scope, including untouched and fully delivered obligations and VC lines (DEV-053). Findings of the pseudocode: `MOD_PROGRESS_UNDEFINED`, `VC_QUANTITY_NOT_ALLOWED`, `VC_TARGET_INVALID`, `VC_ALLOCATION_NEGATIVE`, `NON_FINITE_AMOUNT`, `MOD_REMAINING_NEGATIVE` (Plan_i < 0 on a non-VC line, or RemQty_i < 0) |
| S06-R-29 | **Segments after a template.** Every obligation receives a segment with basis `PROSPECTIVE`, `progress_measure = UNITS_SINCE_BOUNDARY` (legacy recognises remaining allocation ÷ remaining quantity per unit, legacy 01 §3.7), `base_revenue_exact` = E_i after the template, `x_exact` = E_i + RemAlloc_i, `totals.quantity` = RemQty_i, `unit_ssp` = RemSSP_i ÷ RemQty_i (0 when RemQty_i = 0), `remaining_ssp` = RemSSP_i, `remaining_billing_plan` = Plan_i. `a_posted` = `largest_remainder(TP_c in minor units, [X_j over every obligation of the contract], keys)[i]` (PJR-1). `base_revenue_posted` = C at the boundary per CV-63 |
| S06-R-30 | **Catch-up at a template.** CU_exact_i = the CU_i of the pseudocode; CU_posted_i = C_after(d) − C_before(d). POL-107 `LEGACY_BY_TEMPLATE` fixes the progress basis: units share for `LEGACY_PROSPECTIVE`, SSP-delivered share for `LEGACY_RETROSPECTIVE` and `LEGACY_POB_VC` |
| S06-R-31 | **Obligations added by a template (DEV-052).** Creation-time values: `original_stated_price` = Mod Billing; `original_quantity` = Mod Qty; `original_ssp_mid`, `original_ssp_high`, `original_ssp_low` = the modification band; `original_ssp_selected` = the clamped modification SSP; `original_total_contract_price` = contract TP after the event; `original_total_contract_ssp` = Σ remaining SSP after the event; `original_allocated_exact` = X_i immediately after the event; `original_unit_ssp` and `original_unit_revenue_rate` from those values; pre-standard fields 0 |
| S06-R-32 | **Pre-standard revenue.** Modification events carry a pre-standard amount of 0; the cumulative column rolls on every version (DEV-050; POL-008) |

### 6.7 Worked examples

EX-06-A (CHK-042; mixed 25-13(c)): inception TP 120,000.00; allocations A 40,000.00, B 60,000.00, C 20,000.00. At d: A satisfied (S); B cost-to-cost 50% complete, R 30,000.00, EAC 60,000.00 (class N); C unstarted (class D). Lines: non-distinct scope added to B for 20,000.00 (d SSP 25,000); EAC +15,000; C's d SSP 30,000. Pool = (60,000 − 30,000) + (20,000 − 0) + 20,000 = 70,000.00.

| POL-080 | Weight B | Weight C | Share B | Share C | A′_B | f′_B | C′_B | CU_B | Remaining B |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `D18_DEFAULT` | 0.5 × 75,000 + 25,000 = 62,500 | 30,000 | 47,297.30 | 22,702.70 | 77,297.30 | 30,000 ÷ 75,000 | 30,918.92 | +918.92 | 46,378.38 |
| `INCEPTION_ALL` | 62,500 | 25,000 | 50,000.00 | 20,000.00 | 80,000.00 | 0.4 | 32,000.00 | +2,000.00 | 48,000.00 |

B takes an `INCEPTION` segment (x′ = 30,000 + 70,000 × 62,500 ÷ 92,500); C a `PROSPECTIVE` segment with base 0.

EX-06-B (CHK-043; FASB Example 7, series): Year 3 unrecognised 100,000.00; Year 3 fee reduced by 20,000.00; three more years for 200,000.00; d SSP 80,000 a year. All remaining increments are class D: Pool 280,000.00 over four equal weights → 70,000.00 a year.

EX-06-C (research 04 S6-EX5 Case A): 120 products at 100.00; after 60 transferred, 30 added at 95.00 each; d SSP point 95.00; `point_tolerance_pct` 0. Every line is `ADD`, distinct, and passes S06-R-04 → proposal `SEPARATE_CONTRACT`; total revenue 14,850.00.

EX-06-D (CHK-027; FASB Example 8, pure 25-13(b)): A 1,000,000.00, R 600,000.00; ΔC +150,000.00; the bonus version now includes 200,000.00; costs 420,000.00; updated EAC 820,000.00. Pool = 400,000 + 150,000 + 200,000 = 750,000.00; A′ = 1,350,000.00; C′ = `cumulative_posted`(1,350,000, 135,000,000, 420/820) = 691,463.41; CU +91,463.41 (JET-05a).

EX-06-E (CHK-050; material right): TP 1,000.00; SSP P1 900 (satisfied), P2 200 (unstarted), option 100 → allocations 750.00 / 166.67 / 83.33. Exercise: product P3 for 300.00 (SSP 400); P2 d SSP 200. `CONTINUATION`: P3 = 83.33 + 300.00 = 383.33; P2 unchanged 166.67. `MODIFICATION` (`D18_DEFAULT`): Pool = 166.67 + 83.33 + 300.00 = 550.00; weights P2 200, P3 400 → 183.33 / 366.67; no catch-up.

EX-06-F (golden `catchup-08-Contract2-POB1` and `-POB3`; `LEGACY_RETROSPECTIVE`, 2023-05-15). Contract 2 before the event: POB #1 E 58.8462 (1 of 8 units), SSPDel 76.5, RemSSP 535.5; POB #3 E 46.1538 (0.4 unit), SSPDel 60, RemSSP 90; POB #2 RemSSP 408; VC 0. Line: POB #1 +2 units for 400.00; band [153, 207] → mod SSP 207. TP_c = 1,300; SSP_c = 742.5 + 408 + 90 + 0 + 76.5 + 60 = 1,377; r_c = 1,300 ÷ 1,377. CU POB #1 = r_c × 76.5 − 58.8462 = +13.3761; POB #3 = r_c × 60 − 46.1538 = +10.4910; remaining allocation POB #1 700.9804, POB #3 84.9673; revenue cumulative POB #1 72.2222 (golden values; D-17 tolerance).

EX-06-G (golden `catchup-09-Contract2-POB1`; `LEGACY_POB_VC`, 2023-05-31): M = −200.00 on POB #1; A1 = 700.9804 − 200 = 500.9804; W = 742.5 + 76.5 = 819; CU = (500.9804 + 72.2222) ÷ 819 × 76.5 − 72.2222 = −18.6813 (closed form M × D ÷ W, the latent term being 0); remaining 519.6617; revenue cumulative 53.5409.

EX-06-H (golden `catchup-10-Contract1-POB3`; `LEGACY_PROSPECTIVE`, 2023-06-15). Contract 1 before the event (after the 2023-04-30 return restored POB #1 to 5 units): RemAlloc 322.1011 / 118.5332 / 48.3152 / 644.2022; RemSSP 500 / 184 / 75 / 1,000; E 0 / 118.5332 / 48.3152 / 0. Line: POB #1 −5 units, −500.00; band [−517.5, −382.5], billing inside → mod SSP −500 → RemSSP POB #1 0. Pool = 1,133.1516 − 500 = 633.1516 over weights 0 / 184 / 75 / 1,000: POB #2 92.5337, POB #3 37.7178…, POB #4 502.9004. POB #3 is `Nondistinct`: progress 0.5 ÷ (0.5 + 0.5) = 0.5; CU = 0.5 × (48.3152 + 37.7178) − 48.3152 = −5.2988; remaining 43.0163; TP after 800.0000.

EX-06-I (CHK-115; unpriced change order): TP 1,000,000.00, EAC 800,000.00, costs 400,000.00, revenue 500,000.00; approved scope change adds 100,000.00 of costs; `UNPRICED_CHANGE_ORDER` most likely 120,000.00, constrained 90,000.00 (ΔVC). A′ 1,090,000.00; f′ = 400,000 ÷ 900,000; C′ 484,444.44; CU −15,555.56. Price agreed at 110,000.00 when costs are 600,000.00 (stage 08): before 726,666.67, after 740,000.00, JET-04a +13,333.33.

EX-06-J (CHK-112; termination): subscription billed 120,000.00 a year in advance; at month 18 the customer terminates with a refund of 60,000.00 for months 19 to 24. `REMOVE` line ΔC = −60,000.00; Pool = 60,000.00 − 60,000.00 = 0.00; no remaining obligation; the subscription obligation takes a `TERMINATION` segment at revenue 180,000.00; a `TERMINATION` refund component of 60,000.00 (JET-04b) is released by the credit memo.

## 7. Stage 07: onboarding and migration

### 7.1 Scope, inputs and outputs

Stage 07 establishes cutover state for contracts onboarded from legacy eRev (D-31), other systems (REQ-MIG-009) and business combinations (PT-11), and measures the `ONBOARDING_DIFFERENCE` of POL-210 `RECOMPUTE_FROM_INCEPTION`. Package `erev_engine/stages/s07_onboarding/` (`opening.py`, `recompute.py`, `acquired.py`, `checks.py`). The platform migration pipeline (`erev_api/domain/migration/`) builds the events; §7.4 specifies it because it decides the engine inputs.

| Direction | Content |
|---|---|
| Inputs | `OPENING_BALANCE_ESTABLISHED` payload {`reason`, `cutover_date`, `migration_batch_id`, `obligations[]` with `delivered_quantity_cum`, `ssp_delivered_cum`, `remaining_quantity`, `remaining_ssp`, `revenue_cum`, `billed_cum`, `catch_up_cum`, `pre_standard_revenue_cum`, `remaining_allocation`, `remaining_billing`, `position_obligation`, `netting_reclass_amount`} (04 §16.3); the contract's inception state from stages 02 to 05; resolved `onboarding.method` (pinned from the import batch onto the contract, level C), `bc.*` elections; policies of Table 0.10-A row 07 |
| Outputs | Segments with cause `OPENING_BALANCE`; `ObligationState.opening`; opening baselines for stage 14 (`OpeningBaseline` per obligation and role); findings |

```python
class OpeningBaseline:                          # the cumulative amounts the ERP already holds at cutover (C_0 of ALG-01 §2.1.3)
    subject_key: str; cutover_date: date
    revenue_cum: int                            # posted-basis minor units, CV-63 at cutover
    billed_cum: int; deposit_liability: int; pre_standard_revenue_cum: int
    method: str                                 # OPENING_BALANCES_AT_CUTOVER | RECOMPUTE_FROM_INCEPTION
```

### 7.2 Pseudocode

```python
def apply(ctx, st: AllocatedState, ev: EventView, tb) -> AllocatedState:
    method = ctx.policies.value("onboarding.method", contract=ev.contract_key)
    rows = {r["obligation_key"]: r for r in ev.payload["obligations"]}
    checks.consistency(ctx, st, ev, rows)                               # S07-R-03
    if ev.payload["reason"] == "BUSINESS_COMBINATION" and ctx.framework == "IFRS15":
        return acquired.ifrs_fair_value(ctx, st, ev, rows, tb)           # S07-R-08
    if method == "OPENING_BALANCES_AT_CUTOVER":
        return opening.establish(ctx, st, ev, rows, tb)                  # S07-R-04, S07-R-05
    return recompute.baseline(ctx, st, ev, rows, tb)                     # S07-R-06, S07-R-07
```

### 7.3 Rules

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S07-R-01 | **Event.** `OPENING_BALANCE_ESTABLISHED` is a boundary event (Table 0.3-A), effective on `cutover_date`, or on the contract's `inception_date` when that is later (rev 1.38; it then establishes nil opening state), appended after the contract's `CONTRACT_BOOKED` and `CONTRACT_ACTIVATED` and before any measure event dated after the cutover. It posts nothing itself (POLICIES table 2.3-B note; T-MIG-01; PT-11). **Period-end cutover (rev 1.18; D-98 candidate 22).** `cutover_date` is the end of an accounting period of every contracting entity of the contract's obligations: the deemed-posted baseline (S07-R-05) and the onboarding difference (S07-R-07) then hold pre-cutover history only; otherwise stage 07 records `ONBOARDING_CUTOVER_NOT_PERIOD_END` (ERROR; CV-15; 04 table 15.4-C) and establishes nothing. Measuring a mid-period cutover at D exactly is post-rc. **A contract that begins after the cutover (rev 1.38; supervisor rulings R-5 / R-5a, variant E).** A contract whose inception date is after `cutover_date` holds no history at the cutover. Its inception is never moved onto the cutover: the event keeps the batch's `cutover_date`, is effective on the contract's inception date — the later of the two; an event before inception is S01-R-15's `EVENT_BEFORE_INCEPTION` — and carries no cumulative amount (S07-R-03), so the period-end rule above still reads `cutover_date` and the baseline is nil. A `BUSINESS_COMBINATION` event has no such case: a contract that begins after the acquisition date is not an acquired contract (PT-11; S07-R-08 has no fair value to apportion for it), and the event is always effective on its `cutover_date`. Any other effective date is a bundle-assembly error (CV-45; `ENGINE_INVARIANT_VIOLATED`, rule `S07-R-01`) |
| S07-R-02 | **Booking of migrated contracts (D-31 mode a).** The platform books each contract from the latest legacy version's `Original …` columns (terms and inception allocation) with `inception_date` = the legacy minimum `Current Period`, so stage 05 reproduces the legacy inception allocation (golden `initial_allocation`; LM-CL-23 to 31) |
| S07-R-03 | **Consistency.** For each obligation row: X_i = `revenue_cum` + `remaining_allocation` (exact, legacy full precision through the importer's shortest decimal string); require X_i and `revenue_cum` of the same sign or 0, \|`revenue_cum`\| ≤ \|X_i\|, `remaining_quantity` ≥ 0, and Σ_i X_i = the contract transaction price within 1e-4 (D-17). A failure raises `OPENING_BALANCE_INCONSISTENT` (`ERROR`; 04 table 15.4-C, X `MIGRATION`, rev 1.2; OQ-A-13 resolved by D-76) with detail rule `S07-R-03`. An obligation of the booking missing from the payload, or a payload row for an unknown obligation, fails the same check. **No history before inception (rev 1.38).** For a contract that begins after the cutover (S07-R-01) every member that holds history through the cutover — `revenue_cum`, `billed_cum`, `delivered_quantity_cum`, `ssp_delivered_cum`, `catch_up_cum`, `pre_standard_revenue_cum`, `position_obligation`, `netting_reclass_amount`, `deposit_liability` — is 0 in every row: a row that carries one is the source read past the cutover and fails the same check (detail check `history_before_inception`, naming the member) |
| S07-R-04 | **`OPENING_BALANCES_AT_CUTOVER`.** Each obligation receives a segment with cause `OPENING_BALANCE`, basis `PROSPECTIVE`, `base_revenue_exact` = `revenue_cum`, `x_exact` = X_i, `totals.quantity` = `remaining_quantity`, `unit_ssp` = `remaining_ssp` ÷ `remaining_quantity` (0 when the quantity is 0), `remaining_ssp`, `remaining_billing_plan` = `remaining_billing`, `progress_measure` = the obligation's measure since the boundary (legacy `UNITS_SINCE_BOUNDARY`). `a_posted` = `largest_remainder(TP_c minor, [X_j], keys)` over the contract (PJR-1). `base_revenue_posted` = C_0 = `cumulative_posted(X_i, A_i, revenue_cum ÷ X_i)` (0 when X_i = 0). No revenue is recognised before the cutover (ENGINE_SPEC_B S09-R-07). The segment's remaining term starts the day after `cutover_date`, the date the imported cumulative amounts cover (rev 1.38: not the day after the event — the two coincide except for a contract that begins after the cutover, S07-R-01, whose term then starts at its inception) |
| S07-R-05 | **Opening baseline.** `OpeningBaseline.revenue_cum` = C_0; `billed_cum` = the payload value; the ledger's pre-cutover billing, deliveries and pre-standard amounts are replaced by the payload cumulative values from the cutover date. Stage 14 treats the baseline as posted for every period ending on or before the cutover: targets for those periods equal the baseline and emit no intent. [J] The ERP already holds the cutover balances; posting them would double count (T-MIG-01) |
| S07-R-06 | **`RECOMPUTE_FROM_INCEPTION` (POL-210).** The contract is replayed from inception with its full event history (booking, deliveries, billing, modifications). The payload of `OPENING_BALANCE_ESTABLISHED` supplies the imported cumulative amounts at the cutover; it creates no segment |
| S07-R-07 | **`ONBOARDING_DIFFERENCE` (POL-210 `RECOMPUTE_FROM_INCEPTION`; rev 1.17, ONB-DIFF-ROLES).** For each obligation with an `OpeningBaseline` and each role family driven by a cumulative target, difference = the recomputed cumulative role target at `cutover_date` D (the stage 14 cumulative role targets, the same amounts every later period is measured from) − the imported cumulative the ERP holds, mapped through the family's own Table 14-A template: revenue relief (JET-02 principal, JET-04a; JET-05a, JET-05b and JET-08 flow through the same target) ← `revenue_cum` (C_0; stage 07 records this family's difference as node `onboarding_difference@<event key>:<ob>:-` for the reconciliation report, S07-INV-03); billing in `ENGINE` mode (JET-03 invoice) ← `billed_cum`, the tax side imported as 0, only where the ledger holds `ENGINE`-mode lines of the contract dated on or before D (on the S10-R-03 date) (ENGINE_SPEC_B S10-R-03 rev 1.16: those lines are recomputed and the baseline `billed_cum` is not counted beside them; otherwise the imported billing is the ERP's, the baseline is counted and JET-03 has no difference; the imported `billed_cum` is net of the ERP's credit memos and maps to the invoice part while the credit-memo part imports 0; under `ENGINE` billing the JET-03 target is the net unconditional billing B_u (ENGINE_SPEC_B S10-R-08: invoices less credit memos), carried by the invoice part alone, so the difference is measured net against net and a pre-cutover `ENGINE` credit memo changes nothing when the imported net equals the recomputed net — the gross-part limitation accepted at rc, D-98 candidate 22 (no gross invoice and credit-memo cumulatives are imported); post-rc an optional `credit_memo_cum` member would map the parts separately); deposits (JET-01b receipt, criteria met, refund) ← the optional payload member `deposit_liability` (04 §16.3, rev 1.18; D-98 candidate 22): an explicit 0 posts the whole recomputed pre-cutover deposit at D, while a payload that omits the member when the recomputed cumulative is non-zero records `ONBOARDING_MEMBER_MISSING` (ERROR, stage 14, CV-15) — an absent member is never read as 0, and the same holds for an absent `billed_cum` where JET-03 carries a difference (recomputed `ENGINE` billing through D; an explicit 0 posts the full difference; D-98 candidate 35, P2-Q-5); refund liabilities (JET-04b) and return assets (JET-07c) ← 0 (the payload carries no member; the acquirer's opening balance sheet holds none). Stage 14 posts each non-zero difference once, dated D, in the period RCP-04 assigns (the first open period, with origin D's period and `reason_code = ONBOARDING_DIFFERENCE`, when D's period is closed), class `EVENT`, through the family's template and role split; from D on the recomputed cumulative is deemed posted (ENGINE_SPEC_B S14-R-26), so imported + difference + later movements = the recomputed cumulative, later periods post target minus posted as usual, and a repeat compute posts nothing new (S07-INV-03). **Component owners (rev 1.19).** Every refund-liability component resolves to the opening of its owner before the cutover, eligibility and refusal checks (D-98 35a; ENG-C5 phase 3): `RETURN`, `CONCESSION` and `UNCLAIMED_PROPERTY` to the obligation their source names; `TERMINATION` (source = the `CONTRACT_TERMINATED` event key) and `VARIABLE_CONSIDERATION` (source = the estimate key, whose head is the CV-21 encoded contract id) to the `<contract>@<entity>` opening of the contract the source names at the component's entity — the source head is the CV-21 encoded contract id and is matched against the encoded ids of the openings' contracts exactly, never against a raw id (a raw id can equal another contract's encoded form, `K%2FA` against `K/A`; Codex C5-PH3-R1, D-98 candidate 53) — the opening that exists when every obligation of the contract at that entity carries a baseline with one cutover and one method; the role keys keep the component subject and the difference node names the owner (param `owner`). A component whose owner cannot be resolved while its contract carries openings — a mixed contract (an obligation without a baseline, or differing cutovers or methods) for a contract-owned kind, or an obligation-owned component whose obligation carries no baseline — raises `ONBOARDING_DIFFERENCE_UNPOSTED` (reason `component_owner_unresolved`; detail `kind`, `contract`, `entity`, `owner_candidates`) for its parts in periods ending on or before the latest candidate cutover; the rev 1.18 interim containment, which refused the three kinds outright, is withdrawn. The per-obligation return assets carry the obligation subject and need no resolution. A role key whose imported amount cannot be mapped through the template — an agent split (JET-02 agent), an intercompany counterparty, or a split side that is not the family's plain debit or credit — raises `ONBOARDING_DIFFERENCE_UNPOSTED` (ERROR; fail closed; narrowed from the rev 1.11 interim containment, which refused every non-zero revenue difference). Under `OPENING_BALANCES_AT_CUTOVER` the revenue and billing differences are 0 by construction (S07-INV-01, S07-R-05) and the cutover cumulative of every role is deemed posted (S14-R-26). The migration reconciliation report lists every difference above 1e-4 with its DEVIATIONS reference or an exception (REQ-MIG-003) |
| S07-R-08 | **Business combinations (PT-11; POL-215 to POL-217; 805-20-30-27 to 30-30).** ASC606 book (`ASC606_AS_IF_ORIGINATED`, FORCED): the acquired contract is computed as if originated. With POL-216 `APPLY` it is booked once at the acquisition date with its final scope and consideration and no modification events. With POL-217 `APPLY`, SSP rows effective at the acquisition date weight the allocation (S05-R-01). The balances at the acquisition date become engine opening state by S07-R-04 and S07-R-05 (`reason = BUSINESS_COMBINATION`); refund liabilities acquired are imported at the acquirer's measurement. IFRS15 book (`FAIR_VALUE_IFRS3`, FORCED): the acquisition-date fair value V of the contract liability (`OPENING_BALANCE_ESTABLISHED` payload member `fair_value_contract_liability`, 04 rev 1.2; OQ-A-19 resolved by D-76) is apportioned over the remaining obligations by `largest_remainder(V, remaining allocations measured in the ASC606 book, keys)`; each obligation's PROSPECTIVE segment has `base_revenue_exact` 0 and `x_exact` = its share; revenue thereafter recognises those amounts. The rollforward shows the opening amounts on the line "business combinations" (606-10-50-10(a); IFRS 15.118(a); stage 15) |
| S07-R-09 | **Parity preset on migrated tenants (REQ-MIG-005).** Migrated tenants run under the `LEGACY_PARITY` preset; switching to native policies is a prospective policy change whose differences post under ALG-09 (POLICIES §0.5 rule 3) |
| S07-R-10 | **Migrated history.** `migrated_legacy_row` rows are read-only platform data and never enter the bundle (T-MIG-02) |

### 7.4 Platform migration mapping (D-31)

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S07-R-11 | **Mode (a) opening balances (REQ-MIG-001).** Read `Contract_Live` read-only (`file:<path>?mode=ro&immutable=1`; DG-LAY-11); the latest version per `Record Unique ID without time` by `Processing Time Log`, then SQLite `rowid` (legacy 01 §4.3); per contract emit `CONTRACT_BOOKED` (S07-R-02), `CONTRACT_ACTIVATED`, and one `OPENING_BALANCE_ESTABLISHED` with `reason = LEGACY_MIGRATION`, `cutover_date` = the user parameter, and one row per obligation from LM-CL-39 to LM-CL-68 (values as stored, converted by the shortest decimal string). VC rows become VC lines with their VC element (POL-213); material-right rows follow POL-212; `Nondistinct` rows follow POL-211. Every legacy row is stored as `migrated_legacy_row`. **A contract whose legacy minimum `Current Period` is after the cutover (rev 1.38; supervisor rulings R-5 / R-5a, variant E).** It is booked and activated at that inception (S07-R-02 unchanged; never clamped to the cutover) and receives its one `OPENING_BALANCE_ESTABLISHED` with the batch's `cutover_date`, effective on the inception (S07-R-01), with the rows as stored — no cumulative amount (S07-R-03) — so it is recognised from its inception on its legacy terms and the capture's evidence model (one opening event per captured version, 04 T-MIG-04) is unchanged. WLD-F-15: Contracts 3 and 4 (inception 1 Feb 2023, cutover 31 Jan 2023; PRD WLD-X-27 TP 1,300.00 and 950.00) |
| S07-R-12 | **Mode (b) replay (REQ-MIG-002).** Replay the legacy template files in order through the v1 importer (§1.4) into a sandbox tenant under the preset; after the reconciliation is approved, re-execute the same import batches into the production tenant (onboarding method `RECOMPUTE_FROM_INCEPTION` with no imported baseline, so no `ONBOARDING_DIFFERENCE` arises) |
| S07-R-13 | **Other systems (REQ-MIG-009).** Per import batch the tenant chooses POL-210: full inception data with events (`RECOMPUTE_FROM_INCEPTION`, with an `OPENING_BALANCE_ESTABLISHED` of `reason = SYSTEM_ONBOARDING` carrying the source system's cumulative amounts at cutover) or cutover balances only (`OPENING_BALANCES_AT_CUTOVER`) |

### 7.5 Invariants, findings, trace and references

| Item | Content |
|---|---|
| Invariants | S07-INV-01: under `OPENING_BALANCES_AT_CUTOVER`, every revenue target for a period ending on or before the cutover equals the baseline, and E at the cutover equals `revenue_cum`. S07-INV-02: Σ a_posted over the contract = TP_c in minor units. S07-INV-03: an `ONBOARDING_DIFFERENCE` is posted at most once per (obligation, role) |
| Findings | `OPENING_BALANCE_INCONSISTENT`, `ONBOARDING_DIFFERENCE_UNPOSTED` (rev 1.11) |
| Trace nodes | `opening_revenue_cum@<event key>:<ob>:-` (exact payload value with the posted C_0); `onboarding_difference@<event key>:<ob>:-` per role (params role, imported, recomputed) |
| Formula ids | `onb.opening_segment.v1`, `onb.baseline.v1`, `onb.difference.v1`, `onb.ifrs_fair_value_split.v1` |
| POL | POL-210 to POL-217 |
| ALG, JET, CHK | ALG-01 §2.1.3 (C_0 at cutover); ALG-09 (assignment of the difference); JET-02, JET-04a; CHK-121 |
| Answer-key families | `ONB`, `PAR`, `IFRS`; AK-FAM:business-combination; golden `point_in_time_equivalence` (GT-20) |
| DEVIATIONS | DEV-094, DEV-004 (normalised values) |
| Requirements | REQ-MIG-001 to -009 |

### 7.6 Worked examples

EX-07-A (PRD WLD-X-27; golden step 04 state; D-31 mode a, cutover 31 January 2023). Contract 1 payload: POB #1 `revenue_cum` 128.840436…, `remaining_allocation` 193.260647…; POB #2 118.533201… and 118.533201…; POB #3 48.315164… and 48.315164…; POB #4 0 and 644.202180…; `billed_cum` 300.00 in total. X = 322.1011… / 237.0664… / 96.6303… / 644.2022…; A = `largest_remainder`(130,000, X, keys) = 322.10 / 237.07 / 96.63 / 644.20. C_0 = 128.84 / 118.53 / 48.32 / 0.00 (f = 0.4, 0.5, 0.5, 0) → baseline revenue 295.69; net position 300.00 − 295.69 = 4.31 (contract liability). No line posts at cutover.

EX-07-B [A] (`RECOMPUTE_FROM_INCEPTION`; ALG-11 `DAILY`). Twelve-month support contract for 12,000.00 billed upfront, term 10 November 2025 to 9 November 2026 (365 days). The source system recognised 2,000.00 through the cutover of 31 December 2025. Recomputed target at the cutover: `cumulative_posted`(12,000, 1,200,000, 52/365) = 1,709.59. `ONBOARDING_DIFFERENCE` = 1,709.59 − 2,000.00 = −290.41, posted once dated 31 December 2025: Dr `REVENUE` 290.41 / Cr `CONTRACT_LIABILITY` 290.41. Contract liability 10,290.41. January 2026 revenue = 2,728.77 − 1,709.59 = 1,019.18 (83/365 cumulative).

EX-07-C (CHK-121; business combination). 24-month subscription for 240,000.00 billed upfront on 1 January 2025; acquisition on 1 January 2026. ASC606 book: opening contract liability 120,000.00; 2026 revenue 10,000.00 a month. IFRS15 book with valuation 90,000.00: 7,500.00 a month. Expedient (b) (POL-217): TP 200,000.00; inception SSPs licence 150,000 and two-year support 50,000; acquisition-date SSPs 120,000 and 80,000. After one year the opening support liability is 25,000.00 as if originated, or 40,000.00 with the expedient.

## 8. Stage 08: estimate reassessment and late events

### 8.1 Scope, inputs and outputs

Stage 08 applies approved estimate versions (D-20; ALG-10; 606-10-32-14, 32-42 to 32-45), fixes the posting-period assignment of every effect dated in a closed period (D-19; ALG-09; RCP-04), raises late-event findings and register facts, and decomposes prior-period revenue (POL-204; ALG-10 §2.11.3). Package `erev_engine/stages/s08_estimates_late_events/` (`dispatch.py`, `tp_change.py`, `routing.py`, `assign.py`, `late.py`, `decompose.py`, `gates.py`).

| Direction | Content |
|---|---|
| Inputs | `AllocatedState` before the event; `ESTIMATE_CHANGED` events and the pin index (S01-R-18); `price_at`; modification lineage (S06-R-16); `EntityInput.periods` states; `InputBundle.posted` (prior-period late attribution only); policies of Table 0.10-A row 08 |
| Outputs | Segments with causes `TP_CHANGE` and `ESTIMATE_CHANGE`; catch-up nodes; `Assignment` for any date; `LATE_EVENT` findings and `late_event_register` facts; `revenue_prior_period` targets; `close_gate_facts` for POL-042 |

```python
class Assignment:
    posting_period_key: str | None              # None: nothing posts (future period, RCP-04)
    origin_period_key: str | None               # set only when the effective period is closed or permanently locked
    reason_code: str | None                     # "LATE_EVENT" with an origin
```

### 8.2 Pseudocode

```python
KIND_EFFECT = {                                  # E-09 kind -> effect of a new version
    "VARIABLE_CONSIDERATION": "REALLOCATE", "IMPLICIT_PRICE_CONCESSION": "REALLOCATE",
    "RETURN_RATE": "MEASURE", "EAC": "MEASURE", "BREAKAGE": "MEASURE", "ROYALTY_ACCRUAL": "MEASURE",
    "EXERCISE_LIKELIHOOD": "MEASURE", "EXPECTED_PURCHASES": "MEASURE", "RENEWAL_EXPECTATION": "MEASURE",
}

def apply(ctx, st: AllocatedState, ev: EventView, tb) -> AllocatedState:
    v_new = st.estimates.version(ev.payload["estimate_version_id"])
    v_old = st.estimates.replaced_by(v_new.estimate_key, ev)                          # None for version 1 (S08-R-01, rev 1.145)
    if KIND_EFFECT[v_new.estimate_kind] == "MEASURE":                                   # S08-R-02
        tb.node(measure=f"estimate_pin@{ev.event_key}", subject_key=v_new.estimate_key, ...)
        return st                                                                       # stages 09 to 11 read the pin
    if ev.effective_date <= st.inception_date:                                          # S08-R-01 (rev 1.145): in force at inception
        tb.node(measure=f"estimate_pin@{ev.event_key}", subject_key=v_new.estimate_key, ...)
        return st                                                                       # the inception price holds the version (S04-R-01)
    d = ev.effective_date
    before = st.price_at(ctx, st, d, before=ev)
    after = st.price_at(ctx, st, d, before=next_event(st, ev))
    delta = after.allocation_basis.posted - before.allocation_basis.posted              # ΔTP, minor units
    shares = routing.route(ctx, st, v_new, delta, d)                                    # S08-R-04, S08-R-05
    segs = tp_change.segments(ctx, st, shares, ev, cause=cause_of(v_new), pair=(key(v_old), key(v_new)))   # S08-R-06
    catch_up.measure(ctx, st, segs, ev, tb)                                             # S06-R-17 formula, cause TP_CHANGE
    return st.with_segments(segs)

def assign_posting_period(ctx: BookContext, entity: str, effective_date: date) -> Assignment:   # S08-R-08
    p = dates.period_of(ctx.entities[entity], effective_date)
    s = ctx.entities[entity].state(p, ctx.book_code)
    if s in ("open", "closing", "reopened"):
        return Assignment(p.key, None, None)
    if s == "future":
        return Assignment(None, None, None)
    q = dates.first_open_period_on_or_after(ctx.entities[entity], p.end + timedelta(days=1), ctx.book_code)
    return Assignment(q.key if q else None, p.key, "LATE_EVENT")
```

### 8.3 Estimate rules

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S08-R-01 | **Pinning (05 §3.6.8).** At any date, the version in force for an element is `pin(estimate_key, at, before)` (S01-R-18). Versions effective before d are unchanged; several versions effective in one period apply in ENG-06 order, each with its own trace (ALG-10 §2.11.2 step 4). Only APPROVED versions whose `ESTIMATE_CHANGED` event is included count; approval order and event order therefore agree. **An estimate change on or before the inception (rev 1.145; item ENG-INCEPTION-ESTIMATE-1).** An `ESTIMATE_CHANGED` effective on or before the group inception re-measures nothing: its version is in force at inception and the inception price holds it (S01-R-18, S04-R-01). The version an event replaces — `v_old` of its `estimate_pin` node and of its `tp_delta` node — is the version in force before the event with every version counted from its own position, so the first version of an element replaces none |
| S08-R-02 | **Measure-only kinds.** A new version of `RETURN_RATE`, `EAC`, `BREAKAGE`, `ROYALTY_ACCRUAL`, `EXERCISE_LIKELIHOOD`, `EXPECTED_PURCHASES` or `RENEWAL_EXPECTATION` changes no allocation: stages 09 to 11 read the pin at each date (returns, progress totals, breakage, royalty accruals, redemption estimates, incentive release rate, amortisation period), and their target differences post as catch-ups by cause (ENGINE_SPEC_B §9.2.10). The option SSP of stage 03 is never re-measured after inception (606-10-32-43) |
| S08-R-03 | **Reallocating kinds.** A new `VARIABLE_CONSIDERATION` or `IMPLICIT_PRICE_CONCESSION` version changes the transaction price: ΔTP = the change of `allocation_basis.posted` between the price immediately before the event and after it (S04-R-01). `ENTERED_AMOUNT` elements under POL-043 `DELTA` imports arrive as full versions v(n) = v(n − 1) + delta (platform) |
| S08-R-04 | **Allocation basis of a change (606-10-32-43, 32-44).** An element with `allocation_target = OBLIGATIONS` routes ΔTP only to its `target_obligation_keys` by relative SSP within the targets (the 32-40 `allocation_criteria_evidence` conditions the stage 05 inception selection and the POL-200 exclusion, S05-R-14; a `WARNING` for a targeted element without evidence is post-rc, D-91 F5); a version whose target set differs from the element's version 1, or whose evidence state (`allocation_criteria_evidence` present and non-empty, the S05-R-14 `_evidenced` test) differs from version 1 in either direction, fails closed in `estimates.apply` before its zero-delta early return and before any segment or quota-history mutation, whether or not the price changes (`ENGINE_INVARIANT_VIOLATED`, detail rule S08-R-04, `reason = target_set_changed` or `evidence_changed`; CV-45); `INCREMENTS` elements are realised amounts (no segment). Otherwise ΔTP routes on the inception basis: weights = the inception selected SSPs of the obligations of the contracts the element belongs to, recorded by stage 05 as `ObligationState.inception_weight` and read by every routing path (within targets, 32-45 pre-weights, S08-R-07); for an obligation allocated by the residual approach at inception (S05-R-12) the weight is its inception residual R, the 32-34(c) estimate of its SSP (D-91; FASB Example 34 Case B + 13.00 → 43.06 / 37.21 / 30.44 / D 32.29); for an approved discount exception (S05-R-11) the weights are the inception selected SSPs of all obligations (Example 34 Case A + 14.00 → 44.00 / 38.50 / 31.50). SSP changes after inception never re-weight (rev 1.6, D-91) |
| S08-R-05 | **Routing after a 25-13(a) modification (POL-106 `ASC_606_10_32_45`; FASB Example 6).** When the element's version 1 is effective before a 25-13(a) boundary of the contract: Δ_pre = `largest_remainder(ΔTP, pre-modification inception weights, pre-modification keys)` over the obligations identified before the boundary (lineage); the shares of obligations satisfied at the boundary go to those obligations; the shares of obligations unsatisfied at the boundary sum to ΔPool, which is apportioned over the post-boundary obligations by `largest_remainder(ΔPool, post-boundary weights, keys)` (the weights of S06-R-11 recorded at the boundary). An element promised on or after the boundary routes over the obligations of the modified contract by S08-R-04. **Promised at inception (rev 1.145; item ENG-INCEPTION-ESTIMATE-1).** An element whose version 1 is in force at inception (S01-R-18) was promised before every 25-13(a) boundary of the contract, one dated at the inception and recorded before the version's event included: a later change of the estimate is routed through that boundary as above |
| S08-R-06 | **Segments of a change.** For each obligation p with a share Δ_p (minor units) and exact share δ_p = (share total ÷ 10^μ) × w_p ÷ Σw of its routing step: a new segment with cause `TP_CHANGE` (VC and concession versions), `estimate_pair` = (v_old, v_new), the basis, base members and totals of the segment in force copied unchanged, `x_exact` = x + δ_p and `a_posted` = a + Δ_p. For an `INCEPTION` segment E(d) = x′ × f(d), and for a `PROSPECTIVE` segment E(d) = b + (x′ − b) × g(d), so the catch-up is δ_p × f(d) or δ_p × g(d) (ALG-10 §2.11.2 step 2). A change that moves an obligation's exact quota from x_p ≥ 0 to x′_p < 0 raises `VC_ALLOCATION_NEGATIVE` (`ERROR`) and adds no segment, on every routing path; the predicate is on the exact quota (the exact test as S05-R-15), so an exact quota already negative at inception (S05-R-10 symmetric negative T, PROP:P3) that stays negative does not raise even when its posted amount moves from 0 to below 0, and a newly negative exact quota below 0 by less than one minor unit raises even when both posted amounts are 0 (rev 1.6, D-91) |
| S08-R-07 | **Re-apportionment on a pure inception basis (rev 1.6; D-91).** When every obligation of the group is on an `INCEPTION` segment with no 25-13(a) boundary and the element is not targeted: (a) the exact share of each obligation is δ_p = (ΔTP ÷ 10^μ) × w_p ÷ Σw over the S08-R-04 weights w of the element's obligations, and x′_p = x_p + δ_p (S08-R-06), so every pool of the quota in force (the relative share S05-R-10, targeted VC S05-R-14, the discount exception S05-R-11, the residual S05-R-12 and earlier changes) is carried unchanged (606-10-32-43; a change that does not meet 32-40 never undoes an allocation made under 32-40, 32-37 or 32-34(c)); Σw = 0 raises `TOTAL_WEIGHT_ZERO` (`ERROR`) before any division (CV-32); (b) when every x′_p ≥ 0 the posted allocations are one apportionment of the posted price over the exact quotas, a′ = `largest_remainder(allocation_basis.posted after, [x′_p], keys)` and Δ_p = a′_p − a_p (POLICIES §2.1.2 Uses; `_coverage/answer-keys-topics` T-02, OQ-AKT-04); a basis of 0 posts 0 to every obligation; PROP:P1 and PROP:P2 hold exactly after every change; a sequence of changes whose cumulative ΔTP is 0 restores the pre-cycle carried state (exact quotas and posted amounts) when the weights and the other components are unchanged and the cycle-start carried posting satisfies a_0 = `largest_remainder(basis_0, [x_0])`, where a_0, x_0 and basis_0 are the state carried into the cycle, not the original inception (a canonical inception 70/140 carried to 70/140.03 by three deferred targeted +0.01 changes is restored to 70.01/140.02, not 70/140.03, by an untargeted +0.03/−0.03 cycle, so the first untargeted change may re-round a cent carried from the deferred targeted path, as it can for Q5) (on an inception state posted otherwise the first change re-rounds the stage 05 cent inside its Δ_p, traced by the v2 params); (c) when any x′_p < 0 the posted shares are incremental, Δ = `largest_remainder(ΔTP, w, keys)`; whether the change is appended or refused is decided by the S08-R-06 transition predicate; (d) the `tp_share` nodes carry `estimate.route.inception.v2` with params `ssp_weights` = w, `quotas_after` = [x′_p], `basis_after`, `a_before`, `keys`, `key`, `mode`. On a state whose quotas are proportional to w, (b) equals `largest_remainder(basis after, w, keys)`, the rule as previously written. Changes of a targeted element keep incremental shares within the targets (S08-R-04; post-rc question L9-C606-02-Q4). [J: the former operative text re-derived every quota from raw SSP and destroyed the 32-40, 32-37 and 32-34(c) allocations; the one-minor-unit drift of incremental shares is avoided on this route by (b), not by re-weighting] |

### 8.4 Late events and assignment

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S08-R-08 | **Assignment (RCP-04; POL-180).** The posting period of an amount is the period of its effective date in the owning entity's calendar (CV-12) when that period is `open`, `closing` or `reopened`; when it is `closed` or `permanently_locked`, the first later period whose state is `open`, `closing` or `reopened`, with `origin_period_key` = the effective period and `reason_code = LATE_EVENT`; when it is `future`, nothing posts and the group is re-marked dirty when the period opens (SCH-06). With no later open period in the bundle, `posting_period_key` is None and nothing posts (bundle assembly supplies the horizon; RCP-15) |
| S08-R-09 | **Replay (ALG-09 steps 3 to 5).** A late event is folded at its (effective date, record_seq) position like any event; targets through every period are recomputed; stage 14 posts the cumulative difference of closed periods into the assigned period. Rates follow POL-181 (ENGINE_SPEC_B S12-R-13). Locked-period reclasses are never re-reversed; the reclass of the posting period is recomputed (ALG-09 step 5; ENGINE_SPEC_B S14-R-07) |
| S08-R-10 | **`LATE_EVENT` (`WARNING`).** Raised once per book for each new event (`is_new`) whose effective period is `closed` or `permanently_locked` for the contracting entity, and for each new event whose effective date is earlier than the effective date of an event of the same contract recorded before it (out-of-order arrival; DEV-024). Detail: `event_key`, `origin_period_key`, `posting_period_key` |
| S08-R-11 | **Register facts.** For each new late event: {event key, contract key, origin period, posting period, event type}; stage 14 attaches the amounts by role, and the platform builds the out-of-period register (research 07 §5.4; REQ-CLS-004) |
| S08-R-12 | **Change classification (POL-183).** The engine never decides between a change in estimate and an error correction. An error correction reopens the period with dual approval; the recomputation then posts into the `reopened` period under S08-R-08 (REQ-CLS-011) |
| S08-R-13 | **Reassessment gate facts (POL-042).** For each open VC element and each period end through the horizon, `close_gate_facts` lists elements without a version effective at the period end or an approved `no_change_attestation` version (T-CON-13 parameters); the close step `INVARIANTS` raises `VC_REASSESSMENT_MISSING` (source `CLOSE`). `NOT_ENFORCED` (parity) produces no facts |

### 8.5 Prior-period decomposition (POL-204; ALG-10 §2.11.3; 606-10-50-12A)

```python
def decompose_prior_period(ctx: BookContext, st: AllocatedState, period_key: str, tb: TraceBuilder) -> tuple[Target, ...]:
    """Called by stage 15 (ENGINE_SPEC_B §0.4, S15-R-13) with the AllocatedState consumed by stage 09."""
    s = period_start(period_key)
    out = []
    for ob in st.obligations:
        amt = 0
        for k in boundaries_effective_in(st, ob, period_key):             # S08-R-14: every boundary changing E at s
            e_before = exact_cum_revenue(ob, s, state=before(k))            # CV-63 at the day before s
            e_after = exact_cum_revenue(ob, s, state=after(k))
            amt += money.round_half_up(e_after - e_before, mu)
        amt += late_attribution(ctx, st, ob, s)                             # S08-R-15
        out.append(Target(measure="revenue_prior_period", subject_key=ob.subject_key, period_key=period_key, value=amt, ...))
    return tuple(out)
```

| Id | Rule |
|---|---|
| S08-R-14 | **Boundaries in the period.** For each boundary event k effective in period t (start s) that changes an obligation's exact cumulative revenue at s: reallocating estimate versions, modifications, terminations, exercises, and measure-only versions that change progress totals (for example `EAC`): prior-period part = round(E_after,k(s) − E_before,k(s)), both evaluated with the measure quantities at the end of the day before s under the state immediately before and after k (CV-63). For an allocation change this equals round(ΔX_p,k × f_p(s)) (ALG-10 §2.11.3); for a totals change it captures the effect on performance already satisfied (OQ-B-20 resolved by D-76; POLICIES ALG-10 §2.11.3 rev 1.2). Rev 1.20 (D-97 (10)): a measure-only `EAC` version applied in t to an input-measured obligation adds no segment (S08-R-02) and is a boundary of its own; every E_k of an input-measured obligation is measured with the `EAC` version in force after k (the S06-R-15 updated EAC of a class N amendment), dated no later than the day before s so that the measure quantities at s meet the totals after k; a version the previous boundary already read moves nothing and adds no part. After a 25-13(a) boundary effective in t (a `PROSPECTIVE` segment with a new base) the basis in force starts at that boundary, so nothing of it is measured at s: the boundary contributes 0 (CV-63) and so does every later measure-only version of t — its totals change reaches only performance after the boundary (C4-EAC-R1); a measure-only version before that boundary keeps its part. The catch-up posted at d splits into the prior-period part and the current-period remainder |
| S08-R-15 | **Late events.** For a revenue role key, the late attribution posted in t = the recomputed revenue target through the end of t − 1 minus the posted revenue through the end of t − 1 (`InputBundle.posted`), when positive or negative and non-zero (ALG-09 step 7) |
| S08-R-16 | **Output.** Node `revenue_prior_period:<ob>:<period key>` (formula `estimate.prior_period.v1`; params `boundaries`, `carries`, `e_before_<k>`, `e_after_<k>`, `rule_<k>` and, for an input-measured obligation, the additive `version_<k>` naming the `EAC` version in force after k; rev 1.20, D-97 (10)); stage 15 aggregates the nodes (ENGINE_SPEC_B §15). Journal lines are not split (05 §3.6.8) |

| Item | Content |
|---|---|
| Invariants | S08-INV-01: after a reallocating version, Σ a_posted = `allocation_basis.posted` (PROP:P1). S08-INV-02: Σ_p Δ_p = ΔTP exactly. S08-INV-03: `assign_posting_period` never returns a `closed`, `permanently_locked` or `future` period (PROP:P11). S08-INV-04: for every obligation and period, the prior-period part plus the current-period part of a catch-up equals the catch-up |

| Trace nodes | `estimate_pin@<event key>:<estimate key>:-`; `tp_delta@<event key>:<group>:-`; `tp_share@<event key>:<ob>:-`; `catch_up@<event key>:<ob>:-` (params v_old, v_new, f(d)); `revenue_prior_period:<ob>:<period>`. 05 §3.6.8 names the catch-up node `catch_up:<obligation key>:<period key>`; CV-50 governs (one node per boundary event) |

| POL | POL-042, POL-043, POL-106, POL-180 to POL-183, POL-204 |
| ALG, JET, CHK | ALG-09, ALG-10; JET-04a, JET-04b; CHK-090, CHK-091, CHK-100, CHK-101 |

### 8.7 Worked examples

EX-08-A (CHK-090; golden Contract 1): deliveries effective 31 January 2023 recorded on 5 February after January is locked; February open. `assign_posting_period(US entity, 2023-01-31)` → posting `FY2023-P02`, origin `FY2023-P01`, `LATE_EVENT` (`WARNING`). Targets for January: POB #1 128.84, POB #2 118.53, POB #3 48.32 (CV-63 over the inception segments: f = 2/5, 1/2, 1/2). February batch: Dr 21001 295.69 / Cr 5001 128.84, 5002 118.53, 5003 48.32 with origin `FY2023-P01`; the January batch is unchanged; the register lists 295.69.

EX-08-B (CHK-100; research 04 S3-EX21-EXTENDED, Year 2): bonus element v1 constrained 0.00 → v2 150,000.00, effective at the start of Year 2 with f = 0.40. ΔTP = +150,000.00 on the single obligation (INCEPTION segment): x′ = 2,655,000. Catch-up = `cumulative_posted`(2,655,000, …, 0.40) − 1,002,000.00 = 1,062,000.00 − 1,002,000.00 = 60,000.00 (JET-04a); progress revenue to f = 0.80: 2,124,000.00 − 1,062,000.00 = 1,062,000.00; Year 2 revenue 1,122,000.00; prior-period decomposition round(150,000 × 0.40) = 60,000.00; trace ΔTP +150,000.00 with pair (v1, v2).

EX-08-C (CHK-101; research 04 S3-EX23 Case B): concession v1 constrained at 50% (TP 50,000.00) → v2 resolved at 45% (TP 55,000.00) in February 20X8; the obligation was satisfied in December 20X7 (f = 1). Catch-up +5,000.00; prior-period revenue 5,000.00; trace ΔTP +5,000.00.

EX-08-D (variant of EX-08-C; S08-R-08): had December 20X7 and January 20X8 been locked with v2 effective 31 December 20X7, `assign_posting_period` would give posting `FY20X8-P02` with origin `FY20X7-P12` and `reason_code = LATE_EVENT`.

EX-08-E (research 04 S4-EX6; FASB Example 6): X and Y with equal SSPs; fixed 1,000.00 plus VC 200.00 → 600.00 each; X transferred. Modification (25-13(a)): Z added for 300.00 → Pool 900.00 → Y 450.00, Z 450.00. VC estimate rises to 240.00 (promised before the modification): Δ_pre = `largest_remainder`(4,000, [1, 1]) = 20.00 / 20.00; X was satisfied at the boundary → +20.00 immediately; Y's 20.00 flows to the post-boundary pool → Y +10.00, Z +10.00 → Y 460.00, Z 460.00; total revenue 1,540.00.

EX-08-F (D-91; C606-02): A and B, SSP 100.00 each; fixed 100.00 → 50.00 / 50.00; evidenced targeted VC 100.00 → A; A transferred 10 January, B unsatisfied. Untargeted estimate 0 → 100.00 on 1 February: δ = 50.00 / 50.00, x′ = 200.00 / 100.00, a′ = `largest_remainder`(30,000, [200, 100]) = 200.00 / 100.00; catch-up on A +50.00 (FY2026-P02). Back to 0 on 1 March: 150.00 / 50.00, catch-up −50.00. To 100.00 on 1 April: 200.00 / 100.00, +50.00. Four successive +0.01 changes from 150.00 / 50.00 post 150.01 / 50.00, 150.01 / 50.01, 150.02 / 50.01, 150.02 / 50.02 (exact 150.02 / 50.02). A change of −150.00 gives x′_B = −25.00 → `VC_ALLOCATION_NEGATIVE` (the guard is on each obligation's total quota: with targeted 100.00 to each obligation the same change gives 75.00 / 75.00 and no finding). With POL-200 `APPLY` and both unsatisfied, a targeted change 100.00 → 200.00 on 1 February gives A 250.00 / B 50.00, excluded 200.00, RPO 100.00; the FY2026-P02 rollforward reads the quota in force at 31 January (100.00): opening 100.00, VC estimate changes +100.00, exemptions −100.00, closing 100.00.

EX-08-G (D-91; FASB Example 34): Case A (approved exception for bundle B+C; 40.00 / 33.00 / 27.00) with an untargeted +14.00: δ by SSP 40 / 55 / 45 → 44.00 / 38.50 / 31.50. Case B (D residual 30.00; 40.00 / 33.00 / 27.00 / 30.00) with +13.00: weights 40 / 55 / 45 / 30 (D at its inception residual) → 43.06 / 37.21 / 30.44 / 32.29.
